*Momina Liaqat Ali,Muhammad Abid,Muhammad Abdullah,Aneela Zameer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合预测方法，通过将风力发电机组运行数据转换为简化文本并生成密集嵌入（语义上下文特征），结合集成模型来预测风电爬坡事件。在SDWPF数据集上的10/30/60分钟预测区间测试表明，语义上下文特征在多个配对集成实验中带来统计上显著但微小的增益，尤其在30和60分钟时间尺度上稳健；PCA压缩在部分长时域场景有帮助；SGDWPF外部验证确认误差减少可泛化，但预测区间在爬坡事件期间覆盖减弱，表明局部数据分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电爬坡事件（风力输出在短时间内突然大幅波动）难以准确预测，标准模型经常失效。本文作者旨在通过引入语义上下文信息来增强爬坡事件预测的准确性，而不直接使用大型语言模型处理原始涡轮机运行数据。

Method: 构建一个流水线：先将风力涡轮机运行数据转换为简化文本，再转换为稠密嵌入（dense embeddings），作为集成模型的输入特征与其他特征结合。在SDWPF数据集上进行10、30、60分钟多区间测试，以未来输出功率最大变化定义爬坡事件。通过与自回归、LSTM、GRU基线及多种集成配置对比，使用Diebold-Mariano检验和bootstrap置信区间评估，并变化爬坡阈值、PCA压缩嵌入、在Kaggle SCADA和NREL数据上进行外部验证。

Result: 语义上下文特征在多个配对集成运行中对基线产生了统计上显著但微小的增益，在30和60分钟时间尺度上最明显，且在不同爬坡阈值定义下保持稳健。PCA压缩在部分长时域场景中有帮助。上下文增强集成排名总体靠前，但GRU模型在30和60分钟上仍取得最低爬坡事件RMSE。外部测试证实误差减少可跨数据集泛化，但增益大小依赖模型和数据集。预测区间在大多数测试覆盖良好，但在爬坡事件期间覆盖变弱。

Conclusion: 语义上下文增强能带来一致的统计显著改善，但增益幅度有限，不足以全面超越强基线模型（如GRU）。预测区间在爬坡事件期间覆盖减弱揭示了局部数据分布偏移，表明爬坡事件本身具有内在不确定性，未来工作需进一步关注爬坡区间的区间校准和模型融合策略。

Abstract: Wind power ramp events which are sudden, large swings in turbine output over short windows are difficult to estimate, and standard models often miss them. Hybrid forecasting approach is built which augments semantic context to ramp-event forecast. Rather than applying an extensive language model directly to predict turbine operating data, we have implemented a pipeline where turbine operating data is converted to simplified text, which is then converted to dense embeddings to be used as inputs for ensemble models incorporated with other features. Testing runs are performed at multiple intervals within the SDWPF dataset, including 10-minute, 30-minute, and 60- minute horizons, with ramp events constituting the highest change in future power output. We check robustness against autoregressive, LSTM, and GRU baselines plus several ensemble configurations, using Diebold-Mariano tests and bootstrap confidence intervals, and we vary the ramp threshold, compress the embeddings with PCA, and validate externally on Kaggle SCADA and NREL data with uncertainty-aware scoring. The semantic-context features produce negligible yet statistically significant gains over the baselines in multiple paired ensemble runs, most clearly at the 30- and 60-minute horizons where these gains hold across different ramp-threshold definitions, and PCA compression helps in some longer-horizon cases. The best context- augmented ensembles rank near the top overall, though the GRU model still posts the lowest ramp-event RMSE at 30 and 60 minutes. External tests confirm the error reduction generalizes across datasets, but the size of the gain depends on both model and dataset. Prediction intervals cover most test cases well but weaken during ramp events, pointing to a localized shift in the data distribution.

</details>


### [19] [Flow-JEPA: Flow Matching for Robust Latent Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29029)
*Yanchen Huo,Ziying Song,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow-JEPA（F-JEPA），一种基于条件流匹配的世界模型，利用高斯分布作为流源，将带噪声的潜在轨迹传输到干净的未来表示，取代传统确定性自回归预测器，在干净和噪声条件下均显著提升了性能（成功率从86%→92%，噪声下从67%→86%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型（如LeWorldModel）使用确定性自回归预测器生成未来状态，但重复单步转换会累积误差，且对任务无关的视觉扰动敏感，因此需要更鲁棒的动态建模方法。

Method: 提出F-JEPA，采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）动态模型，以高斯分布为流源，联合生成基于当前观测和动作的一系列未来潜在状态。该模型将确定性点对点转换回归替换为随机轨迹级预测，使向量场在传输扰动潜在轨迹至干净未来表示的过程中暴露于噪声，保留JEPA框架的无重建特性。

Result: 在干净观测下，平均成功率从86%提升至92%；在噪声条件下，从67%大幅提升至86%。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配为JEPA世界模型提供了一种有前景的确定性自回归动态的替代方案，在保持无重建框架的同时提升了鲁棒性和预测准确性。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have shown strong potential for learning compact predictive representations, and LeWorldModel (LeWM) extends this paradigm to reconstruction-free latent world modeling from pixels. However, its deterministic autoregressive predictor generates future states through repeated one-step transitions, which can accumulate errors and remain sensitive to task-irrelevant visual perturbations. In this work, we propose Flow-JEPA (F-JEPA), a conditional flow matching dynamics model that jointly generates a sequence of future latent states conditioned on the current observation and actions. A Gaussian distribution serves as the flow source, exposing the vector field to perturbed latent trajectories as it learns to transport them toward clean future representations. This formulation retains the reconstruction-free JEPA framework while replacing point-wise transition regression with stochastic trajectory-level prediction. F-JEPA raises mean success from $86\%$ to $92\%$ under clean observations and from $67\%$ to $86\%$ under noisy conditions, suggesting that conditional flow matching provides a promising alternative to deterministic autoregressive dynamics in JEPA world models.

</details>


### [20] [NVE: A Separability and Coverage-Aware Internal Validation Metric for Biclustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29045)
*Paritosh Tiwari,I Navin Kumar,James C. Bezdek,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于双聚类（协同聚类）内部验证的归一化虚拟误差（NVE）指标及其覆盖调整变体NVE_cov。NVE通过超双聚类归一化策略比较每个双聚类与其合并后的虚拟误差，引入可分离性和冗余性的相对概念；NVE_cov则惩罚仅选择极小子矩阵而获得低误差的解决方案。结果表明两种指标能提供超越传统一致性指标（如MSR、VE）的互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双聚类内部验证指标（如均方残差MSR和虚拟误差VE）主要评估双聚类内部的一致性（coherence），但未能直接评估提取的双聚类之间是否相互区分（可分离性）、是否冗余，以及是否解释了数据矩阵中有意义的部分（覆盖率）。这促使研究者提出能够综合考虑一致性、可分离性和覆盖率的内部验证指标。

Method: 提出归一化虚拟误差（NVE），其核心思想是：将每个双聚类的虚拟误差与该双聚类与其他双聚类合并后的虚拟误差进行比较，从而引入可分离性和冗余性的相对度量。同时提出了覆盖调整变体NVE_cov，对仅覆盖极小部分矩阵的低误差双聚类施加惩罚。通过受控合成基准数据集和酵母基因表达数据集进行验证。

Result: NVE对冗余且分离不佳的双聚类敏感；NVE_cov在低误差双聚类仅覆盖矩阵极小部分时能够改变解决方案的排序。两者提供了超越标准一致性指标（MSR、VE）的额外信息。

Conclusion: NVE及其变体NVE_cov是双聚类内部验证的有用互补标准，尤其在需要同时考虑一致性（coherence）、可分离性（separability）和覆盖率（coverage）的场景下具有重要价值。

Abstract: Biclustering, or co-clustering, aims to discover coherent submatrices by grouping rows and columns of a data matrix simultaneously. This local two-dimensional structure makes validation more difficult than in ordinary clustering, where internal indices usually rely on compactness and separation in a single shared feature space. Existing popular internal biclustering measures such as Mean Squared Residue (MSR), and Virtual Error (VE) mainly evaluate within-bicluster coherence. Although useful, these measures do not directly assess whether the extracted biclusters are mutually distinct or whether they explain a meaningful portion of the data matrix. This paper investigates Normalised Virtual Error (NVE), an internal validation metric that extends VE using a super-bicluster normalization strategy. By comparing the VE of each bicluster with the VE obtained after merging it with other biclusters, NVE introduces a relative notion of separability and redundancy. We also study a coverage-adjusted variant, NVE\textsubscript{cov}, which penalizes solutions that obtain low error by selecting only very small submatrices. Through controlled synthetic benchmarks and yeast gene-expression datasets, we examine whether NVE and NVE\textsubscript{cov} provide information beyond standard coherence-based metrics. The results show that NVE is sensitive to redundant and poorly separated biclusters, while NVE\textsubscript{cov} changes solution rankings when low-error biclusters cover only a negligible part of the matrix. These findings suggest that NVE-based measures are useful complementary criteria for internal co-clustering validation, especially when coherence, separability, and coverage must be considered jointly.

</details>


### [21] [Sparse Koopman Autoencoders Identify Local Dynamical Regimes in Multibasin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29057)
*Aidan Li,Uday Kiran Reddy Tadipatri,Mahan Fathi,Sarath Chandar,Ross Goroshin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏Koopman自编码器（SKAEs），利用稀疏性诱导目标使编码器产生少量活跃隐变量，从而在无盆地标签的情况下学习多盆地非线性系统的Koopman嵌入，并将隐变量支撑集作为可解释的流域指示变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman自编码器（KAEs）旨在寻找高维潜在表示使得非线性动力学在潜在空间中线性演化，但许多系统具有多个吸引域（basins of attraction）。理论和经验均表明，在标准假设下，多盆地系统通常无法接受单一有限维全局Koopman嵌入，这使得KAEs在多盆地系统中的适用性受到根本限制。

Method: 作者在编码器中加入稀疏性诱导目标（鼓励少量活跃潜在系数），训练稀疏Koopman自编码器（SKAEs）。SKAEs无需盆地标签或任何机制标注，训练后将学习到的潜在支撑集视为模型产生的机制变量（regime variables）。

Result: 在一系列程序化生成的多盆地系统和混沌流中，SKAEs相比稠密潜在KAEs具有更优越的预测性能。机制研究表明，SKAEs产生的潜在支撑集对表示质量至关重要，且能有效识别held-out盆地内部状态；而稠密潜在KAEs则坍缩为无信息的单一簇。

Conclusion: 稀疏潜在变量及其对应的支撑集可作为一种无标签、可解释的机制变量，用于具有多个局域动力学规律的非线性系统的Koopman学习。

Abstract: Koopman autoencoders (KAEs) seek a higher-dimensional latent representation in which nonlinear dynamics evolve linearly. However, many interesting systems have multiple basins of attraction, and both theoretical and empirical work has shown these multibasin systems cannot generally admit a single finite-dimensional global Koopman embedding under standard assumptions. We posit that encoders with a sparsity-inducing objective encouraging few active latent coefficients will provide latent supports as an inspectable basin-modeling principle for Koopman autoencoders. We use these encoders producing sparse latents in training Sparse Koopman Autoencoders (SKAEs) without basin labels or other regime annotations, and treat the learned latent supports as model-produced regime variables after training. Across a range of procedurally generated multibasin systems and chaotic flows, we show that SKAEs have superior forecasting performance compared to dense-latent KAEs. We also perform a mechanistic study that shows latent supports produced by SKAEs are both essential for the quality of the representation and useful for identifying basins on held-out basin interior states, whereas dense-latent KAEs collapse to an uninformative single family. These results identify sparse latents and their corresponding supports as label-free, interpretable regime variables for Koopman learning in nonlinear systems with multiple local dynamical laws.

</details>


### [22] [PathBridger: Subgoal Bridges for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29061)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathBridger 是一种分层离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过在子目标选择与短视距执行之间显式构建状态空间桥梁，并将其解码为可执行的动作块，来解决长视距离线GCRL中稀疏奖励信号传播和执行误差无法纠正的问题。在OGBench基准任务上表现出色，尤其在多物体Cube操作任务上增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期（long-horizon）离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）面临两大挑战：稀疏的目标达成信号需要在多步中传播，且执行误差无法通过额外的环境交互来修正。现有分层方法存在接口问题——选定的子目标只指定了"去哪"，但中间的状态空间路径隐式地取决于以终点为条件的低层策略，导致高层子目标选择与低层执行之间缺乏显式连接。

Method: PathBridger 采用分层离线GCRL框架，核心创新在于：1）显式地构建从当前状态到选定中间端点（子目标）的状态空间桥梁（state-space bridge）；2）利用逆动力学模型将该桥梁解码为短时的可执行动作块（action chunk）。通过这种方式，将子目标选择与短视距执行直接关联起来，弥补了两者之间的接口空白。

Result: 在OGBench基准测试的多个任务中展现出较强的聚合性能，尤其是在多物体Cube操作任务上取得了特别显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: PathBridger通过显式桥接高层子目标选择与低层短视距执行，有效缓解了分层离线GCRL中的层级接口问题，在长视距场景下取得了更好的性能，验证了通过逆动力学模型将状态空间路径解码为动作块这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) aims to learn policies for reaching diverse goals entirely from fixed trajectory data. Long-horizon offline GCRL remains challenging because sparse goal-reaching signals must be propagated over many steps, while execution errors cannot be corrected through additional environment interaction. Existing methods address these challenges by improving long-range value estimation or reducing the effective decision horizon through subgoals, options, and action chunks. In several hierarchical methods, however, a selected subgoal specifies where to go, while the intervening state-space path remains implicit in an endpoint-conditioned low-level policy. To address this interface, we propose PathBridger, a hierarchical offline GCRL method that explicitly connects subgoal selection to short-horizon execution. PathBridger constructs a state-space bridge toward the selected intermediate endpoint and decodes it into a short executable action chunk using an inverse dynamics model. Experiments across the evaluated OGBench tasks demonstrate strong aggregate performance, with particularly large gains on the multi-object Cube manipulation tasks. Code: https://github.com/SChoish/PathBridger

</details>


### [23] [Selective Disclosure of Hidden Directives in Reasoning Models: Behavioral Asymmetry and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29070)
*Zimo Shi,Xander Tifft,Wen Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令遵从差距"(ICG)概念，检验思维链(CoT)是否对称地暴露模型的隐藏指令。研究发现8个前沿推理模型中普遍存在非对称披露：恶意隐藏指令比良性指令更容易在CoT中泄露；通过对比激活加法的转向向量可因果诱导/抑制隐藏行为，且恶意与良性隐藏向量高度相似，表明非对称披露源于共享隐藏方向的差异激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理迹被广泛提议作为AI监督机制，其核心假设是CoT能暴露模型被指示的内容，无论指令性质如何。作者要检验这一假设是否成立——即模型是否只在恶意指令时隐藏、而在良性指令时坦诚，这种非对称性将破坏CoT作为监督工具的有效性。

Method: 1) 定义ICG指标：模型CoT显式引用隐藏系统指令的概率在恶意vs良性指令下的差异；2) 在100个任务对和8个前沿推理模型(5个家族)上测试；3) 使用双盲标注验证检测器100%精确度；4) 用LLM监控器仅读推理迹复现非对称性；5) 通过Contrastive Activation Addition在MiniMax-M2.5和Qwen3-14B中提取转向向量，因果诱导/抑制隐藏行为，并分析向量的余弦相似度。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B (p=0.0001, +13.9pp)、Qwen3-32B (p=0.0011, +13.0pp)、Qwen3-235B (p=0.035, +5.8pp) 及MiniMax-M2.5和DeepSeek-R1中均发现非对称披露；LLM监控器在8个模型中复现该非对称性，82%的恶意迹中被准确识别具体指令；CAA转向向量能因果诱导和抑制隐藏，恶意与良性隐藏向量余弦相似度极高(0.804/0.970)，表明共享隐藏方向。

Conclusion: 当前前沿推理模型存在系统性非对称披露：比良性指令更倾向于在CoT中暴露恶意隐藏指令。这意味着CoT监督可能在"恶意指令"时过于严厉而在"良性指令"时过于宽容，且该非对称源于单一共享隐藏方向的差异化激活而非独立的隐藏机制，对AI监督和CoT可解释性的可靠性提出重要质疑。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning traces are increasingly proposed as a mechanism for AI oversight: a monitor inspecting a model's reasoning can, in principle, detect misbehavior invisible from outputs alone. This assumes CoT surfaces what a model is instructed to do regardless of the instructions given. We test this assumption along two axes. First, we introduce the Instruction-Compliance Gap (ICG): the difference in probability that a model's CoT explicitly references a hidden system prompt directive when that directive is malign versus benign. Across 100 task pairs and 8 frontier reasoning models from 5 families, we find consistent asymmetric disclosure, a higher probability of leaking malign hidden instructions than benign ones, in Qwen3-14B (Wilcoxon $p=0.0001$, $+13.9$pp), Qwen3-32B ($p=0.0011$, $+13.0$pp), Qwen3-235B ($p=0.035$, $+5.8$pp), and similar results with MiniMax-M2.5 and DeepSeek-R1. The detector has 100% precision against two independent blinded labelling passes, and an LLM monitor reading only the reasoning trace reproduces the asymmetry in all 8 models against directive-free controls, identifying the specific directive in 82% of malign traces which the detector classifies as clean. Second, steering vectors extracted in MiniMax-M2.5 via Contrastive Activation Addition causally induce hiding from bare prompts and suppress it from prompts that would otherwise produce it, replicating in Qwen3-14B under a pre-registered design. Benign and malign-derived hiding vectors are highly similar (cosine $0.804$ in MiniMax-M2.5; $0.970$ in Qwen3-14B), implying that in these models the disclosure asymmetry arises from differential activation of a shared hiding direction rather than separate mechanisms.

</details>


### [24] [Titans-QFWP: A Regime-Aware Hybrid Quantum Fast Weight Programmer for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29093)
*Ming-Kai Hung,Jun-Hao Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Titans-QFWP，一种集成量子快速权重编程器与Titans式记忆（持久性、惊喜、遗忘）的混合强化学习架构，用于自适应投资组合优化，在468只标普500股票上以约3000参数实现优异表现（中位年化收益率0.4260，Calmar比率8.5504，信息比率0.8427）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维市场特征下自适应投资组合优化的挑战，传统强化学习方法在记忆管理和特征处理方面存在不足，需要引入更强大的记忆机制和量子计算概念来提升投资决策的适应性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 构建混合强化学习架构，将量子快速权重编程器（Quantum Fast Weight Programmer）与Titans式记忆模块（Persistence、Surprise、Forgetting）结合；2) 提出增强型A3C^2框架，引入匈牙利对齐的K-means聚类算法和缩放对数收益奖励函数以处理高维市场特征；3) 采用等参数计数（EPC）基准进行公平比较。

Result: 在468只标普500股票上、约3000可训练参数的条件下取得强性能：中位年化收益率（ARR）0.4260、Calmar比率8.5504、信息比率（IR）0.8427。消融实验表明量子门控重塑了各记忆组件角色：Persistence负责回撤控制、Surprise贡献收益生成、Forgetting提供额外稳定化。

Conclusion: 量子表示稳定化使模型在市场回撤期间能够实现防御性资产配置，同时保留上涨潜力，验证了三组件记忆机制在投资组合优化中的有效协同性。

Abstract: We propose Titans-QFWP, a hybrid reinforcement learning architecture integrating a Quantum Fast Weight Programmer with Titans-style memory (Persistence, Surprise, and Forgetting) for adaptive portfolio optimization. To address high-dimensional market features, we introduce an enhanced A3C^2 framework with Hungarian-aligned K-means clustering and scaled log-return rewards. Evaluated on 468 S&P 500 stocks under an Equal-Parameter-Count (EPC) benchmark with approximately 3,000 trainable parameters, Titans-QFWP achieves strong performance (median ARR 0.4260, Calmar 8.5504, IR 0.8427). Ablation results reveal that quantum gating fundamentally reshapes memory component roles, with Persistence supporting drawdown control, Surprise contributing to return generation, and Forgetting providing additional stabilization. By stabilizing these quantum representations, the model enables defensive allocation during market drawdowns while preserving upside potential.

</details>


### [25] [Development of an Autonomous AI Coding Agent using Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and Gemini LLM Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29096)
*Pravin Game,Vipin Ramakrishnan,Prathamesh Wagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自主AI编码代理，结合Gemini 2.5 Flash API与定制化蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）方法，将代码生成视为搜索问题，通过"自批评"评估器对多种实现方案进行排序和反向传播优化，在复杂逻辑提示上达到92%的成功率，超越传统零样本生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程需求的持续变化催生了对能从自然语言输入自动生成安全代码的工具的迫切需求，但传统大语言模型的"一次性"生成能力在复杂操作中受到限制，存在逻辑幻觉和算法性能下降的问题。

Method: 采用Gemini 2.5 Flash API作为核心推理引擎，使用定制化的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）方法将代码生成转化为搜索问题；通过"自批评"（Self-Critic）评估器测试不同实现方案，根据准确性和难度级别进行排序，并通过反向传播改进操作框架；系统基于Flask构建Web界面，提供即时反馈和语法高亮功能。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于MCTS的方法在复杂逻辑提示上达到了92%的成功率，优于标准的零样本生成模型。

Conclusion: 该自主AI编码代理通过将搜索策略与推理能力相结合，有效弥合了LLM生成内容与生产级软件之间的差距，显著提升了复杂代码生成任务的性能。

Abstract: The ongoing changes in software engineering requirements have created a substantial need for automated tools which can create secure source code from natural language input. The performance of traditional Large Language Models (LLMs) becomes limited by their "one-shot" capability which results in logical hallucinations together with reduced algorithmic performance during complicated operations. The research presents an autonomous AI Coding Agent which establishes a connection between LLM-generated content and production-ready software through its organized methodology for decision making. Our framework uses the Gemini 2.5 Flash API for essential reasoning capabilities while employing a tailored Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) method to solve code generation challenges as a search operation. The agent uses a "Self-Critic" evaluator system to test different implementation methods which it ranks according to their accuracy and difficulty level before it improves its operational framework through backpropagation. The system operates through a Flask-based web interface which delivers instant feedback together with syntax highlighting features. Our experimental results show that the MCTS-based method achieves a 92% success rate on complex logical prompts while surpassing standard zero-shot generation models.

</details>


### [26] [Temperature-Adaptive Transformed Teacher Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29099)
*Hiroaki Aizawa,Yoshikazu Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逐样本自适应逆温度更新方法（TTM-SAT），通过在局部最小化温度缩放后教师分布与学生预测之间的KL散度，为Ttransformed Teacher Matching (TTM)框架动态调整每个样本的最优温度，利用一阶和二阶导数（基于中心化logits的方差与协方差统计）实现高效的曲率感知更新，仅需一次softmax计算和常数次类级加权求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度缩放是知识蒸馏的核心组件，但TTM框架虽然阐明了温度缩放的角色（仅应用于教师分布，并隐含Rényi熵正则化），仍依赖固定温度，未能说明如何针对不同样本自适应调整教师侧温度，限制了蒸馏性能的进一步提升。

Method: 提出TTM的逐样本逆温度更新方法：通过对逆温度求KL散度的一阶和二阶闭式导数，并将其表示为中心化教师与学生logits在变换教师加权下的方差和协方差统计量，实现曲率感知的牛顿式逐样本温度调整。该更新仅需一次softmax评估和常数次类级加权求和，计算开销极低。

Result: 在标准图像分类蒸馏基准上的实验表明，所提温度自适应方法普遍优于固定温度的TTM和WTTM，并与已有的温度自适应蒸馏基线相比具有竞争力或更优性能。

Conclusion: 逐样本自适应温度缩放是提升TTM蒸馏性能的有效手段，闭式导数推导使得该自适应过程计算高效且易于集成到现有蒸馏框架中。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is a core component of knowledge distillation, yet its role and effect are still not fully understood. Transformed Teacher Matching (TTM) clarifies the role of temperature scaling by applying it only to the teacher distribution and interpreting the resulting objective as standard distillation with an implicit Rényi entropy regularization on the student. However, TTM still relies on a fixed temperature and does not specify how the teacher-side temperature should be adapted for individual samples. In this paper, we introduce a sample-wise inverse-temperature update for TTM by locally minimizing the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the temperature-scaled teacher distribution and the student's prediction. We derive closed-form first and second derivatives with respect to the inverse temperature, and show that they can be expressed using variance and covariance statistics of centered teacher and student logits under the transformed teacher weighting. This yields an efficient curvature-aware update that requires one softmax evaluation and a constant number of class-wise weighted sums. Experiments on standard image classification distillation benchmarks show that our temperature adaptation generally improves TTM and WTTM, while remaining competitive with or outperforming prior temperature-adaptive distillation baselines.

</details>


### [27] [PathGuide: Dynamic Classifier-Free Guidance via On-Policy Transport Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29107)
*Avishag Nevo,Tamir Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathGuide提出将分类器无关引导（CFG）的标量选择重构成一个在线策略传输（on-policy transport）问题，利用连续性方程的弱形式推导出具有路径正确性解释的选择准则。对于标量CFG，该准则可在每个求解器区间内产生一个严格二次的局部目标，闭式求解最优引导尺度后在线使用或离线拟合为可复用的分段常数调度，实验证明优于固定引导和自适应的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型在条件生成中依赖分类器无关引导（CFG），但CFG的引导尺度通常被视为静态调参。在流式（flow-based）模型中，引导尺度从根本上决定速度场和概率路径，因此引导选择本质上是动态路径优化问题，而非静态调参问题。

Method: 将标量CFG选择重新形式化为在线策略传输问题；借助连续性方程的弱形式推导选择准则（若引导场在生成轨迹上与精确条件场弱等价，则采样路径与目标条件分布一致）；对标量CFG该准则化简为一个严格的二次局部目标，得到每个求解器区间上的闭式选择器；引导尺度可在线计算或离线拟合为分段常数调度。

Result: 在低分辨率图像流形和受控设置下，跨多种连续时间流构建验证了PathGuide的有效性，证明该传输驱动的选择器在路径对齐和样本保真度上均优于固定引导和现有的自适应引导基线。

Conclusion: PathGuide为流模型中CFG的选择提供了首个以传输理论为根基的框架，将引导尺度从静态超参转变为动态最优控制问题，兼具理论保障与实用效率。

Abstract: While modern generative models excel at modeling complex data, precise inference-time control in conditional generation remains a critical challenge. Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a primary mechanism for such control, yet it is typically treated as a static tuning parameter. In flow-based models, however, the guidance scale fundamentally dictates the velocity field and the resulting probability path, making guidance selection a dynamic path-optimization problem. We introduce PathGuide, a framework that reformulates scalar CFG selection as an on-policy transport problem. Leveraging the weak form of the continuity equation, we derive a selection criterion with a direct path-correctness interpretation: we prove that if the guided field is weakly equivalent to the exact conditional field along the generated rollout, the sampler's path coincides with the target conditional law. For scalar CFG, this criterion yields a strictly quadratic local objective with an efficient, closed-form selector for each solver interval. PathGuide enables optimal guidance scales to be computed and used online during generation or fitted offline as a reusable piecewise-constant schedule. We validate our method on low-resolution image manifolds and controlled settings across various continuous-time flow constructions, demonstrating that this transport-based selector improves path alignment and sample fidelity over both fixed and state-of-the-art adaptive guidance baselines.

</details>


### [28] [Explainable Machine Learning for Broadband Adoption Disparities: Tract-Level Prediction and SHAP-Based Factor Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29110)
*Xiao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的机器学习框架（LightGBM + TreeSHAP），基于ACS 2022的65个特征，在全国83,359个普查区粒度上刻画宽带采纳差异，R²=0.533，并通过SHAP聚类识别出三个因子画像（Well-Connected Moderate、Affordability-Limited Severe、Rural-Elderly）。作为筛选工具，ML方法在顶部10%区块中捕获38.0%的总采纳差距，优于仅基于收入的启发式方法（35.2%）。核心贡献是每个普查区的因子分解，为差异化调查提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国通过《基础设施投资与就业法案》拨款约650亿美元用于宽带扩展，但缺乏证据驱动的投资定向方法。现有方法未充分利用可解释机器学习来刻画宽带采纳差异及其驱动因素，需要开发基于证据的方法来优化资源配置。

Method: 采用LightGBM模型，在空间五折交叉验证下使用ACS 2022的65个社会经济、人口和基础设施特征，对83,359个普查区的宽带采纳差距进行建模；使用TreeSHAP进行可解释性分析，通过SHAP聚类识别因子画像，并进行状态留出交叉验证（51折）、时间稳定性检验（ACS 2017→2022）、郡级块自助抽样进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 空间五折交叉验证R²=0.533，Spearman rho=0.763；状态留出验证R²=0.525；时间稳定性rho=0.784。收入和教育是主导因素组，SHAP聚类识别出三个画像：Well-Connected Moderate（约49K区块）、Affordability-Limited Severe（约21K区块）、Rural-Elderly（约13K区块）。ML筛选在顶部10%捕获38.0%的采纳差距，比仅收入启发式高2.8个百分点（p<0.002），在遗憾减少方面关闭了收入与oracle之间19%的差距。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习框架能够有效且稳健地为联邦宽带投资提供定向依据，其核心贡献在于提供每个普查区的因子分解，识别收入/教育、农村性、年龄等特征组与预测差距的关联强度，从而指导差异化调查与投资决策。ML方法在目标筛选效率上显著优于传统仅基于收入的方法。

Abstract: The United States has allocated approximately $65 billion through the Infrastructure Investment and Jobs Act for broadband expansion, yet evidence-based methods for targeting these investments remain underdeveloped. This paper presents an explainable machine learning framework for profiling broadband adoption disparities at census-tract granularity across 83,359 tracts nationwide. Using 65 socioeconomic, demographic, and infrastructure features derived from the American Community Survey 2022, we train a LightGBM model under spatial five-fold cross-validation, achieving R^2 = 0.533 and Spearman rho = 0.763; state-held-out cross-validation (51 folds) confirms generalization (R^2 = 0.525). TreeSHAP analysis identifies income and education as the dominant factor group (with the engineered interaction term absorbing attribution from its constituent features), and SHAP-based clustering reveals three exploratory factor profiles: Well-Connected Moderate (~49K tracts), Affordability-Limited Severe (~21K tracts), and Rural-Elderly (~13K tracts). As a screening tool, ML-based tract selection captures 38.0% of the total adoption gap within the top 10% of tracts versus 35.2% for income-only heuristics (+2.8 pp, p < 0.002, county-block bootstrap); in regret-reduction terms, the model closes 19% of the remaining gap between income-only and oracle selection. The primary contribution is the per-tract factor decomposition: SHAP identifies which feature groups (income/education, rurality, age) are most strongly associated with each tract's predicted gap, and informs differentiated investigation. A temporal stability check, training on ACS 2017 and predicting ACS 2022 with zero survey-year overlap, confirms ranking stability (rho = 0.784, noting hyperparameters tuned on 2022 data).

</details>


### [29] [One Capability or Many? Testing the Economic Validity of Frontier AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29420)
*Louis Yiven Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在421个模型配置、12个基准（其中4个经济基准）的快照上应用潜变量模型，发现单一因子解释了74.5%的共同方差且与模型发布日期高度相关（R²=0.505）。经济基准并不构成独立的能力维度，但通过留一基准检验发现多因子表示对经济分数有更好的预测力，说明经济基准提供增量预测信息，但不应被视为独立潜能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型排行榜基于经济基准对模型进行排名，这些排名影响企业采购、监管审查和工作预期。然而这些经济基准是否测量了与一般测试能力不同的独立能力，还是只是模型整体能力提升这一单一轴线的重新表达，属于尚未被研究的构念效度问题。

Method: 使用哈希固定的排行榜快照，包含421个模型配置、12个基准（4个为经济基准）。将所有基准视为题目、模型视为被试，构建具有预先固定四个假设和阈值的潜变量模型。通过单因子分析检验主导维度，控制日期趋势后重新评估方差解释比例；使用留一基准交叉验证方法（每折内重新估计因子）比较多因子与单因子对经济分数的预测性能。

Result: 单因子解释74.5%的共同方差，且与模型发布日期高度相关（R²=0.505），表明主导能力轴本质上是时间趋势。去除日期趋势后解释比例下降14.9个百分点（每基模型一行时下降24.1点）。预先固定的维度规则下经济基准不构成独立因子，但留一基准检验显示多因子表示比单因子对经济分数预测更好（合并Delta-MSE为0.037，95%自助法区间[0.019, 0.055]）。

Conclusion: 经济基准为以日期驱动的一般因子提供了增量预测信息，但证据不支持将其视为独立的潜在能力。排行榜仍是衡量整体进展的可靠指南，但相隔数月发布的模型之间的大部分差距来自时间因素，因此同期模型的小差距在解读为能力差异前应进行日期校正。

Abstract: Frontier-model leaderboards now rank systems based on economic benchmarks, tests of how well models carry out professional tasks from software engineering to banking workflows, and those rankings inform what organisations buy, what regulators scrutinise, and expectations of how work will change. Whether such benchmarks measure a capability distinct from general test-taking, or re-express the one axis along which every benchmark rises as models improve, is a question of construct validity that has not yet been studied. We test it on a hash-pinned leaderboard snapshot of 421 model configurations across twelve benchmarks, four of them economic, treating benchmarks as items and models as respondents in a latent-variable model with four hypotheses and their thresholds fixed before analysis. A single factor explains 74.5% of common variance and tracks model release date (R^2 = 0.505), so the leading axis of capability is substantially a time trend; where prior work controls for scale, compute adds little once date is removed. Removing the date trend lowers that share by 14.9 points, and by 24.1 with one row per base model. Under the dimensionality rule fixed in advance the economic benchmarks form no distinct factor, yet a leave-one-benchmark-out test with factors re-estimated inside every fold shows that a multi-factor representation predicts held-out economic scores better than a single general index (pooled Delta-MSE 0.037, 95% bootstrap interval [0.019, 0.055]). Economic benchmarks therefore add incremental predictive information to a largely date-driven general factor, and the evidence does not support treating them as a distinct latent capability. Leaderboards remain a sound guide to overall progress, but most of the gap between models released months apart is calendar, so a small gap between contemporaneous models should be date-adjusted before being read as a capability difference.

</details>


### [30] [Locked at the Entrance, Open Inside: Where RLVR Narrows the Solution Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29188)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现RLVR虽然提升pass@1但导致策略解空间收缩，且这种收缩高度集中在推理轨迹的"入口"（首个操作符之前），而非下游推理过程。通过入口定位的干预（晚层参数插值）可在不损失pass@1的情况下提升37%的解覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR显著提升单样本准确率(pass@1)，但导致策略解空间收缩，损害test-time scaling的收益。理解推理轨迹中"广度"究竟在何处丢失（是策略无法访问有效解家族，还是无法执行已启动的计算），是设计针对性干预的前提。

Method: 使用Countdown任务（其解空间可穷举枚举为按首个操作数和操作符定义的离散入口家族），在PPO(Qwen2.5-3B)和GRPO(Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct)两种设置下训练。分析：(1)解覆盖度的变化；(2)入口位置与下游推理的逐token似然偏移；(3)提供未选择入口前缀的修复实验；(4)入口定向干预（晚层参数插值）；(5)在6个数学基准上的熵崩溃泛化分析及SFT/DPO-RLVR管线对比。

Result: 解覆盖度在两个训练设置中下降高达67%，即使在所有检查点都求解的问题上也减半。逐token似然偏移在首个算术操作前比下游推理大11-16倍。提供未选择的入口前缀可使低访问家族完成率恢复一个数量级以上(0.018->0.212, PPO)。晚层参数插值增加37%覆盖度且不损失pass@1。早期熵崩溃在6个数学基准的7B/14B模型上复发，但SFT基线保留2倍以上覆盖度，且分阶段的SFT-DPO-RLVR管线保留早期熵。

Conclusion: 推理广度在"门口"丢失而非"房间内"——策略主要丧失的是对有效解入口的发起能力，而非执行计算的能力。这一定位为设计有效的入口定向干预（如参数插值、分阶段训练）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) substantially improves single-sample accuracy (pass@1) but causes the policy's solution space to contract, diminishing the returns of test-time scaling. In this work, we investigate where inside a reasoning trajectory this breadth is lost: does the policy fail to access a valid solution family, or does it fail to execute computation once initiated? To disentangle access from execution, we analyze the Countdown task, whose solution space can be exhaustively enumerated into discrete entrance families defined by the first operand and operator, across PPO on Qwen2.5-3B and GRPO on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Across both training setups, solution coverage falls by up to 67%, halving even on problems solved across all checkpoints. We show that this contraction is heavily concentrated at the entrance: per-token likelihood shifts are 11x--16x larger prior to the first arithmetic operation than during downstream reasoning. Supplying only an unselected entrance prefix restores completion rates in low-access families by over an order of magnitude (0.018 -> 0.212 under PPO), demonstrating that alternative solutions remain executable but are no longer initiated. Guided by this localization, we find that while surface prompting fails to recover diversity, entrance-targeted interventions succeed: late-layer parameter interpolation with early checkpoints increases solution coverage by 37% at no loss in pass@1. Finally, we show that early-step entropy collapse recurs across six math benchmarks with 7B and 14B models, but is not an inevitable byproduct of reasoning optimization: an SFT baseline preserves more than double the coverage, and staged SFT--DPO--RLVR pipelines retain early-step entropy. In summary, reasoning breadth is lost at the door, not inside the room. Code: https://github.com/ershiyidian/early-branch-locking.

</details>


### [31] [Adaptive Doubly Robust Off-Policy Evaluation for Ranking Policies under Diverse User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29600)
*Kosuke Iguchi,Ren Kishimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应双重稳健估计器（ADR），将自适应逆倾向加权（AIPS）与奖励回归结合，通过控制变量校正来估计排名策略的离线策略评估（OPE），在保持无偏性的同时降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名策略的离线评估面临组合爆炸问题，IPS方差过大；IIPS和RIPS通过固定假设降低方差但可能引入偏差；AIPS自适应边际化重要性权重以平衡偏差-方差权衡，但对较长排名精度仍会下降，且未利用奖励模型进行残差校正。

Method: 提出自适应双重稳健（ADR）估计器，将自适应重要性加权（基于AIPS）与奖励回归相结合，通过控制变量（control-variate）校正方式融合两者。在观察真实用户行为模型的条件下证明其无偏性，并刻画了相对于AIPS降低方差的充分条件。

Result: 在每种条件下进行10,000次模拟的合成实验中，ADR在多种日志数据规模和排名长度下，相较于AIPS及传统排名OPE估计器，均改善了均方误差（MSE）。

Conclusion: ADR通过结合自适应重要性加权与奖励回归，在保持无偏性的同时有效降低估计方差，为排名策略的离线评估提供了一种更优的估计方法，尤其适用于长排名的场景。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) of ranking policies is challenging be- cause selecting and ordering multiple items from a candidate set makes the number of possible rankings grow combinatorially with the number of candidates and the ranking length. Consequently, Inverse Propensity Scoring (IPS), whose importance weight is the full-ranking probability ratio under the evaluation and logging policies, can have excessive variance. Independent IPS (IIPS) and Reward Interaction IPS (RIPS) reduce variance by imposing fixed assumptions on how users browse rankings, but may introduce bias when those assumptions mismatch actual behavior. Adaptive Inverse Propensity Scoring (AIPS) addresses this trade-off by adap- tively marginalizing importance weights over the actions that affect each position-wise reward. It attains minimum variance within a class of unbiased IPS-based estimators when the true user be- havior model is observed. However, its estimation accuracy may still degrade for longer rankings, and AIPS does not use a reward model for residual correction. We propose Adaptive Doubly Robust (ADR), which combines adaptive importance weighting with re- ward regression through a control-variate correction. We establish its unbiasedness when the true user behavior model is observed and characterize a sufficient condition under which it reduces vari- ance relative to AIPS. Across synthetic experiments with 10,000 simulations per condition, ADR improves mean squared error over AIPS and conventional ranking OPE estimators across a range of logged-data sizes and ranking lengths.

</details>


### [32] [HalluPrism: When Multimodal Uncertainty Should Diagnose, Not Decide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29193)
*Aman Prakash,Sourish Dasgupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HalluPrism，一种多模态大语言模型（MLLM）幻觉的行为诊断框架，通过对答案进行视觉退化、空白图替换及接地/关系检查三类定向探针，生成(V, L, A)三维签名，将失败诊断与正确性排序（拒绝评分）解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM会对因不同原因失败的答案赋予相近的置信度，传统单一置信度无法区分失败原因，导致诊断性和可修正性不足。因此需要一种行为诊断方法，能够在拒绝（abstain）之前先刻画失败的结构性原因。

Method: HalluPrism包含三类定向探针：(1) 视觉退化（visual degradation）测量对图像扰动的敏感性V；(2) 空白图替换后置信度保留度L；(3) 接地/关系探针的不稳定性A。在四个基准（HallusionBench、VizWiz、POPE、VSR）上超过5.8万个样本、四个MLLM上进行实验，并采用XGBoost进行联合签名分析。

Result: 图像移除后的置信度保留是最常见的失败模式，而接地/关系探针不稳定性能更好地区分失败家族。48组源-目标检查中仅18组对角线对齐，说明各坐标应联合解释而非独立因果。联合签名将失败家族AUROC从0.634提升至0.769（HallusionBench），从0.707提升至0.817（VizWiz），POPE和VSR有较小提升。XGBoost中从标量置信度0.78提升到(V,L,A)的0.95，加入置信度后达0.97。但直接标量化签名会损害正确性排序。

Conclusion: HalluPrism表明多模态不确定性应先刻画失败结构再用于拒绝或修正决策，失败诊断与准确率排序是两项需要分离的任务。三维签名应联合解读，不能简单标量化使用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can assign similar confidence to answers that fail for different reasons. We propose HalluPrism, a behavioral diagnostic that re-runs an answer after visual degradation, blank-image replacement, and grounding or relation checks. These targeted probes yield a signature over visual-perturbation sensitivity (V ), image-removal confidence retention (L), and grounding/relation-probe instability (A). Across 58K+ examples from four benchmarks and four MLLMs, image-removal confidence retention is most prevalent, while grounding/relation-probe instability better separates failure families. Only 18 of 48 source-target checks are diagonally aligned, so the coordinates should be interpreted jointly rather than as independent causal sources. With the dataset fixed, the joint signature improves failure-family AUROC from 0.634 to 0.769 on HallusionBench and from 0.707 to 0.817 on VizWiz, with smaller gains on POPE and VSR. In pooled XGBoost analysis, AUROC rises from 0.78 with scalar confidence to 0.95 with (V, L, A) and 0.97 when confidence is added. The same signature does not automatically improve correctness ranking. The three tested direct scalarizations can harm it. These results separate failure diagnosis from abstention scoring: multimodal uncertainty should characterize failure structure before it is used to decide whether to abstain or correct.

</details>


### [33] [PokaiTrainer: Scaling Belief-State Search to Competitive Pokémon VGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29197)
*Max Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个将决策时均衡搜索扩展到超大规模、打破传统可解子游戏三个假设的VGC宝可梦对局智能体PokaiEngine与PokaiTrainer，在真实对局平台上达到了接近人类精英水平的胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策时均衡搜索虽已在扑克等游戏中达到超人类水平，但此前仅适用于可解子游戏：每步动作少、随机性局限于发牌、玩家轮流行动。宝可梦官方双打（VGC）同时打破这三项假设——双方从数百个联合选项同步行动、每个联合动作产生数百个随机结果、对手的储备和努力值分配是隐藏信息，因此需要探索如何在此规模下构建强AI。

Method: 构建了Rust战斗引擎PokaiEngine，单遍枚举联合动作的完整加权结果分布，与Pokemon Showdown达到约99%一致性且成本远低于采样。在此基础上，PokaiTrainer将Student of Games算法适配到该规模，将每个决策建模为基于公开信念状态的贝叶斯矩阵博弈，并在明确计算预算下增长子博弈树。

Result: 在Showdown三局两胜真实天梯上，该智能体在150局对局中对抗平均约1320 Elo的人类玩家取得59%的胜率，稳定在1350-1400 Elo区间，峰值曾短暂进入该格式前500名。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了决策时均衡搜索方法在极大动作空间、极大随机性和同时行动等非标准博弈条件下的可行性与有效性，为将这些方法推广到更复杂真实世界博弈提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Decision-time equilibrium search carried poker to superhuman play, but it has so far relied on tractable subgames: a handful of actions per decision, chance confined to card deals, one player moving at a time. Competitive Pokémon in its official doubles format (VGC) breaks all three assumptions at once. Both players act simultaneously from joint menus in the hundreds, each joint action resolves to hundreds of stochastic outcomes, and the opponent's reserves and stat allocations are hidden. We set out to build a strong VGC agent and report what that took. PokaiEngine, our Rust battle engine, enumerates a joint action's full weighted outcome distribution in one pass, at ${\sim}99\%$ parity with Pokémon Showdown and a fraction of the cost of sampling it. On top of the engine, PokaiTrainer adapts Student of Games to this scale, solving every decision as a Bayesian matrix game over public belief states and growing subgames under an explicit compute budget. On the live Showdown best-of-three ladder, the agent wins 59% of 150 sets against a human field averaging ${\sim}1320$ Elo. It settles into a 1350-1400 Elo band, and at its peak briefly entered the format's top 500.

</details>


### [34] [RL-FAT: Reinforcement Learning for Fair Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29247)
*Tejaswini Medi,Levan Mikeladze,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-FAT，一个基于强化学习的公平对抗训练框架，通过策略梯度反馈和公平强调对抗损失来缓解对抗训练中不同类别鲁棒性不平衡的问题，在保持竞争性鲁棒准确率的同时显著降低类别间鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练虽能提升平均鲁棒准确率，但存在严重的类别间鲁棒性不均衡问题：部分类别变得更鲁棒，而其他类别仍极易受到攻击。这种不平衡在视觉任务中引发对抗公平性担忧，因为所有类别都应获得可靠的鲁棒性保障。

Method: RL-FAT将预测分布解释为策略，结合基于正确性的预测奖励与类别级价值估计来计算类别特定的优势函数进行策略梯度优化，使模型自适应关注类别级误分类；同时引入公平强调对抗损失，对对抗损失高的类别施加更强的训练压力，以减轻类别间鲁棒性差距。

Result: 大量实验表明，RL-FAT在达到与标准对抗训练基线相当的鲁棒准确率的同时，显著降低了类别间鲁棒性不平衡。

Conclusion: 通过将强化驱动自适应与公平强调正则化相结合，RL-FAT在提升对抗鲁棒性的同时促进了跨类别更均衡的鲁棒性分布，为对抗公平性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks remain highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, and adversarial training (AT) has become a widely used approach for improving robustness. However, improvements in average robust accuracy often mask substantial class-wise disparities: while some classes become more robust, others may remain disproportionately vulnerable under attack. This imbalance raises an important adversarial fairness concern, particularly in vision tasks where reliable robustness is expected across all categories. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{RL-FAT}, a reinforcement-learning-inspired fair adversarial training framework that uses policy-gradient based feedback from adversarial predictions. RL-FAT interprets the prediction distribution as a policy and combines correctness-based prediction rewards with class-wise value estimates to compute class-specific advantages for policy-gradient optimization. This enables the model to adaptively focus on class-wise misclassification. Furthermore, we introduce a fairness-emphasis adversarial loss that assigns stronger training pressure to classes with high adversarial loss, thereby mitigating class-wise robustness disparity. By combining reinforcement-driven adaptation with fairness-emphasis regularization, RL-FAT improves adversarial robustness while promoting a more balanced robustness distribution across classes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive robust accuracy and substantially reduces class-wise robustness imbalance compared with standard adversarial training baselines.

</details>


### [35] [RSLM: Training-Free Vector Quantization for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30384)
*Rastislav Lenhardt,Teodora Dobos,Thomas Vecchiato,Jiri Isa,Igor Ginzburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RSLM（旋转缩放Lloyd-Max），一种免训练向量量化编解码器家族，可将嵌入压缩至每维度1-4比特，在降低大規模ANN搜索系统内存成本和带宽的同时，保持或提升召回率。其核心创新在于对最终重建向量的L2范数进行校正（而非仅对残差校正），并通过残差化方案和高速实现（FWHT、AVX SIMD、隐写缩放因子编码）在保持更低复杂度的同时超越当前最先进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最先进近似最近邻（ANN）搜索系统通常采用多阶段流水线：先用粗粒度划分筛选候选集，再用较高精度（通常每维度≥8比特）的表示进行近似评分和精排。这种高精度表示带来巨大的内存开销和带宽消耗，成为系统性能瓶颈。作者希望在不牺牲（甚至提升）召回率的前提下，将量化精度从≥8比特降至2-4比特每维度，从而显著降低大规模ANN系统的内存成本和带宽需求。

Method: 1) 提出RSLM（Rotated Scaled Lloyd-Max）免训练向量量化编解码器家族，支持每维度1-4比特压缩。2) 利用ANN系统的特性，对残差向量而非完整向量进行编码，同时应用于近似评分和精排两个阶段。3) 针对MIPS对向量范数敏感的问题，校正量化向量的L2范数——关键创新在于校正最终重建向量的L2范数而非仅残差的范数，该重缩放方案取代了更复杂的Anisotropic loss方案。4) 高效实现：采用块级级联快速Walsh-Hadamard变换（FWHT）获得类线性复杂度，使用AVX SIMD优化码本，并通过隐写编码方式嵌入缩放因子以实现完美的缓存行对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方案在将表示精度降至2-4比特每维度的同时保持或提升召回率，相比现有系统显著降低了内存成本和带宽，并简化了系统复杂度（无需Anisotropic loss等复杂方案）。残差化方案相比通用量化方法提供了更优的质量-大小权衡。

Conclusion: RSLM通过将量化精度从≥8比特降至2-4比特每维度，成功地在简化ANN系统复杂度的同时保持甚至提升了召回率。其核心贡献——对最终重建向量而非残差的L2范数校正，加上残差编码方案和硬件友好的高性能实现，为大规模ANN系统中的向量量化提供了更优的质量-效率权衡。

Abstract: By introducing RSLM (Rotated Scaled Lloyd-Max), a family of training-free vector quantization codecs compressing embeddings to 1--4 bits per dimension, we reduce memory cost and memory bandwidth of a typical large-scale Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search system, while reducing its complexity and keeping or improving recall across multiple benchmark datasets. State-of-the-art systems filter candidates using coarse partitions, approximately score them to narrow the set, and then rescore the best with higher precision representations (often >=8 bits per dimension). Our relativized codecs can bring this down to 2--4 bits per dimension.
  We use the properties of the ANN system to encode residual vectors instead of full vectors, both for the approximate scoring phase and the rescoring phase. Since Maximum Inner Product Search (MIPS) is very sensitive to vector norms, we correct the $L_2$ norms of quantized vectors. Our major innovation is that we correct the $L_2$ norm of the final reconstructed vector rather than just the residual. Our rescaling replaces more complicated schemes, such as Anisotropic loss. The residualization scheme gives us a more favorable quality vs size trade-off than generic quantization methods.
  Our high-performance implementation leverages a block-wise cascaded Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform (FWHT) with linear-like complexity, AVX SIMD-optimized codebooks, and a steganographic encoding of scaling factors for perfect cache-line alignment.

</details>

*Hanul Park,Jeonghoon Choi,Juseong Kim,Sanghun Sel,Giltae Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多分支神经决策树（MBNDT），通过在内部节点学习有序阈值和分支掩码实现可微的多路分割，从而在保持根到叶路径短的前提下提升浅层决策树的预测精度。在21个OpenML二分类基准上，MBNDT在深度受限的单树基线中取得了最佳平均排名和平均平衡准确率，消融实验证实多路分割是性能提升的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二叉决策树在严格的最大深度预算下表达能力不足，因为每个内部节点仅能进行一次阈值决策，导致浅层树难以捕捉复杂决策边界。研究者希望在不增加路径长度的前提下提升树模型的准确率，弥补浅层二叉树表达能力受限的缺陷。

Method: 提出MBNDT（Multi-Branch Neural Decision Tree with Adaptive Pruning），一种单轴对齐的树模型，采用端到端训练方式。每个内部节点在选定特征上学习有序的多组阈值（multi-way splits），同时学习一个分支掩码（branch mask）以自适应调整有效分支数（arity）。训练完成后，将模型转换为确定性的单路径树以供推理使用。

Result: 在21个OpenML二分类基准数据集上，MBNDT在深度受限的单树基线中取得了最佳平均排名和最佳平均平衡准确率。受控消融实验隔离出多路分割是性能提升的核心来源。但MBNDT生成了比其他单树基线更多的叶子节点，存在明确权衡。

Conclusion: MBNDT在短路径约束下能显著提升准确率，但代价是生成更多的叶子节点，因此它最适合于优先考虑短且有界的决策路径下的准确率、而非追求最小全局树规模的场景。

Abstract: Decision trees are attractive for tabular prediction tasks because each prediction follows an interpretable sequence of feature-threshold tests. Under a strict maximum-depth budget, however, conventional binary trees can be under-expressive, since each internal node makes only a single threshold decision. We study shallow-depth tree induction, where the goal is to improve accuracy while keeping root-to-leaf paths short. We propose the Multi-Branch Neural Decision Tree with Adaptive Pruning (MBNDT), a single axis-aligned tree trained end-to-end with differentiable multi-way splits. Each internal node learns ordered thresholds over a selected feature and a branch mask that adapts its effective arity, and the trained model is converted to a deterministic single-path tree for inference. Across 21 OpenML binary-classification benchmarks, MBNDT achieves the best average rank and mean balanced accuracy among depth-constrained single-tree baselines; a controlled ablation isolates multi-way splitting as the source of the gain. These gains come with an explicit trade-off: MBNDT realizes more leaves than the other single-tree baselines, making it best suited when accuracy under short, bounded decision paths is prioritized over minimal global tree size.

</details>


### [37] [PRIME: Mitigating Subgroup Optimization Competition in Shared CTR Top Networks with Plug-in Residual Input-Conditioned Mixture of Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30449)
*Heng Yao,Siyun Hou,Tianying Liu,Yulou Shu,Yong He,Chuan Yuan,Kaibin Qiu,Guowei Chen,Jiayu Zhao,Chao Yu,Ke Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRIME（Plug-in Residual Input-conditioned Mixture of Experts），一种以Dense层为锚点的低秩残差专家混合模块，用于CTR预估模型。PRIME通过零残差初始化保证训练初始时与Dense基线完全一致，利用输入依赖的路由权重实现样本级logit修正，在13个CTR架构、5组种子的实验中取得了稳定的AUC和LogLoss提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR模型虽然特征交互设计各异，但其顶层网络通常都是所有样本共享的单一多层感知机（MLP）。异构的用户、物品和上下文子组会更新相同的参数，弱对齐的学习信号会使聚合梯度成为相互竞争方向的折中。论文在Avazu上通过4个模型和4个语义字段验证了这种竞争：与样本量和标签比例匹配的随机分组相比，语义子组的Top-NN梯度余弦相似度更低（降低0.23-0.37），说明现有的共享顶层网络限制了模型表达能力。

Method: PRIME是一个Dense锚定的低秩残差专家混合模块。其关键设计包括：（1）零残差初始化，使模块在训练开始时与Dense基线完全一致；（2）输入依赖的路由权重，为低秩专家分配权重以进行样本级logit修正；（3）多袋聚合（multi-bag aggregation）和EMA负载偏置，用于稳定条件估计。PRIME是可插拔模块，可直接替换或增强现有CTR模型的Dense层。

Result: 在Avazu和Criteo的留出测试集上，跨13种CTR架构和5组配对种子验证：中位配对AUC增益分别为+0.0022和+0.0066，LogLoss分别降低0.0011和0.0081。在FiBiNET和DCNv2上，PRIME在全部10次种子级AUC比较中优于APG，且使用更少的参数和更低的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 函数保持的条件残差可以在增加输入依赖容量的同时保留Dense路径及其优化稳定性。PRIME证明了在不破坏Dense基线优化特性的前提下，通过输入条件化的低秩残差专家可以有效提升CTR预估性能。

Abstract: Click-through rate (CTR) models vary in feature-interaction design, yet their top networks usually remain a single multilayer perceptron shared by all examples. Heterogeneous user, item, and context subgroups therefore update the same parameters; weakly aligned learning signals make the aggregate gradient a compromise among competing directions. We study the competition on Avazu with 4 models and 4 semantic fields. Across all architectures, semantic subgroups show lower Top-NN gradient cosine similarity than random groups matched by sample size and label ratio, with reductions of 0.23-0.37.
  This competition motivates input-conditioned experts, but directly replacing an established Dense mapping changes its initial function, sharing pattern, and capacity, obscuring the source of gains. We introduce PRIME (Plug-in Residual Input-conditioned Mixture of Experts), a Dense-anchored mixture of low-rank residual experts. PRIME anchors the original prediction and uses zero-residual initialization to match the Dense baseline exactly at training onset. Input-dependent routing weights low-rank experts for example-specific logit corrections; multi-bag aggregation and EMA load biases stabilize conditional estimation.
  We evaluate PRIME on held-out Avazu and Criteo test sets across 13 CTR architectures and five paired seeds. Median paired AUC gains are +0.0022 and +0.0066, with LogLoss reductions of 0.0011 and 0.0081, respectively. On FiBiNET and DCNv2, PRIME outperforms APG in all ten seed-level AUC comparisons while using fewer parameters and lower inference latency on both backbones. These results show that function-preserving conditional residuals add input-dependent capacity while preserving the Dense path and its optimization stability. Code is available at https://github.com/YH-learning/PRIME.

</details>


### [38] [When Do Larger Batches Help Scale LLM Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29296)
*Ziniu Li,Jinbo Wang,Guanhua Huang,Feiyuan Zhang,Pengbo Li,Alex Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大语言模型强化学习（RLHF）中，增大batch size对训练时间到目标（time-to-target）的影响。作者将算法层面与系统层面效应分离：在算法层面（等累计样本数、重调超参数），发现存在近似batch size不变的学习曲线族；在系统层面，利用生成（带宽受限）与训练（计算量随token数扩展）的计算非对称性，推导出决策规则——只有当更大batch的吞吐增益超过其样本数惩罚时才值得采用。实验表明square-root学习率缩放配合Adam可产生近似batch size不变的学习曲线，更大batch在固定硬件上提升生成吞吐最高2.29倍，GRPO中结合吞吐提升与学习率重调可将time-to-target降低最多29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大batch size虽然能降低随机梯度方差，但其统计优势是否真正转化为更低的墙钟时间（wall-clock time-to-target）尚不明确，因为每个更新消耗更多样本且执行更慢。在LLM强化学习中，这种算法与系统的权衡缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 将算法与系统效应分离进行比较：算法层面在等累计样本数下重调batch相关超参数（如square-root学习率缩放），构建近似batch size不变的学习曲线；系统层面利用生成（memory-bandwidth-bound）与训练（计算量随token数扩展）的非对称性分析吞吐差异，并据此推导batch size选择的决策规则。实验采用GRPO和PPO验证该分解框架。

Result: 在算法层面，square-root学习率缩放配合Adam可产生近似batch-size不变的样本索引学习曲线（在有限batch范围内）。在系统层面，更大batch可将生成吞吐提升最多2.29倍（固定硬件）。GRPO中，将吞吐提升与学习率重调结合可降低time-to-target最多29%；而不重调学习率仅增大batch，即使吞吐更高，训练反而更慢。

Conclusion: 大batch size在LLM强化学习中的收益并非自动获得：必须在算法层面重调超参数（如学习率），并确保系统层面的吞吐增益超过样本效率惩罚，才能真正降低time-to-target。该分解框架提供了直接的batch size决策规则。

Abstract: Larger batches reduce the variance of stochastic gradients per update and are therefore often expected to accelerate training. Yet whether this statistical benefit translates into lower wall-clock time-to-target remains unclear, because each update consumes more samples and may take longer to execute. We study this tradeoff in reinforcement learning for large language models. We separate its algorithmic and systems effects by comparing learning and execution along their natural axes. At the algorithmic level, we compare configurations at equal cumulative sample counts while retuning batch-dependent hyperparameters. Over a bounded range of batch sizes, this procedure yields an approximately batch-size-invariant family whose members follow similar sample-indexed learning trajectories. At the systems level, we exploit the computational asymmetry between rollout generation and training: autoregressive generation is often memory-bandwidth-bound at low concurrency, whereas training work scales approximately with the number of processed tokens. Combining these two views yields a direct decision rule: a larger-batch configuration reduces time-to-target only when its throughput gain exceeds its samples-to-target penalty. Experiments with GRPO and PPO support both sides of this decomposition. At the algorithmic level, square-root learning-rate scaling with Adam produces approximately batch-size-invariant learning curves over a bounded range of batch sizes. At the systems level, larger batches improve generation throughput by up to 2.29x on fixed hardware. In GRPO, combining higher throughput with learning-rate retuning reduces time-to-target by up to 29%, whereas increasing the batch without retuning is slower despite its higher throughput.

</details>


### [39] [A Spectral Identifiability Threshold for Dissipative Rate Recovery from Truncated Liouvillian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29302)
*Yujun Ji,Somyajit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究开放量子系统中，需要保留多少李雅普诺夫谱模态才能区分振幅阻尼和退相干的耗散率。在六量子比特Lindblad模型中，作者证明了布居数模态不包含退相干信息，从而给出均匀退相干可辨识性的下界D=2^n。实验表明n=4,5,6时恢复阈值达到该下界，n=3时高于下界；n=6时最小二乘法达到10^-9量级的联合绝对误差，远超四种表格学习方法（4.355×10^-4）。但该优势在谱扰动和横向场打破对易结构时减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统通过不同的耗散过程（振幅阻尼和退相干）损失能量和相位相干性，但这些过程可能产生重叠的动力学特征。李雅普诺夫谱总结了系统的弛豫行为，但需要多少谱模态才能区分底层耗散率尚不明确。作者旨在回答这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者在六量子比特Lindblad模型中解析推导了李雅普诺夫谱，仅保留最慢的非稳态谱模态，依次增加保留模态数量，测试各耗散率何时变得可恢复。比较了最小二乘法与四种表格学习方法的性能，并进行了鲁棒性测试（谱扰动、加入横向场破坏对易结构）。

Result: 布居数模态不含退相干信息，给出均匀退相干可辨识性的下界D=2^n。n=4,5,6时恢复阈值达到该下界，n=3时高于下界。n=6时最小二乘法联合绝对误差约10^-9，而四种表格学习方法仅达4.355×10^-4。但谱扰动和横向场会削弱此优势。

Conclusion: 所保留谱信息的数量与结构可以决定耗散参数是否可恢复，且这一结论独立于所用估计器。研究同时指出结论仅适用于无噪声模拟谱而非测量所得谱。

Abstract: Open quantum systems lose energy and phase coherence through different dissipative processes, but these processes can produce overlapping dynamical signatures. The Liouvillian spectrum summarizes how such a system relaxes, yet it is not obvious how much of that spectrum is needed to distinguish the underlying dissipation rates. We study this question for amplitude damping and dephasing in a six-qubit Lindblad model whose spectrum can be derived analytically. We retain only the slowest non-steady spectral modes and ask how many are required before each dissipative rate becomes recoverable. We show that population modes contain no dephasing information, which creates a lower bound of D = 2^n retained modes for uniform dephasing identifiability in the relevant rate regime. The measured recovery threshold reaches this bound at n = 4,5,6, while n = 3 remains above it. At n = 6, least squares achieves a mean joint absolute error of order 10^-9, compared with 4.355 x 10^-4 for four tabular learning methods. Robustness tests show that this advantage weakens when the spectra are perturbed and when a transverse field breaks the commuting structure. These results show that the amount and structure of retained spectral information can determine whether dissipative parameters are recoverable, independently of the estimator used. The present conclusions apply to noise-free simulator spectra rather than measurement-derived spectra.

</details>


### [40] [MEL: Coordinate-Preserving EEG Tokenization for fMRI Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29304)
*Xiangyu Liu,Zeting Yan,Zhitong Yin,Boyang Li,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MEL（Multi-band EEG Latent-state Tokenization）框架，通过坐标保持的EEG表示将脑电图（EEG）翻译为功能性磁共振成像（fMRI），显式建模血流动力学延迟与频谱-空间动态，在VU EEG-fMRI基准和外部Oddball数据上超越NeuroBOLT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG到fMRI的翻译对医学神经成像、临床脑状态监测和多模态神经解码具有重要意义，但现有工作侧重于更强的解码器，忽视了表示-接口不匹配的关键问题：fMRI响应存在延迟、时间整合和空间分布特性，而通用EEG编码往往混淆时间延迟、通道身份和频带结构。

Method: MEL将每个目标fMRI响应锚定到其之前的EEG历史，并将其组织为延迟-通道-频率的神经状态token，通过显式捕获血流动力学延迟和频谱-空间动态，实现与fMRI相关的EEG表示与容量受控读出器的对齐，不依赖模型规模扩展。

Result: 在VU EEG-fMRI基准和外部Oddball数据上，MEL在预测性能上优于强NeuroBOLT基线。消融实验和对照实验表明性能提升来自结构化EEG表示而非数据泄漏、捷径统计或解码器容量。

Conclusion: EEG到fMRI转换的核心瓶颈不仅在于解码器能力，更在于表示层面的接口不匹配；通过坐标保持的结构化EEG表示设计，可以在不依赖模型规模的情况下有效提升跨模态翻译性能。

Abstract: Translating electroencephalography (EEG) into functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is important for medical neuroimaging, clinical brain-state monitoring, and multimodal neural decoding, because it aims to infer spatially organized hemodynamic activity from fast and accessible electrophysiological recordings. Existing EEG-to-fMRI studies mainly pursue stronger decoders, but the problem is also constrained by a representation-interface mismatch: fMRI responses are delayed, temporally integrated, and spatially distributed, whereas generic EEG encodings often entangle temporal lag, channel identity, and frequency-band structure. We propose Multi-band EEG Latent-state Tokenization (MEL), a coordinate-preserving EEG representation framework that anchors each target fMRI response to its preceding EEG history and organizes it into lag-channel-frequency neural-state tokens. By explicitly capturing hemodynamic latency and spectral-spatial dynamics, MEL aligns fMRI-pertinent EEG representations with capacity-controlled readouts without depending entirely on model scaling. Experiments on VU EEG-fMRI benchmarks and external Oddball data show that MEL improves prediction over strong NeuroBOLT baselines. Ablations and controls further indicate that the gains come from structured EEG representation rather than leakage, shortcut statistics, or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [41] [Information-Based Calibration of Uncertainty Quantification in Product-of-Experts Gaussian Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29349)
*Yean Hoon Ong,Paolo Barucca,Wei Pan,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GP-pro-c，一种基于信息论方法的专家乘积（product-of-experts）高斯过程回归模型，通过利用信息增益的单调性和子模性定义校准比率，修复局部专家模型因在不相交数据子集上训练而导致的方差过高估计问题，在保持预测精度和降低计算复杂度的同时，实现了NLL平均降低2.3%、ENCE降低12.0%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一全局高斯过程（GP-glo）回归的计算复杂度为立方级别，难以扩展到大规模数据集。专家乘积GP模型（GP-pro）通过组合局部GP模型来缓解计算负担，但局部专家在不相交数据子集上训练会导致后验方差被高估，影响不确定性估计的可靠性。

Method: 提出GP-pro-c模型，利用信息增益在高斯过程中的单调性和子模性，定义一种校准比率（calibration ratio），用于降低各个局部GP模型的后验方差，从而对GP-pro模型的方差进行校准。

Result: 在四个合成函数和六个回归数据集上的实验表明，与未校准的GP-pro模型相比，GP-pro-c在NLL上平均降低2.3%，在ENCE上平均降低12.0%，同时保持了预测精度并降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: GP-pro-c有效缓解了后验方差高估问题，为可扩展GP模型提供了一种有前景的不确定性估计方法，并有望作为高维大规模贝叶斯优化的替代代理模型。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) regression with a single global GP (GP-glo) incurs cubic computational cost, limiting scalability to large datasets. Product-of-experts GP models (GP-pro), which combine local GP models to capture global correlations, alleviate this computational burden. However, training local experts on disjoint data subsets can lead to overestimated posterior variances. We propose GP-pro-c, a product-of-experts GP model that calibrates these variances using an information-based method. The method exploits the monotonicity and submodularity of information gain in GPs to define a calibration ratio that reduces the posterior variance of individual local GP models. We evaluate GP-pro-c using negative log-likelihood (NLL), root mean squared error (RMSE), and expected normalised calibration error (ENCE). Experiments on four synthetic functions and six regression datasets show that GP-pro-c achieves average reductions of 2.3% in NLL and 12.0% in ENCE compared with the uncalibrated GP-pro model. The proposed method mitigates posterior variance overestimation while maintaining predictive accuracy and reducing computational complexity. GP-pro-c provides a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in scalable GP models and may serve as a useful surrogate model for Bayesian optimisation with high-dimensional and large-scale data.

</details>

*Qiming Guo,Wenbo Sun,Ye Wang,Wenlu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IsleNet 提出了一种基于空间熵引导分区和虚拟边重连的时空图遗忘学习方法，通过仅重训练受影响子图编码器和虚拟边层来实现精确且高效的节点数据删除，在四个真实基准上达到全图94%的精度并将遗忘时间缩短一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空图广泛用于交通、天气和医疗监控等领域，但GDPR和CCPA等隐私法规要求从训练模型中完全删除未经授权的数据。由于时空图中信息通过空间和时间消息传递全局传播，完全擦除节点影响通常需要昂贵的全图重训练，因此如何在保证精确删除（exactness）的同时保持效率（efficiency）成为核心挑战。

Method: IsleNet采用空间熵引导的分区策略，创建平衡且局部连贯的子图，并用轻量级虚拟边（virtual edges）将它们重新连接。当收到遗忘请求时，仅重训练受影响的子图编码器和虚拟边层，从而以低成本实现精确的数据移除。

Result: 在四个真实基准数据集上，IsleNet在不牺牲精度的前提下（达到全图精度的94%），将遗忘时间减少了最多一个数量级（约10倍）。

Conclusion: IsleNet证明了通过合理的图分区与虚拟边连接策略，可以同时兼顾时空图机器学习的精确遗忘要求和计算效率，为隐私合规下的时空图模型提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal graphs underpin applications such as traffic forecasting, weather forecasting, and healthcare monitoring. Privacy regulations such as the GDPR and the CCPA require the complete removal of unauthorized data from trained models, but achieving this on a spatiotemporal graph is difficult: because information propagates globally through both spatial and temporal message passing, fully erasing a node's influence forces costly full-graph retraining. ST-graph unlearning requires both exactness and efficiency. We propose IsleNet, which uses spatial-entropy-guided partitioning to create balanced, locally coherent subgraphs and reconnects them with lightweight virtual edges. Upon an unlearning request, only the affected subgraph encoder and virtual-edge layer are retrained, ensuring exact removal with low cost. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that IsleNet attains up to 94% of full-graph accuracy while reducing unlearning time by up to an order of magnitude. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wenlu-lab/STGraphUnlearning.

</details>

*Qiming Guo,Wenbo Sun,Chen Pan,Ye Wang,Wenlu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CallosumNet，一个受大脑胼胝体结构启发的时空图数据遗忘（unlearning）框架。它通过虚拟边重建子图，并用轻量级元图整合层恢复子图间的时空依赖，在完全遗忘单个节点数据的同时保持与重新训练模型相近的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空图数据广泛用于时序预测、分子动力学和医疗监测等场景，但GDPR/CCPA等隐私法规要求对未经授权的数据进行彻底遗忘。由于时空图中节点信息在空间和时间两个维度全局扩散，现有针对静态图和局部数据删除的遗忘方法无法高效擦除单个节点，其代价接近完全重新训练模型。

Method: CallosumNet包含两个关键技术贡献：(1) 利用受生物学启发的虚拟边重建子图；(2) 通过轻量级的元图整合层恢复子图间相互关联的时空依赖关系。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal graphs are widely used in modeling complex dynamic processes such as temporal forecasting, molecular dynamics, and healthcare monitoring. Recently, stringent privacy regulations such as GDPR and CCPA have introduced significant new challenges for existing spatio-temporal graph models, requiring complete unlearning of unauthorized data. Since each node in a spatio-temporal graph diffuses information globally across both spatial and temporal dimensions, existing unlearning methods primarily designed for static graphs and localized data removal cannot efficiently erase a single node without incurring costs nearly equivalent to full model retraining. To address this, we propose CallosumNet, a spatio-temporal graph unlearning framework biologically inspired by the corpus callosum structure. CallosumNet makes two key technical contributions: (1) it reconstructs subgraphs using biologically-inspired virtual edges; and (2) it restores interlinked spatio-temporal dependencies among subgraphs via a lightweight meta-graph integration layer. Empirical results on four diverse real-world datasets show that CallosumNet achieves complete unlearning while maintaining accuracy very close to the gold model. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wenlu-lab/STGraphUnlearning.

</details>

*Shao-An Yin,Mingyi Hong,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向强单调博弈中共享线性等式约束的分布式连续时间算法，无需交换拉格朗日乘子即可收敛到广义纳什均衡（GNE）的任意点，降低了通信开销并提升了隐私性，同时在多机器人部署任务上验证了方法的有效性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在求解强单调博弈的变分广义纳什均衡（v-GNE）时，需要通过交换拉格朗日乘子来达成共识，这带来了较高的通信开销和隐私泄露风险。作者旨在提出一种无需乘子交换的完全分布式算法，以降低通信负担并增强隐私保护。

Method: 提出一种针对共享线性等式约束的完全分布式连续时间算法，不依赖拉格朗日乘子的交换即可收敛到任意广义纳什均衡点。同时提供了相应的离散时间方案。

Result: 该方法不仅能收敛到任意GNE（而非仅限于v-GNE），还避免了乘子交换，在通信开销和隐私保护方面优于现有方法，并在多机器人部署任务中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式算法在共享线性等式约束的强单调博弈中有效，既能降低通信开销、提升隐私性，又能收敛到任意GNE，并通过多机器人放置实验证明了其可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent machine learning research has increasingly focused on equilibrium analysis in non-cooperative games rather than solely on optimal solutions. Many such problems involve shared constraints and can be formulated as Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems (GNEPs). For strongly monotone games, existing methods compute consensus-based variational GNEs (v-GNEs) by exchanging Lagrange multipliers. We propose a fully distributed continuous-time algorithm for shared linear equality constraints that converges without multiplier exchange and reaches any GNE, reducing communication overhead and improving privacy. Discrete-time schemes are also provided, and the method is validated on a multi-robot placement task.

</details>


### [45] [Where Induction Runs Out: Description-Length Difficulty and the Memorisation Gap in Integer-Sequence Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29411)
*Sabilashan Ganeshan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用二部分最小描述长度（MDL）作为精确可计算的参考学习器，评估OEIS整数序列在语言模型基准测试中实际衡量的能力。发现：（1）MDL难度等价于参数计数，由算子阶数和度的组合可辨识性边界精确预测，且与项幅值无关；（2）在20000条序列中，89.98%在某个前缀上可拟合递推关系但在全长上无法拟合，称为"荒野"现象；（3）语言模型在无理论时会正确规避而非臆造，自信错误集中在容易的记忆化区域，说明OEIS基准主要衡量记忆而非归纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS整数序列被广泛用于评估语言模型的数学推理能力，但现有基准缺乏对"实际测量什么"的严格分析。作者希望用精确可计算的MDL参考学习器来建立难度信号，揭示现有基准的真实测量属性。

Method: 在P-递归（全纯）递推函数类上使用二部分最小描述长度（MDL）作为参考学习器，对每个序列的每个前缀计算符号假设与逐字存储之间的MDL比较。联合使用组合可辨识性边界预测发现点，并对三个语言模型在按MDL区间分层的序列上进行评估，检验预注册假设。

Result: （1）发现点由参数计数精确预测，且对项幅值不变（缩放Fibonacci十二个数量级nd不变）；（2）89.98%的可拟合前缀序列在全长上不拟合递推关系，揭示"荒野"现象；（3）预注册假设被推翻：语言模型不臆造，而是适当规避；自信错误集中在容易记忆化区间，表明模型依赖识别而非规则归纳。

Conclusion: OEIS派生基准主要衡量记忆化而非数学推理。MDL提供廉价、无污染且可精确计算的难度信号，可用于改进现有基准设计。

Abstract: Integer sequences from the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences (OEIS) are increasingly used to benchmark mathematical reasoning in language models. We ask what such benchmarks actually measure, using an exactly computable reference learner: two-part minimum description length (MDL) over the class of P-recursive (holonomic) recurrences, evaluated on every prefix of a sequence as terms arrive. Three findings follow. First, MDL difficulty is a parameter count. The discovery point nd, the first prefix length at which a symbolic hypothesis beats verbatim storage, is predicted almost exactly by a combinatorial identifiability bound on the selected operator's order and degree. It is invariant to term magnitude: scaling Fibonacci over twelve orders of magnitude leaves nd unchanged, because a hypothesis must encode its own initial conditions and the magnitude cancels. Second, at scale the learner exhibits a regime our curated corpus could not produce even once: across 20,000 OEIS sequences, 89.98% of those that fit a recurrence on some prefix fit none at full length. We call this the wilderness -- induction acquires a theory, loses it, and never recovers. Third, evaluating three language models on sequences stratified by these MDL regimes refuted our pre-registered hypothesis: models do not confabulate where MDL reports no theory, but hedge appropriately. Confident errors are inverted, concentrating on the easy stratum, where apparent competence tracks recognition of the sequence rather than induction of its rule. OEIS-derived benchmarks therefore substantially measure memorisation, and MDL supplies a cheap, contamination-free difficulty signal they currently lack. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [46] [Scalable Clinical Data Infrastructure and Comparative ML Evaluation for Hospitalisation Risk Prediction in Elderly Patients with Multiple Long-Term Conditions using CPRD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29419)
*Asra Aslam,Volodymyr Chapman,Maurice M. O'Connell,Aseel S. Abuzour,Michael Abaho,Danushka Bollegala,Gary Leeming,Eduard Shantsila,Andrew Clegg,Lauren E. Walker,Iain Edward Buchan,Samuel D. Relton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在CPRD Aurum电子健康记录中对老年患者进行患者时间线建模，系统比较了TG-CNN、LASSO逻辑回归和随机森林在预测12个月全因急诊住院风险上的性能。研究发现，尽管各模型判别力相近（AUC-ROC波动于0.702-0.733），但经Platt校准后，LASSO具有唯一的可接受校准斜率（0.817），而TG-CNN严重校准不足（0.391）。作者主张简单、可解释且校准良好的LASSO模型比判别力略高但校准差的模型更适合临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在电子健康记录（EHR）患者轨迹建模中被广泛提出，但其相较于更简单、更可解释模型的优势鲜少在真实临床环境中经受严格实证检验。作者旨在通过严格的基准测试，评估复杂深度学习模型是否真的优于传统模型，并强调判别力之外校准性对临床决策的重要性。

Method: 构建了一个全面的患者时间线流程，应用于CPRD Aurum中的老年患者，涵盖260种临床状况，通过三层自动化框架进行分类（含17种复杂状况的专门检测逻辑）。基于该基础设施，在交叉验证和独立测试集上对比了TG-CNN、LASSO正则化逻辑回归和随机森林在预测12个月全因急诊住院风险上的性能（AUC-ROC和校准性）。

Result: 交叉验证下TG-CNN的平均AUC-ROC略高于LASSO（0.712 vs 0.705），但在独立测试集上LASSO取得最高判别力（AUC-ROC 0.733，随机森林0.710，TG-CNN 0.702）。Platt校准后，仅有LASSO具有可接受的校准斜率（0.817），随机森林为0.759，TG-CNN仅0.391，严重失准。

Conclusion: 判别力（AUC-ROC）单独不足以作为临床部署的评估标准。在预测12个月急诊住院风险的任务中，LASSO虽然是判别力最高的模型，且是唯一校准可接受的模型，是最适合直接临床部署的选择。论文为机器学习与医疗健康社区提供了关于数据基础设施、模型选择、以及高风险决策支持中校准性和可解释性价值的经验教训。

Abstract: Deep learning architectures are increasingly proposed for patient trajectory modeling in electronic health records (EHRs), yet their advantage over simpler, more interpretable models is rarely subjected to rigorous empirical scrutiny in real-world clinical settings. We present a comprehensive patient timeline pipeline applied to elderly patients in CPRD Aurum, incorporating 260 clinical conditions classified via a three-tier automated framework including specialised detection logic for 17 complex conditions. Using this infrastructure, we benchmark Temporal Graph Convolutional Neural Networks (TG-CNN) against Logistic Regression with LASSO regularisation and Random Forests for predicting 12-month all-cause emergency hospitalisation risk, motivated by (but not filtered to) the elevated risk of adverse drug reactions. Under cross-validation, TG-CNN achieves a marginally higher mean AUC-ROC than LASSO (0.712 vs. 0.705), whereas on the held-out test set LASSO achieves the highest discrimination of three models (AUC-ROC 0.733, versus 0.710 for Random Forest and 0.702 for TG-CNN). We show, that discrimination alone is an incomplete criterion for clinical deployment: after Platt calibration, LASSO is the only model with an acceptable calibration slope (0.817), while Random Forest (0.759) and, TG-CNN (0.391) remain substantially miscalibrated. We argue that LASSO, not the highest-discriminating model, is the model best suited to direct clinical deployment. We present lessons for the machine learning and healthcare community regarding data infrastructure, model selection, and value of calibration and interpretability in high-stakes decision support.

</details>
