TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可训练神经细胞自动机（NCA）的代理模型用于学习长时间偏微分方程（PDE）动力学。与一次性地将整个初始场映射到完整轨迹不同，该模型学习一个小的、局部的、均匀的更新规则，在每个网格单元上相同且重复地应用，模拟微分算子的局部性。在五种经典PDE（热方程、平流方程、Burgers方程、Allen-Cahn方程和Fisher-KPP方程）上，与PDE-Net、改进的PINN和FNO三种基线方法进行比较，并在超出训练时间域两倍的条件下进行测试，该模型在大部分实验中取得了最低的长时域相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PDE数值求解器在变化初始条件下反复求解时计算成本高昂，因而需要学习型代理模型。现有的代理模型如PDE-Net、PINN和FNO虽然有效，但在长时域外推方面仍存在精度不足的问题，亟需一种能更好地利用微分算子局部性特征的模型来提高长时间动力学的预测能力。

Method: 提出一种可训练的神经细胞自动机（NCA）代理模型。该模型不一次性将整个初始场映射到完整轨迹，而是学习一个小的、局部的、均匀的更新规则，该规则在每个网格单元上以相同方式重复应用，从而镜像微分算子的局部性特征。通过与PDE-Net、改进的PINN和Fourier Neural Operator（FNO）三种基线进行对比，在五种经典PDE上评估模型的性能。

Result: 在五种规范PDE（热方程、平流方程、Burgers方程、Allen-Cahn方程和Fisher-KPP方程）上，在评估时间域超出训练时间域两倍的条件下进行测试，所提出的NCA模型在大部分实验中取得了最低的长时域相对误差。

Conclusion: 基于NCA的代理模型通过利用更新的局部性和同质性，在长时域PDE动力学预测上优于现有多类主流基线方法，是一种有效的PDE学习代理方案。

Abstract: Classical numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) are computationally expensive to solve repeatedly across varying initial conditions, motivating the need for learned surrogates. In this paper, we propose a trainable Neural Cellular Automata (NCA) based surrogate model for learning long time PDE dynamics. Rather than mapping an entire initial field to a full trajectory in one shot, our proposed model learns a small, local, homogeneous update rule that is applied identically and repeatedly at every grid cell, mirroring the locality of differential operators. We benchmark this framework against three baselines: PDE - Net, a modified physics-informed neural network (PINN), and a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), on five canonical PDEs (heat, advection, Burgers, Allen - Cahn, and Fisher - KPP), evaluated at temporal domain two times beyond the training temporal domain. The proposed model achieves the lowest long-horizon relative errors on the majority of the experiments.

</details>


### [116] [Coarse composition suffices: tabular in-context learning for multi-activity antimicrobial peptide profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30337)
*Raunak Kumar,Anuj Pal,Dhruvi Solanki,Parikshit Pareek,Juhi Singh,Jitin Singla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖序列的简单管线（330个可解释序列描述符 + TabPFN表格基座模型），在ESCAPE多标签抗菌肽活性预测基准上超越了依赖多模态/结构条件特征的复杂深度模型，实现mAP-5=77.8%（此前最优为72.1%），并通过概率分类器链首次在所有五个标签上同时达到或超过最佳平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌肽（AMPs）常具有跨多类病原体的活性，因此多标签活性预测比二分类更切合实际筛查需求。ESCAPE基准形式化了该任务，但现有领先方法依赖多模态、结构条件的深度模型，训练与调参成本高昂。作者旨在探索一条更简单、更可解释且性能更优的纯序列方案。

Method: 将330个可解释的序列描述符与TabPFN（一种无需梯度训练和超参搜索即可进行上下文预测的表格基座模型）相结合，构建纯序列多标签预测管线。采用标签幂集（label-powerset）策略训练TabPFN模型，并使用概率分类器链（probabilistic classifier chain）来显式建模标签间依赖关系。

Result: 在ESCAPE基准（82,359条肽段，五个标签）上，标签幂集TabPFN模型取得mAP-5=77.8%，较此前最优的72.1%有显著提升；概率分类器链在所有五个标签上同时匹配或超过最佳发表的平均精度。在先前最优的单折训练协议下增益依然保持，表明非训练集规模效应；对远程同源（序列同一性低于30%）的增益最大（+11.2个百分点）。消融实验显示推断时无需预测结构，且10个全局物理化学标量即可恢复全特征性能的91%。显式建模标签依赖对稀缺活性有针对性收益，并支持从部分阳性证据中排序下一个待测活性。

Conclusion: 简洁的纯序列管线即可在多标签AMP活性预测中超越复杂多模态深度模型，兼具高性能、低训练成本与可解释性；显式标签依赖建模在多标签场景中具有实用价值，为后续活性筛选的优先级排序提供了新思路。

Abstract: Antimicrobial peptides (AMPs) often act against multiple pathogen classes, making multi-label activity prediction a more realistic screening target than binary antimicrobial classification. The ESCAPE benchmark formalizes this setting, but leading approaches typically rely on multimodal, structure-conditioned deep models that are costly to train and tune. We show that a simple, sequence-only pipeline can match and surpass these methods by combining 330 interpretable sequence descriptors with TabPFN, a tabular foundation model that performs in-context prediction in a single forward pass without gradient-based training or hyperparameter search. On ESCAPE (82,359 peptides; five labels), a label-powerset TabPFN model achieves mAP-5 = 77.8%, improving on the previously best reported 72.1%. A probabilistic classifier chain is the first method to match or exceed the best published average precision on each of the five labels simultaneously. The gains persist under the prior state-of-the-art single-fold training protocol, indicating they are not a training-set-size artefact, and are largest for remote homologues (+11.2 points below 30% sequence identity). Ablations further show that predicted structure is unnecessary at inference and that performance is not driven by any single descriptor family: ten global physicochemical scalars recover 91% of full-feature performance. Finally, explicitly modelling label dependence yields targeted benefits for scarce activities and supports ranking which activity to assay next from partial positive evidence.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Churn: Predicting Financial Fragmentation in Retail Banking with Temporal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30364)
*Ananyaa Chopra,Brandon Xu,Brendan Yuen,Lauren Zung,Sarabroop Aulakh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"金融碎片化"概念（客户在完全流失前先将部分存款、投资和经常性业务转移至外部金融机构的状态），并构建了一个四阶段XGBoost级联的时间机器学习系统，用于在90天内预测外部转账或投资事件。基于595,220条客户月度观测和346个工程特征，系统预测外部流出是否发生、金额、源产品及目的地机构，取得了良好的判别性能（PR-AUC 0.823，F1 0.803）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零售银行流失预测将客户流失视为终止性二元事件，忽视了客户关系在此之前已通过部分资金迁移逐步弱化的现实。作者旨在将分析焦点从终端流失前移到早期资金迁移（即金融碎片化），以便银行能在客户完全脱离之前实施主动干预，从而更有效地进行客户留存。

Method: 研究使用来自大型零售银行的匿名多源数据，构建595,220条客户月度观测，包含346个工程特征（月度客户画像、余额、产品关系、历史资金流行为、宏观经济条件和竞争对手活动）。采用四阶段XGBoost级联架构：（1）预测90天内是否发生外部流出，（2）预测预期流出金额，（3）预测源产品，（4）预测目的地金融机构。

Result: 主分类器测试PR-AUC为0.823；验证阈值下精确率86.4%、召回率75.1%、F1为0.803。按碎片化评分排序，前1%客户精确率达95.3%，前5%捕获了78.7%的流出案例。金额模型将94.9%的预测落在相邻金额桶内；目的地预测在27个类别上宏F1为0.81；源产品预测加权F1为0.92。

Conclusion: 将分析焦点从终端流失转向早期资金迁移，该框架为主动、可解释且具有经济信息量的客户留存决策支持提供了实用基础，使银行能够在客户完全流失前识别并干预。

Abstract: Retail banking attrition is usually represented as a terminal binary event, even though client relationships often weaken earlier through partial movements of deposits, investments, and recurring activity to external financial institutions. This paper defines that preceding state as financial fragmentation and presents an end-to-end temporal machine-learning system for predicting it before complete disengagement. Using anonymized multi-source data from a large retail bank, the framework predicts whether a valid external transfer or investment event will occur within 90 days. The study uses 595,220 client-month observations, with 346 engineered features combining monthly client profiles, balances, product relationships, prior flow-of-funds behavior, macroeconomic conditions, and competitor activity. A four-stage XGBoost cascade estimates (1) whether an external outflow will occur within 90 days, (2) the expected amount, (3) the originating product, and (4) the destination financial institution. The primary classifier achieved a test precision-recall area under the curve of 0.823. At the validation-selected threshold, it produced 86.4% precision, 75.1% recall, and an F1 score of 0.803. Ranking test observations in descending Stage 1 fragmentation score, the top 1% of clients yielded 95.3% precision, while the top 5% captured 78.7% of observed outflow cases. The amount model placed 94.9% of predictions within an adjacent amount bucket. Destination prediction reached a macro-F1 of 0.81 across 27 classes; source-product prediction achieved a weighted F1 of 0.92. By moving the analytical focus from terminal churn to earlier fund migration, the proposed approach provides a practical foundation for proactive, explainable, and economically informed client-retention decision support.

</details>


### [118] [Mode Connectivity Beyond Classifiers: Evidence from Generative and Contrastive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30366)
*Chengzheyi Yao,Yongzhao Zhang,Yongding Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将模式连通性（mode connectivity）从传统的分类器模型扩展到生成模型（DDPM）和对比学习模型（NanoCLIP）领域，提出了一种架构感知的连接构建算法，通过实证研究成功发现独立训练的DDPM和NanoCLIP模式之间存在连续的低损失路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式连通性研究主要局限于基于分类器的模型，尚不清楚类似几何性质是否存在于现代复杂模型（如生成模型和对比学习模型）中，这一开放问题构成了本文的研究动机。

Method: 提出了一种架构感知（architecture-aware）的连接构建算法，专门针对DDPM和CLIP的独特架构进行设计，用于在独立训练的生成和对比学习模型之间构建低损耗连接路径。

Result: 大量实证结果表明，首次成功在独立训练的DDPM和NanoCLIP模式之间发现了模式连通性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解现代生成模型和对比学习模型损失景观的几何性质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The loss landscape of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) exhibits highly complex and non-convex properties. Recent studies have revealed the phenomenon of mode connectivity, demonstrating that independently trained network modes can be connected via a continuous low-loss path. However, existing mode connectivity research is predominantly confined to classifier-based models, leaving it an open question whether similar geometric properties exist in modern complex models. In this paper, we extend the boundaries of mode connectivity to generative and contrastive domains (specifically DDPM and NanoCLIP). Addressing the unique architecture of DDPM and CLIP, we propose an architecture-aware connection building algorithm. Extensive empirical results demonstrate for the first time that we successfully discover mode connectivity between independently trained DDPM and NanoCLIP modes. Our work provides a novel perspective for understanding the geometric properties of the loss landscapes in modern generative and contrastive models.

</details>


### [119] [Beat-Synchronous Tokenization for ECG Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30367)
*Ahmed Sameh,Nolan Wilson,Max Enderlein,Yogatheesan Varatharajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Transformer的心电图（ECG）模型中的分词策略，提出基于心跳同步的tokenization作为固定时间分块的替代方案。实验在PTB-XL和Icentia11k两个数据集上验证了三种心跳对齐策略（重采样心跳、自适应池化心跳、重采样心跳加R-R间期信息），发现重采样心跳token在PTB-XL上取得最高平均macro AUROC（0.8945），并将平均序列长度从100减少到11.2个token，同时在Icentia11k上与固定分块性能相当且更稳定，表明心跳同步分词是高效且有竞争力的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer ECG模型通常将波形分割为固定时间长度的心跳token，但这种固定分块方式可能会将单个心跳结构切碎到多个token边界中，破坏心电图的生理学结构完整性。作者希望探索基于心跳同步的tokenization方法，使token边界与心跳的生理节律对齐，从而保留完整的心跳形态学信息。

Method: 作者将固定时间分块与三种心跳对齐的分词策略进行比较：(1) 重采样心跳（resampled beats），将每个心跳重采样为统一长度；(2) 自适应池化心跳（adaptive pooled beats）；(3) 重采样心跳并附加R-R间期信息。实验设置包括两个场景：10秒12导联诊断分类（PTB-XL，使用MIMIC-IV-ECG掩码预训练）和60秒单导联心律分类（Icentia11k，使用患者级对比预训练）。

Result: 在PTB-XL上，重采样心跳token达到了最高的平均macro AUROC（0.8945），并且在平均macro AUPRC（0.7414）上几乎与最佳固定分块方法持平，同时将平均序列长度从100个token减少到仅11.2个token。在Icentia11k上，心跳同步分词器获得了与固定分块相当的AUPRC，且在不同运行之间具有更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 形态学保留的心跳tokenization是一种紧凑且有竞争力的替代固定时间分块的方法，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下显著压缩序列长度，并提高模型运行的稳定性。这为ECG Transformer模型的tokenization提供了生理学可解释的改进方向。

Abstract: Transformer-based electrocardiogram (ECG) models commonly tokenize waveforms into fixed temporal patches. Though convenient, fixed patching can split heartbeat structures across token boundaries. We study beat-synchronous tokenization as a physiologically grounded alternative, comparing fixed patches with three beat-aligned strategies: resampled beats, adaptive pooled beats, and resampled beats augmented with R--R interval information. Experiments span two settings: 10-second 12-lead diagnostic classification on PTB-XL after MIMIC-IV-ECG masked pretraining, and 60-second single-lead rhythm classification on Icentia11k after patient-level contrastive pretraining. On PTB-XL, resampled beat tokens achieve the highest mean macro Area Under the ROC Curve (AUROC; 0.8945) and nearly match the best fixed-patch macro Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC; 0.7414), reducing average sequence length from 100 to 11.2 tokens. On Icentia11k, beat-synchronous tokenizers obtain comparable AUPRC to fixed patching with better stability across runs. These results suggest morphology-preserving beat tokenization is a compact, competitive alternative to fixed temporal patching.

</details>


### [120] [Convergence rates for the RMSprop optimizer with full control of the hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30382)
*Steffen Dereich,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RMSprop优化器在凸随机优化问题中，首次给出了误差常数对超参数（正则化参数ε和第二矩衰减参数β）一致有界的非渐近误差估计，误差被分解为指数衰减的初始化项、阶为γ_n的随机逼近余项和阶为(1-β)²的记忆误差，且对每一步n均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RMSprop、Adam和AdamW等自适应随机梯度下降方法在训练人工智能系统中被广泛使用，但其理论误差估计仍然是一个开放问题——特别是在凸随机优化情形下，已有方法的误差常数并未能对超参数（如ε接近0、β接近1）保持一致有界。这阻碍了对这些实用优化器行为更深层的理论理解。

Method: 作者针对RMSprop优化器，将目标函数的停止求值的期望上界分解为三部分：随时间指数衰减的初始化项、阶为γ_n的随机逼近余项、以及阶为(1-β)²的记忆误差。所有误差常数对步长、β和ε∈[0,1]（包含ε=0）在所有允许选择下一致有界。证明的核心创新在于建立了RMSprop第二矩过程的适当逆矩界（inverse moment bounds）。

Result: 作者获得了对所有梯度步n=1,2,3,...均成立的非渐近误差估计，所有误差常数都是显式给出的。关键结果包括：误差常数对超参数（步长、β、ε）一致可控，且该结果覆盖ε=0的极端情形。

Conclusion: 该工作本质上解决了凸随机优化问题中RMSprop方法的误差估计问题，为后续将类似技术拓展到Adam和AdamW等更复杂的自适应优化方法奠定了基础。逆矩界作为关键的创新工具，为分析自适应方法的收敛性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Popular adaptive stochastic gradient descent (SGD) methods to train artificial intelligence (AI) systems include the RMSprop, the Adam, and the AdamW optimizers, where the adaptivity parts in Adam and AdamW basically just coincide with RMSprop. Such adaptive methods involve several hyperparameters including the regularization parameter $ε$ (which ensures that one does not divide by 0 and is often chosen to be very close to zero such as $10^{-8}$ in PyTorch by default) and the second moment decay parameter $β$ (which is often chosen to be very close to $1$ such as 0.99 (RMSprop) and 0.999 (Adam and AdamW) in PyTorch by default). Despite the high relevance of such methods, it remains an open research problem to provide error estimates for such methods with the error constants being not exploding but uniformly bounded with the respect to the hyperparameters, even in the situation of convex stochastic optimization problems.
  It is the key contribution of this work to essentially solve this problem for RMSprop. Specifically, we bound the expectation of the stopped evaluation of the objective function at the RMSprop process from above by the sum of an initialization term that decays exponentially in the training time, a stochastic approximation remainder of order $γ_n$, and a memory error of order $( 1 - β)^2$ with the error constants being uniformly controlled over all admissible choices of the step sizes, the second moment decay parameter $β$ and the regularization parameter $ε\in[0,1]$ (also covering $ε=0$). Our non-asymptotic error estimates hold not just for all sufficiently large n but hold for every gradient step $n=1,2,3,...$ with all error constants being explicitly specified. The key innovative new feature in the proof of our analysis are suitable inverse moment bounds for the second moment process in RMSprop.

</details>


### [121] [DASC: Decay-Aware State Compression for Hybrid Linear-Attention Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30386)
*Yanqi Yu,Pingwei Sun,Jianchao Tan,Tao Zhang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Yao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Decay-Aware State Compression (DASC)，一种针对混合线性注意力架构（如Gated DeltaNet和Kimi Delta Attention）的持久状态检查点压缩方法，通过从模型权重推导不同head/channel的"保留视野"（retention horizons），仅保留长视野状态单元并以ragged布局打包，在Kimi-Linear上实现2.63倍的KDA循环状态压缩，固定检查点内存下将平均TTFT降低42.6%、输入吞吐提升68.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合线性注意力架构虽然在质量上接近全注意力且大幅减少KV缓存增长，但其就地循环状态更新使缓存管理复杂化：前缀复用需要状态检查点与全注意力KV并存，而存储完整检查点会增加内存压力，导致更多驱逐和重复prefill。因此需要压缩持久状态检查点以缓解内存瓶颈。

Method: DASC包含四个核心步骤：(1) 分析GDN和KDA的衰减结构，发现不同head和channel以显著不同的时间尺度保留前缀信息（即retention horizons）；(2) 从模型权重推导retention horizons，识别长视野状态单元；(3) 将长视野单元打包为ragged状态检查点布局，并跨张量并行（TP）rank平衡压缩后的检查点以适配推理引擎；(4) 在复用时，对省略单元进行零填充，或从有界后缀以额外计算代价刷新状态。

Result: 在Kimi-Linear的检索和端到端推理基准上，保守DASC配置在接近完整缓存质量的同时将KDA循环状态检查点压缩2.63倍；在固定检查点内存预算下，容量增益使平均TTFT降低42.6%，输入吞吐提升68.4%；更大压缩比下后缀刷新可恢复大部分因激进省略而损失的精度；Qwen上的GDN实验同样展现类似的质量-效率趋势，证明DASC可泛化到head-wise（GDN）和channel-wise（KDA）两种架构。

Conclusion: DASC通过利用混合线性注意力架构中固有的保留视野差异，实现了高效且有效的持态检查点压缩，在服务质量几乎不损失的情况下大幅降低了内存压力和首字延迟，且方法可推广到不同注意力类型的混合线性架构。

Abstract: Hybrid linear-attention architectures have recently scaled to large open-weight models, offering quality competitive with full attention while substantially reducing key/value (KV) cache growth. However, their in-place recurrent-state updates complicate cache management: prefix reuse requires state checkpoints alongside full-attention KV, while storing state checkpoints in full increases memory pressure, leading to more evictions and repeated prefill. By analyzing the decay structure of Gated DeltaNet (GDN) and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), we find that different heads and channels retain prefix information over markedly different timescales, which we term \emph{retention horizons}. This variation suggests substantial compression potential in persistent state checkpoints. Building on this observation, we introduce \emph{Decay-Aware State Compression} (DASC), which derives retention horizons from model weights, selects long-horizon state units, and packs them into a ragged state checkpoint layout. To integrate efficiently with tensor-parallel inference engines, DASC furtherly balances compressed state checkpoints across TP ranks. On reuse, DASC either zero-fills omitted units or refreshes them from a bounded suffix with additional compute cost. Across retrieval and end-to-end reasoning benchmarks on Kimi-Linear, conservative DASC configurations remain close to full caching while compressing KDA recurrent state checkpoints by $2.63\times$. Under fixed state checkpoint memory budgets, the resulting capacity gains reduce mean Time to First Token (TTFT) by 42.6\% and improve input throughput by 68.4\%. At larger compression ratio, suffix refresh recovers much of the accuracy lost to more aggressive omission, at the cost of additional replay computation. Qwen with GDN exhibits a similar quality--efficiency trend, showing that DASC extends from channel-wise KDA to head-wise GDN.

</details>


### [122] [Uncertainty of Vision Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30390)
*Haoxu Huang,Narges Razavian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了医学视觉基础模型在点预测和区域预测两种不确定性量化框架下的校准性能，比较了领域特异性与通用领域预训练模型在视网膜、组织病理和胸部X光三个医学影像数据集上的表现，发现高质量领域特定预训练结合自监督学习能带来更好的点预测校准，领域特异性基础模型也能产生更高效的共形预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学等高危领域中，机器学习系统对准确的不确定性估计至关重要。传统基于概率输出的点预测缺乏对预测覆盖率的正式保证，往往需要额外的校准技术；而共形预测提供了具有有限样本有效性保证的替代方案。然而，预训练方式、数据集规模和领域如何影响这两种不确定性量化框架的性能，尚未得到充分研究，特别是在医学视觉基础模型的背景下。

Method: 研究通过对比领域特异性医学视觉基础模型与通用领域视觉基础模型，在视网膜、组织病理和胸部X光三种数据集上进行全面评估。在点预测层面应用多种校准技术（如温度缩放等），在区域预测层面采用共形预测框架，系统性地考察了预训练策略、数据集规模和领域对两种不确定性量化框架的影响。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1)在更高质量的领域特定数据集上预训练并结合自监督学习，比通用领域预训练产生更好的点预测校准；(2)标准的重新校准方法无法完全消除不同数据源训练模型间的不确定性差异；(3)领域特异性基础模型能够产生更高效的共形预测集。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在医学AI系统中谨慎选择模型并整合点预测与区域预测两种范式的重要性，指出对医学视觉基础模型的发展需要采取更全面的不确定性量化方法，以确保稳健且可解释的AI驱动临床决策。

Abstract: Accurate uncertainty estimation is essential for machine learning systems de- ployed in high-stakes domains such as medicine. Traditional approaches primarily rely on probability outputs from trained models (point predictions), which provide no formal guarantees on prediction coverage and often require additional calibra- tion techniques to improve reliability. In contrast, conformal prediction (region prediction) offers a principled alternative by generating prediction sets with finite- sample validity guarantees, ensuring that the ground truth is contained within the set at a specified confidence level. In this study, we explore the impact of pre-training approach, dataset scale and domain on both point and region-level uncertainty quantification, by studying domain-specific vision medical foundation models vs. general domain vision foundation models. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across foundation models trained on retinal, histopathological, and Chest X-Rays data, applying various calibration techniques. Our results demonstrate that (1) pre-training on higher-quality domain-specific datasets along with self-supervised learning leads to better-calibrated point predictions than general domain pre-training, (2) stan- dard re-calibration methods alone cannot fully mitigate uncertainty discrepancies across models trained on different data sources, (3) domain-specific foundation model can lead to more efficient conformal prediction. These findings highlight the importance of careful model selection and the inte- gration of both point and region prediction to enhance the reliability and trust- worthiness of medical AI systems. Our work underscores the need for a holistic approach to uncertainty quantification in recent development of medical vision foundation model, ensuring robust and interpretable AI-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [123] [Foundation Models Meet Agriculture: Challenges Beyond Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30392)
*Vishal Nedungadi,Xingguo Xiong,Marc Rußwurm,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了地球观测基础模型在农业任务中表现不佳的两个关键瓶颈：预训练-部署模态差距（农业任务常需非影像数据模态，而EO基础模型无法处理）和农业任务空间的结构化差异（导致模型排名不稳定），并提供了开发领域感知基础模型的战略路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测基础模型在通用遥感领域表现优异且标注高效，但在农业应用中效果出乎意料地差。作者假设这一性能差距源于农业景观的极端异质性和当前EO基础模型无法适应任务特定细微差别的固有限制，因此需要系统诊断其背后的结构性瓶颈。

Method: 作者系统评估了两个制约基础模型应用于农业任务的关键瓶颈：(1) 在7个真实农业数据集上（涵盖产量预测、物候估计和作物分类），对两个EO基础模型、一个面向表格数据的基础模型及传统监督基线进行全面基准测试；(2) 识别预训练-部署模态差距，即农业下游任务常需要多样化非影像数据模态而EO基础模型架构上无法处理；(3) 沿五个结构轴形式化农业任务空间，以解释模型泛化失败和排名不稳定的原因。

Result: 研究发现：首先，农业任务对非影像数据模态的需求使EO基础模型存在架构性缺陷，而面向表格数据的基础模型能更自然地处理这种异质性；其次，农业任务空间的五轴结构特征导致当前模型无法可靠泛化，模型排名在不同评估设置中高度不稳定。

Conclusion: 通过刻画结构性和模态性差距，本文揭示了通用架构与农业专项下游数据之间的摩擦，为开发下一代领域感知基础模型提供了战略性路线图。

Abstract: Global food security and sustainable climate action increasingly rely on robust, scalable agricultural monitoring. Earth observation foundation models have emerged as powerful, label-efficient tools across general remote sensing domains, yet early attempts to deploy them for agricultural applications have yielded surprisingly poor results. We hypothesize that this performance gap stems from the extreme heterogeneity of agricultural landscapes and the inherent inability of current earth observation foundation models to adapt to task-specific nuances. In this work, we systematically evaluate two critical bottlenecks hindering the deployment of foundation models in agricultural tasks, benchmarking two earth observation foundation models, a foundation model designed for tabular data, and conventional supervised baselines across seven real-world agricultural datasets spanning yield prediction, phenology estimation, and crop classification. First, we identify a pretraining-deployment modality gap: agricultural downstream tasks frequently require diverse, non-imagery data modalities that earth observation foundation models are architecturally unequipped to ingest, while a foundation model built for tabular data handles this heterogeneity more naturally. Second, we formalize the agricultural task space across five structural axes to demonstrate why current models fail to generalize reliably, resulting in highly unstable model rankings across evaluation settings. By characterizing these structural and modal gaps, our insights highlight the friction between general-purpose architectures and specialized agricultural downstream data, providing a strategic roadmap for developing the next generation of domain-aware foundation models.

</details>


### [124] [TopGQ: Fast GNN Post-Training Quantization Leveraging Topology Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30394)
*Dain Kwon,Kanghyun Choi,Hyeyoon Lee,Sunjong Park,Seoyong Lee,Sukjin Kim,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopGQ是一个后训练GNN量化框架，通过双轴尺度吸收和基于TopPIN的节点分组，将量化时间降低一个数量级同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN量化方法存在显著的量化开销问题，严重限制了它们在实际场景中的实用性。

Method: 1) 提出双轴尺度吸收（dual-axis scale absorption），通过将激活量化的尺度合并到邻接矩阵中，实现沿外部和内部两个维度的激活量化；2) 提出TopPIN作为节点局部结构的代理表征，用于将拓扑结构相似的节点在量化过程中进行分组。

Result: 实验结果表明，TopGQ将量化时间减少了约一个数量级，同时保持了量化精度。

Conclusion: TopGQ通过消除冗余量化开销，实现了高效、准确的后训练GNN量化方案，在实用性和精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Existing GNN quantization methods suffer from considerable quantization overhead, which severely limits their practical usage in real-world scenarios. To this end, we present TopGQ, an accurate post-training GNN quantization framework, alleviating redundant quantization overhead. We propose dual-axis scale absorption, which enables activation quantization along both the outer and inner dimensions by merging one into the adjacency matrix. On top of that, we introduce TopPIN, a proxy for nodes' local structure, and use it to group nodes with similar topology during quantization. Experimental results show that TopGQ reduces quantization time by an order of magnitude while preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [Locally-Guided Actor-Critic: Training a Goal-conditioned Actor with a Subgoal-aware Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30406)
*Olivier Serris,Stéphane Doncieux,Olivier Sigaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了长期目标条件强化学习中的两种规划辅助范式（动作正则化与基于势的奖励塑形）的失效案例，并提出LG-AC方法，通过显式地以完整的中间目标序列为条件进行值函数估计，同时将值函数表示为子目标条件值函数之和，实现密集的后见重标注，在具有挑战性的目标链任务上取得了最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件强化学习在稀疏奖励和长时域任务中表现不佳。现有两类利用高层规划器辅助学习的方法各存在缺陷：RIS的动作正则化在低成本中间目标下会产生目标链问题，而PBRS在终态会产生欺骗性奖励。作者希望提出一种既避免这些缺陷、又能保持密集型奖励信号的方法。

Method: 首先提出RS方法，通过移除PBRS在终态的欺骗性奖励来修正其缺陷（但牺牲了PBRS的理论保证）。在此基础上提出LG-AC，该方法显式地以完整的中间目标序列为条件来估计值函数，但将值函数表示为各子目标条件值函数之和，从而支持密集的后见重标注（hindsight relabeling），在不引入动作正则化的情况下为到达中间目标提供奖励。

Result: 在具有挑战性且需要目标链的任务上进行了系统评估，实验表明动作正则化和奖励塑形方法均存在各自的低性能场景，而LG-AC在所有任务上取得了最佳的整体性能。

Conclusion: 论文通过对比分析揭示了现有规划辅助强化学习方法（RIS与PBRS）的失效模式，并验证了所提出的LG-AC方法在目标链任务中的优越性，为长期目标条件强化学习提供了一种更鲁棒的框架。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning struggles with long horizons when rewards are sparse. While a planner can provide subgoals to guide a low-level policy, its use at test time may introduce practical subgoal management difficulties. An alternative paradigm utilizes a high-level planner to assist learning, while the policy remains conditioned only on the final goal, enabling planner-free deployment. Among these methods, Reinforcement Learning with Imagined Subgoals (RIS) introduces a regularization term that encourages the policy to take the same actions for the final goal as it does for an intermediate goal. This regularization, however, may lead to goal-chaining issues when intermediate goals are low-dimensional. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) translates plans into an additional reward while ensuring that the optimal policy remains unchanged. Yet, it can generate deceptive rewards in terminal states. We study these failure cases and first propose an alternative reward shaping method (RS) that removes these deceptive rewards at the expense of theoretical guarantees of PBRS. Similar to this RS variant, we then propose another method named Locally-Guided Actor Critic (LG-AC) that rewards the agent for reaching intermediate goals. Unlike RS, where intermediate rewards are implicit in the shaping signal, we explicitly condition a value estimator on the full sequence of intermediate goals but represent the value function as a sum of subgoal-conditioned value functions, enabling dense hindsight relabeling. We evaluate all these methods in tasks with challenging goal-chaining requirements and empirically highlight specific cases in which either action regularization or reward shaping yield low performance, while LG-AC achieves the best overall performance across tasks.

</details>


### [126] [No Equivariant Architecture Covers All Equivariant Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30417)
*Tīkun Ông*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了等变多头自注意力（MHSA）的结构：若MHSA层关于对称群G等变，则G只能作用于头簇的置换，且QK和OV矩阵需满足与群作用相关的等变约束。进一步证明，任何通过多项式参数化无约束MHSA参数来实现精确等变的固定架构，都会不可避免地导致等变映射类内的表达力损失——等变轨迹由极多Zariski不可约分量组成，而单个架构最多覆盖一个分量。对D4群作用于正则表示的C份拷贝的特征空间，8个注意力头对应Ω(C^64)个分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管等变MHSA在视觉和拓扑任务中已被广泛应用，但现有方法缺乏对"何种等变结构是可能的"以及"等变约束是否损害表达力"的系统性理解，本文旨在给出完整的理论刻画并揭示参数化方法的内在局限性。

Method: 通过代数几何分析，将MHSA的等变条件转化为对QK和OV矩阵的约束条件，并在约化参数空间中研究等变轨迹的几何结构，利用Zariski不可约分解来刻画不同等变架构所能覆盖的表达空间。

Result: 证明了任意固定MHSA架构仅能覆盖等变轨迹中至多一个Zariski不可约分量，而完整等变轨迹包含极多分量（D4群、8个头时至少Ω(C^64)个），从而揭示了表达力损失的本质源于轨迹的复杂不可约分解。

Conclusion: 等变MHSA本质上只能通过头簇置换和特定的QK/OV约束实现，而任何多项式参数化的固定架构都只能覆盖等变映射空间的一小部分，这首次从代数几何角度严格证明了通用等变MHSA参数化的不可行性。

Abstract: We give a complete characterization of equivariant multi-head self-attention (MHSA): if an MHSA layer is equivariant to a symmetry group $G$, then $G$ can only act by permuting head-clusters, with QK and OV matrices satisfying an equivariance constraint tied to the group action. As a consequence, we prove that any fixed MHSA architecture that achieves exact equivariance by polynomially parameterizing unconstrained MHSA parameters inevitably leads to expressivity loss within the class of equivariant maps: the equivariance locus of unconstrained MHSA forms a union of extremely many Zariski-irreducible components in a reduced parameter space, and any single architecture covers at most one. For $G=D_4$ acting on $C$ copies of the regular representation as the token feature space, we show that there are $Ω(C^{64})$ components for eight attention heads.

</details>


### [127] [Confounding Masquerading as Improvement: A Systematic Evaluation of Offline Reinforcement Learning for Stroke Antithrombotic Treatment in a 129,000-Patient Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30442)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对急性缺血性卒中患者的离线强化学习算法进行系统评估，发现标准FQE评估存在"奖励嵌入混淆"效应，导致策略改善信号被严重高估（+0.0069至+0.0101）；经DML启发式GBM奖励残差化去除混淆后，估计值衰减至+0.0033（p=0.132），确认无临床意义的汇总改善，并提供六步评估清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期离线强化学习研究声称其策略在临床结局上优于医生决策，但这类评估可能受奖励设计中的混淆因素影响。作者希望系统性地审视这些"看似改善"的信号是否真实可靠，特别是终端奖励中嵌入的基线严重程度和预后信息是否扭曲了政策改进的估计。

Method: 在44,894例2018年后的急性缺血性卒中患者（来自N=129,033的全国注册数据）中进行部分交叉设计，评估5种离线RL算法族和14种奖励设计。采用标准FQE、增加早期神经功能恶化惩罚变体、2x2因子分析、DML启发的GBM奖励残差化、完整去混淆、T-learner分析、直接复发分析及1年mRS因子分析，并制定六步评估清单。

Result: 标准FQE显示+0.0069的表现改善估计，加入END惩罚后增至+0.0101；但2x2因子分析显示终端奖励混淆解释了观察信号变化的218.6%，去除后过度回调。经奖励残差化后FQE估计衰减至+0.0033（p=0.132），完整去混淆为+0.0025（p=0.291），均不具统计显著性。1年mRS因子分析复现衰减结果；NIHSS分层异质性为前瞻性试验设计提供假设，医院层面差异在完整去混淆后消失。

Conclusion: 离线RL在临床决策评估中的表面改善很大程度源于奖励嵌入混淆而非真实政策优势。研究者提出经验驱动的六步评估检查清单以防范此类假阳性，并建议对NIHSS分层异质性进行前瞻性试验验证。

Abstract: Recent offline reinforcement learning (RL) studies report policies that outperform physician decisions on clinical outcomes. We conduct a systematic, partially crossed evaluation of five offline RL algorithm families and 14 reward designs in 44,894 post-2018 acute ischemic stroke patients from a nationwide registry (N = 129,033).
  Standard Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) yields an apparent policy-improvement estimate of +0.0069; adding an Early Neurological Deterioration penalty increases it to +0.0101. We identify reward-embedded confounding, in which a proxy terminal reward encodes baseline severity and prognosis as well as treatment efficacy. A 2 x 2 factorial analysis finds that terminal reward confounding accounts for 218.6% of the observed signal change, so its removal overshoots the null.
  After DML-inspired GBM reward residualization, the FQE estimate attenuates to +0.0033 (p = 0.132), and full deconfounding yields +0.0025 (p = 0.291). FQE-based diagnostics, T-learner analyses, and direct recurrence analyses converge away from a clinically meaningful aggregate improvement. A 1-year mRS factorial analysis replicates the attenuation. We provide an empirically motivated six-step evaluation checklist. NIHSS-stratified heterogeneity is hypothesis-generating for prospective trial design; hospital-level disagreement does not persist after full reward deconfounding.

</details>


### [128] [Self-Supervised Pretext Tasks for Infant Cry Analysis: A Controlled Comparison and a Cautionary Result on Donateacry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30456)
*Luigi Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在固定预算（相同编码器、相同预训练音频、相同评估协议）下系统比较了六种自监督预训练任务在婴儿哭声分析中的表现。发现重建类目标在哭声检测上表现优异（掩码谱图编码器达到0.988 AUC），但在哭声原因分类上所有编码器均接近随机水平（0.38-0.54宏AUC）。通过复现实验证明donateacry文献中90%以上的准确率来自有泄漏的评估协议（clip-wise切分和数据增强后切分），而非模型真实学习能力。在无泄漏切分下，即使将标注集扩大二十倍，跨主体AUC仍不变，表明该任务的有效样本量取决于婴儿数量而非音频片段数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿哭声分析是自监督学习的一个新应用场景，但缺乏在不同预训练任务之间的公平系统比较；同时donateacry基准上报告的90%+准确率与直觉不符，可能存在评估协议泄漏问题，亟需通过受控实验进行严格验证。

Method: 在固定预算下比较六种自监督前置任务（含掩码谱图和重建类目标），使用统一的1.17M参数紧凑编码器、115小时公开音频预训练和相同的评估协议；分别进行哭声检测（subject-wise切分）和哭声原因分类（donateacry基准）实验；通过只改变切分方式（clip-wise vs subject-wise、增强前后切分）来检验文献结果；并测试数据增强（vocoder说话人扰动+噪声混合）和领域自适应对跨主体性能的影响。

Result: 重建类自监督任务在哭声检测上占优，掩码谱图编码器达到0.988 AUC；所有编码器在哭声原因分类上均接近随机（0.38-0.54宏AUC），且领域自适应和端到端微调均无法改善。复现实验表明：clip-wise切分将准确率提升到85.2%（仅略高于83.8%多数类基线），增强后切分提升到97.9%，与文献报告的SOTA一致。无泄漏条件下增强二十倍标注集，跨主体AUC保持不变。

Conclusion: donateacry基线中的高准确率主要归因于评估协议泄漏（切分方式将同一婴儿数据混入训练和测试集），而非编码器学习到了可泛化的哭声原因表征。哭声原因分类任务的瓶颈在于标签本身（标注噪声或类别定义不清），而非模型容量或预训练策略；该任务的有效样本量由婴儿数量决定。

Abstract: We compare six self-supervised pretext tasks for infant cry analysis under a fixed budget, meaning the same compact encoder of 1.17M parameters, the same 115 hours of license-verified public pretraining audio, and the same evaluation protocol for every candidate. On cry detection the reconstructive objectives dominate, and a linear probe over a masked-spectrogram encoder reaches 0.988 AUC with subject-wise splits even though the encoder never observed a cry during pretraining. On cry-reason classification over donateacry, the de facto public benchmark for cry reasons, every encoder performs at chance (0.38 to 0.54 macro AUC over 5 classes), and neither domain adaptation on 1.8 hours of real cries nor end-to-end fine-tuning moves the result. Since a frozen HuBERT-base with 80 times more parameters shows the same pattern, the bottleneck must sit in the labels and not in model capacity. We then reproduce the 90\%+ accuracies of the donateacry literature on our own system by changing nothing but the evaluation protocol: clip-wise splits raise accuracy to 85.2% (barely above the 83.8% majority-class baseline), and applying augmentation before splitting raises it to 97.9%, matching the reported state of the art, from the same model that measures 0.49 macro AUC under subject-wise splits. Under leakage-free splits, a twentyfold augmentation of the labeled set (vocoder speaker perturbation and noise mixing, 21 hours) leaves cross-subject AUC unchanged: for this task the effective sample size is the number of infants. We release code, seeds and per-clip license manifests.

</details>


### [129] [Learning Where Outcomes Change:Credit-Addressable Reasoning for Multimodal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30457)
*Jiani Guo,Junjie Wang,Jie Wu,Pengxiang Zhao,Dongdong Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Yujiu Yang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"信用可寻址推理"（credit-addressable reasoning）框架，通过将推理过程中的语义单元同时作为学习时比较与分配信用的节点，实现多模态几何推理中精细化的过程监督。具体实例化为Code-CoT（将视觉关系表示为可寻址执行代码）与CE-GRPO（基于结构先验和类型归一化熵选择事件边界，将结果差异转化为局部优势）。在九个几何基准上平均准确率达76.04，分别超越Qwen3-VL-8B和轨迹级GRPO达8.09和3.43个百分点，且优势随中间事件数增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM多模态几何推理中，自由形式的推理轨迹模糊了决定答案的关键步骤，而轨迹级强化学习将单一的终局信号均匀分布到整个响应中，导致信用分配不精确、学习效率低。作者希望让推理过程中暴露的语义单元同时成为学习时比较与分配信用的位置，实现表示与优化的协同设计。

Method: 提出credit-addressable reasoning原则，即推理中暴露的语义单元同时定义学习中的信用分配节点。具体方法包括：(1) Code-CoT：保留原始图表，将视觉关系编码为行可寻址的可执行代码，并将推理组织为类型化事件（typed events）；(2) CE-GRPO：利用结构先验和类型归一化熵选择事件边界，从共享前缀采样完整延续，并将结果差异转换为局部化优势信号，实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 在九个几何基准上，CE-GRPO平均准确率76.04，较Qwen3-VL-8B提升8.09个百分点，较轨迹级GRPO提升3.43个百分点。相对优势随中间事件数量增加而增大，验证了表示-优化协同设计在处理长链依赖多模态推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将推理语义单元与信用分配单元对齐，Code-CoT + CE-GRPO显著提升了多模态几何推理的性能，尤其在需要多步演绎的长推理链中优势愈发明显，证明了representation-optimization co-design的价值。

Abstract: Multimodal geometry reasoning requires VLMs to extract precise visual relations and preserve them through multi-step deduction. Existing free-form traces obscure the decisions that determine the answer, and trajectory-level reinforcement learning distributes a single terminal signal across the entire response. We introduce credit-addressable reasoning, in which the semantic units exposed during inference also define where learning compares alternatives and assigns credit. We instantiate this principle with Code-CoT, which retains the diagram, represents visual relations as line-addressable executable code, and organizes reasoning into typed events, and CE-GRPO, which selects event boundaries using structural priors and type-normalized entropy, samples complete continuations from shared prefixes, and converts outcome differences into localized advantages. Across nine geometry benchmarks, CE-GRPO achieves an average accuracy of 76.04, outperforming Qwen3-VL-8B and trajectory-level GRPO by $8.09$ and 3.43 points, respectively. Its relative advantage increases with the number of intermediate events, demonstrating the value of representation--optimization co-design for long, dependency-heavy multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [130] [ToxLens: A Reproducible Graph-Learning Framework for Leakage-Aware, Uncertainty-Calibrated Molecular Toxicity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30472)
*Magnus H. Strømme,Alex G. C. de Sá,David B. Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ToxLens，一个可复现的多任务图学习框架，用于11种毒性终点预测。该框架包含保守化学整理、sphere-exclusion过滤、泄漏感知的UMAP-HDBSCAN数据划分、并行图编码器与全局特征编码器后拼接融合、温度缩放MC-dropout与共形预测集、适用域分析及SHAP引导的毒效团发现。在泄漏控制测试集上，五种子软投票集成模型MCC为0.44、AUC为0.83、AUPRC为0.58，全面超越四种ECFP4基线；消融实验表明全局通路重要、晚拼接优于门控和FiLM融合；共形预测集显示端点间效率差异显著，且判别力与校准度随训练域相似度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子毒性预测基准的性能评估会因训练集和测试集中出现结构相关分子而高估实际实用性，亟需引入泄漏感知的评估方案和更稳健的预测框架。

Method: ToxLens框架：(1) 保守化学整理与sphere-exclusion过滤；(2) 泄漏感知的UMAP-HDBSCAN数据划分；(3) 并行图编码器和全局特征编码器，经晚拼接融合；(4) 温度缩放的Monte Carlo dropout搭配共形风格预测集；(5) 适用域分析；(6) SHAP引导的毒效团发现并设闭塞对照。

Result: 在泄漏控制测试集上，五种子软投票集成的MCC=0.44，AUC=0.83，AUPRC=0.58；在11个毒性端点上全面超越四种ECFP4浅层基线。消融证实全局通路重要，晚拼接优于门控和FiLM融合。44个子图通过预设反事实标准。在固定发布的Tox21 Challenge和TDA划分上重训练表现具有竞争力但并非全面最优。

Conclusion: ToxLens展示了一套端到端的泄漏感知毒性预测工作流，既提供可靠的结构外推性能评估，又能产出可解释的毒效团结构假设，但跨不同数据划分的泛化能力仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Molecular toxicity prediction is increasingly used to prioritise compounds before experimental testing, but conventional benchmark performance can overstate practical utility when structurally related molecules occur across training and test folds. We introduce ToxLens, a reproducible multi-task graph-learning framework for 11 toxicity endpoints spanning Ames mutagenicity, acute oral toxicity, hERG inhibition, and Tox21 nuclear-receptor and stress-response assays. The workflow combines conservative chemical curation, sphere-exclusion filtering, a leakage-aware UMAP-HDBSCAN split, parallel graph and global-feature encoders joined by late concatenation, temperature-scaled Monte Carlo dropout with conformal-style prediction sets, applicability-domain analysis, and SHAP-guided toxicophore discovery with occlusion controls. On the leakage-controlled test fold, a five-seed soft-voting ensemble achieved a Matthews correlation coefficient score of 0.44, an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve score of 0.83, and an area under the precision-recall curve score of 0.58. It exceeded four ECFP4-based shallow baselines on all 11 endpoints under the same split and validation-based threshold-selection protocol. Controlled ablations showed that the global pathway was important, whereas late concatenation outperformed the tested gated and feature-wise linear modulation fusion variants. Conformal-style prediction sets revealed substantial endpoint-specific variation in set efficiency, and discrimination and calibration improved with similarity to the training domain. Retraining on fixed published Tox21 Challenge and TDA folds produced competitive, but not uniformly state-of-the-art, performance. SHAP-guided occlusion and consensus subgraph mining yielded model-derived structural hypotheses, 44 of which contained at least one occurrence that passed the predefined counterfactual criteria.

</details>


### [131] [Measuring Memory and Generalization as Separable Geometric Channels: The Topo^2 Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30487)
*Zhanbo Zhang,Ming Liu,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Topo²框架，利用持久同调的H1结构将深度网络在噪声标签下的"泛化"与"记忆"两种行为在因果上分离，并给出了可测量、可干预（FM0处方）且遵循法律（定律L1-L3及TLS）的拓扑记忆层理论，将"记忆"从一个模糊的能力概念转化为可测量、可分离、可逆的拓扑层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在噪声标签上训练时会同时发生两种行为：对干净数据的泛化和对翻转标签的记忆。现有工作常将二者笼统地混为一谈，视为对单一容量（capacity）的压力，无法在因果上加以区分、测量或干预。作者旨在构建一个测量框架，使二者可分离、可量化并受定律支配。

Method: 采用表示空间的持久同调（persistent homology）H1结构，将其分解为两条通道：类内流形通道（依赖训练停止点）和跨类通道（单调反映被记忆的翻转样本）。提出FM0干预处方，即从epoch 0起对翻转样本施加零损失，使模型达到各设置下的泛化上限而几乎不记忆任何内容。基于此建立定律集，包括L2（FM0分离处方）、L1（类内通道作为训练位置函数）、L3（环构造恒等式，定义为非定律）以及TLS（记忆-泛化拓扑分层），并给出记忆化成本的定量定律（斜率系数C≈0.38）及其随容量的依赖性与清洁样本特征位移的溯源。最后发布框架边界：九个死胡同的证伪清单和排除六族全局统计量解释的仪器验证部分。

Result: 框架在多个数据集与架构上得到分级证据：L2处方一致成立（9/9）；L1类内通道作为训练位置函数在CIFAR上6/6、CIFAR收敛回退3/3、SVHN 2/3；记忆成本定律的有效斜率系数C约0.38（CIFAR-10 0.3801、SVHN 0.3806、CIFAR-100 0.384、VGG 0.3715），且该系数总体依赖容量并溯源至清洁样本特征位移。FM0方法在每个设置下均能达到泛化上限且几乎零记忆。同时框架给出了可证伪性与仪器验证的边界。

Conclusion: Topo²框架将"记忆"重新定义为一种可测量、可分离、因果可加、可锚定、可逆且可定量计费的拓扑层，从而把深度学习中的记忆化行为从模糊的能力概念转化为精确的科学对象，为理解噪声标签下的泛化与记忆提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep networks trained on noisy labels simultaneously generalize on clean data and memorize flipped labels. These are usually conflated as pressures on one capacity. We present Topo^2, a measurement framework that makes them causally separable, measurable, and law-governed. Persistent-homology H1 structure of the representation space separates into a within-class manifold channel (a function of the training stopping point) and a cross-class channel (a monotone readout of memorized flipped samples). An intervention, the FM0 prescription (zero loss on flipped samples from epoch 0), reaches each setting's generalization ceiling while memorizing essentially nothing. Within the framework we establish a law set with graded evidence: (L2) FM0 separation prescription (9/9); (L1) the within-channel as a training-position function (mid-rise 6/6; convergence-back CIFAR 3/3, SVHN 2/3); (L3) a ring-construction identity (definitional, not a law); and TLS (memory-generalization topological layering): memory is causally additive, anchored (silencing clean collapses the representation), invertible (stripping memory restores near-ceiling generalization), and quantitatively billable (the memorization cost law, effective slope coefficient C ~ 0.38 at the reference capacity: CIFAR-10 0.3801 / SVHN 0.3806 / CIFAR-100 0.384 / VGG 0.3715, capacity-dependent in general and traced to clean-sample feature displacement). We also publish the framework's boundaries: a falsification ledger of nine dead ends, and an instrument-vindication section that excludes six families of global statistics as explanations of the within-channel. The framework turns "memorization" from an ill-defined capacity into a measurable, separable, invertible topological layer.

</details>


### [132] [When the Martingale Never Stops Firing: Anytime-Valid Gating on Real Forecast Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30502)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册的案例研究评估了任意时间有效推理（anytime-valid inference）在真实部署中的有效性。研究发现，基于共形检验鞅（conformal test martingales）的漂移检测器在可交换合成流上表现良好（60次中至多1次误报），但在5个真实时序流上以α=0.05的显著性水平，135次干净流运行全部触发警报，表明其有效性前提（数据可交换性）在实际部署中不成立，且检测器与更新机制的交互会放大瞬态扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统在运行时越来越多地被统计监控器纠正，任意时间有效推理承诺在任何时刻可行动的证据，正从理论走向部署。然而其保证是有条件的：仅当数据流表现可交换时才成立。这个前提在依赖数据和循环内部（监控器修改学习器）恰恰最难满足，而且很少被测量。作者旨在实证检验这一前提在真实场景中的有效性。

Method: 作者设计了一个预注册案例研究：共形检验鞅监控器作为门控（gate），控制在线更新的Kalman适配器是否更新被冻结的时序基础模型，应用于5个预测流。在可交换合成流上测试同一实现以验证校准，再在真实流上评估干净运行下的误报率。同时对比Huber风格门控机制作为替代方案。

Result: 在可交换合成流上，该实现在60次中最多只触发1次。但在真实流上，α=0.05时135次干净流运行全部触发警报。构造本身不能解释触发原因，故障来自部署的分数流本身。反复触发保持门的漂移响应激活，门控滤波器放大了其本应防止的瞬态。而Huber风格门控在无需数据集特定调参的情况下，将孤立尖峰退化减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 任意时间有效方法在提出时若声称适用于依赖数据，应附带零模型校准控制和机制追踪（mechanism traces）。该方法中值得保留的组件（Huber风格门控）并不做有效性声明，却在实用上表现优越。这凸显了理论保证在实际部署中可能失效，需要更严谨的验证实践。

Abstract: Machine learning systems are increasingly corrected while they run, and the decision of when to intervene is increasingly delegated to statistical monitors. Anytime-valid inference promises evidence that can be acted on at any moment, exactly the guarantee this setting needs, and it is moving from theory into deployed monitoring. Conformal test martingales are the change-detection instrument, and Ville's inequality caps their false-alarm probability on exchangeable data. The guarantee is conditional. A deployment inherits it only if the stream it monitors behaves exchangeably. The premise is hardest to satisfy where these monitors are most useful, on dependent data and inside loops where the monitor modifies the learner whose scores it reads. It is also rarely measured. We measure it in a pre-specified case study, where such a monitor gates the online updates of a Kalman adapter correcting frozen time-series foundation models on five forecasting streams. On exchangeable synthetic streams, the same implementation fires in at most 1 of 60 runs. On the real streams, at alpha = 0.05, 135 of 135 clean-stream runs fired. The construction does not explain the firing; the failure comes from the deployed score stream itself. Repeated fires hold the gate's drift response active, and the gated filter amplifies the very transient it was designed to prevent. The component worth keeping makes no validity claim. Huber-style gating of the filter's own updates cuts isolated-spike degradation by an order of magnitude with no dataset specific tuning. Anytime-valid methods proposed for dependent data should therefore be accompanied by null-calibration controls and mechanism traces.

</details>


### [133] [Tensor Methods for Language Models: From Token Representation to Training, Adaptation, Inference, Compression, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30505)
*Matvei Tarasov,Salman Ahmadi-Asl,Andre L. F. de Almeida,Andrzej Cichocki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述论文，系统性地将张量分解和张量网络方法组织为面向大语言模型（LLM）的两大互补视角：七阶段生命周期分类法（覆盖词元化、嵌入、预训练、适配、压缩、推理和可解释性）以及组件视角（覆盖嵌入、注意力、前馈网络）。论文提供统一符号和理论基础、分析Transformer各组件的张量化策略，并提出压缩-实现差距度量ρ_gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM是由高维结构化对象（如词元表示、权重、自适应更新、缓存和激活）构建的，这些对象的多线性结构未被传统的矩阵中心视角充分利用。尽管张量分解和张量网络为这种结构提供了原则性的代数语言，但现有文献往往将它们视为孤立的压缩机制，缺乏系统性的组织与比较。

Method: 通过两个互补视图组织张量方法：(1) 七阶段生命周期分类法，涵盖tokenization、embeddings、pre-training、adaptation、compression、inference和interpretability；(2) 组件视图，涵盖embeddings、attention和feed-forward networks。提供统一符号和理论基础，分析Transformer各组件的张量化策略，逐阶段比较方法，并明确评估协议和模型规模的差异，同时连接相邻效率技术和概率张量网络。

Result: 提出ρ_gap指标，用于量化理论内存缩减与实测系统级加速之间的压缩-实现差距。通过将张量化视为共同的结构性原理，为张量化语言模型提供了结构化入口点，并阐明参数节省何时能合理转化为内存效率、计算效率或可解释性。

Conclusion: 张量方法应被视为超越单纯压缩机制的通用结构原则。通过对生命周期各阶段和Transformer各组件的系统比较，论文明确了张量化技术在不同场景下的适用性及局限性，并指出压缩-实现差距是未来需要重点攻克的开放挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are built from structured high-dimensional objects such as token representations, weights, adaptation updates, caches, and activations, whose multilinear structure is underexploited by the conventional matrix-centric view. Tensor decompositions and tensor networks provide a principled algebraic language for this structure, yet the literature often treats them as isolated compression mechanisms. This survey organizes tensor methods for LLMs through two complementary views: a seven-stage lifecycle taxonomy covering tokenization, embeddings, pre-training, adaptation, compression, inference, and interpretability, and a component view covering embeddings, attention, and feed-forward networks. We provide unified notation and theoretical foundations, analyze tensorization strategies for individual Transformer components, and compare methods at each lifecycle stage while making differences in evaluation protocols and model scales explicit. We further connect tensor methods to neighboring efficiency techniques and probabilistic tensor networks. Finally, we synthesize open challenges and introduce $ρ_{\rm gap}$, a metric for the compression-realization gap between theoretical memory reduction and measured system-level speedup. By treating tensorization as a common structural principle, the survey provides a structured entry point to tensorized language models and clarifies when parameter savings can plausibly translate into memory efficiency, computational efficiency, or interpretability. The GitHub page dedicated to this paper is accessible at \href{https://github.com/ma-tt-a/awesome-tensor-methods-for-llms}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [134] [Trajectory-Initialized Neural Double Q-Routing for Large-Scale Overhead Hoist Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30512)
*Cheng Gu,Qiusheng Zhao,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经双Q路由（Neural Double Q-routing），用于半导体工厂天花板悬挂式运输（OHT）系统的机器人车队路径规划。该方法用共享状态-动作值网络替代传统的按目的地索引的Q表，通过模拟轨迹的return-to-go回归进行离线热启动，再结合Double-Q更新、局部拥塞修正和事件分层结构化回放进行在线精调。在9种匹配的车队规模-到达率设置下，相比表格型Double Q-routing，平均完成任务时间降低0.8%-8.8%，离线初始化可使完成任务数提升最多23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工业机器人车队共享受限物理基础设施，车辆行驶时间受安全间距、交叉口访问、下游阻塞和站点争用影响。静态最短路径路由无法应对时变的交通成本，而表格型Q-routing虽能在线自适应，但每个目的地-节点-动作价值独立学习，限制了稀疏访问路由上下文间的信息共享，且启动阶段对不准确的价值估计敏感。

Method: 提出Neural Double Q-routing框架：(1) 用共享的状态-动作价值网络替换目的地索引表；(2) 通过混合模拟器生成的路线轨迹进行return-to-go回归实现离线热启动（warm start）；(3) 在线阶段采用Double-Q更新、局部拥塞校正和事件分层结构化回放进行精细调优。

Result: 在100、150、200个OHT的9种匹配车队规模-到达率设置下：相对表格型Double Q-routing，平均完成任务时间降低0.8%-8.8%；在6个150和200-OHT设置中取得最低平均完成任务时间（而Dijkstra在3个100-OHT设置中最佳）；完成任务数在9个设置中的8个中与表格型Double Q-routing偏差在1%以内；95百分位完成任务时间在8个设置中下降；在2个匹配启动场景中，离线初始化使完成任务数提升最多23%，尾部完成任务时间降低最多15%。

Conclusion: 神经Double Q-routing通过价值网络共享和信息泛化，在较大车队规模下显著优于表格型方法，且离线热启动能有效缓解启动阶段的脆弱性。然而在小规模（100 OHT）场景中，静态Dijkstra仍具优势，表明神经网络方法在低复杂度和稀疏交通情境下的优势有限。

Abstract: Large-scale industrial robot fleets share constrained physical infrastructure, making vehicle travel times dependent on safety separation, intersection access, downstream blocking, and station contention. We study this problem in overhead hoist transport (OHT) systems, a representative ceiling-mounted material-handling system used in semiconductor fabs. Static shortest-path routing cannot account for these time-varying traffic costs, whereas tabular Q-routing adapts online but learns each destination--node--action value independently, limiting information sharing across sparsely visited routing contexts and making startup behavior sensitive to inaccurate value estimates. We propose Neural Double Q-routing, which replaces destination-indexed tables with a shared state--action value network. The network is warm-started through return-to-go regression on mixed simulator-generated routing trajectories and then refined online using Double-Q updates, local congestion correction, and event-stratified structured replay. Across nine matched fleet-size--arrival-rate settings with 100, 150, and 200 OHTs, the proposed framework reduces mean completion time relative to tabular Double Q-routing by $0.8\%$--$8.8\%$. It achieves the lowest mean completion time among all compared methods in the six 150- and 200-OHT settings, whereas Dijkstra remains best in the three 100-OHT settings. Completed-task counts remain within $1\%$ of tabular Double Q-routing in eight of nine settings, and 95th-percentile completion time decreases in eight settings. In two matched startup scenarios, offline initialization increases the number of completed tasks by up to $23\%$ and reduces tail completion time by up to $15\%$.

</details>


### [135] [PAC: Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30528)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Yanzhao Zheng,Zhentao Zhang,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Jiashun Liu,Xinle Deng,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC（Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum），一种用于LLM多任务RL后训练的在线课程学习方法。PAC结合两种任务级信号——优势推导的可学习性（衡量任务可诱导的策略更新幅度）和近期奖励增益（反映更新是否实际提升任务性能），并利用贝叶斯Thompson Sampling控制器在GRPO训练中动态分配各任务的rollout预算。在多层次推理和多领域推理两种设置下，PAC相比随机采样和基于优势的课程基线，提高了样本效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练管线大多依赖固定或手工设计的任务混合比例，但任务的有用性会随训练推进而变化。已有的在线课程方法通常用更新幅度定义可学习性，忽略了更新是否真正转化为奖励增益，这可能导致rollout预算被浪费在更新幅度大但效果不佳的任务上。

Method: PAC结合两类任务级信号：(1) 优势推导的可学习性——衡量一个任务能诱导的策略更新幅度；(2) 近期奖励增益——反映这些更新是否实际改善了任务性能。采用贝叶斯Thompson Sampling控制器，在GRPO训练过程中动态分配rollouts到不同任务，实现自适应的在线课程。

Result: 在多层次推理和多领域推理两种设置下，PAC相比随机采样和基于优势的课程基线，以更少的rollout步数达到可比的验证分数，并在最终平均分数上取得更高成绩，展现出更好的样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 联合追踪优势信号与实际奖励增益可以构建有效的LLM后训练在线课程，这一结果验证了将更新幅度与性能改善结合作为任务分配依据的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is used to improve the reasoning abilities of LLMs, while training data span heterogeneous tasks. However, most RL post-training pipelines rely on fixed or manually designed task mixtures, even though task usefulness changes as training progresses. Online curriculum methods often define learnability by update magnitude, ignoring whether the update translates into reward gains, which can misallocate rollout budget toward tasks with large but ineffective updates. We propose PAC, a Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for multi-task RL of LLMs that combines two task-level signals: advantage-derived learnability, which measures the magnitude of the policy update a task can induce, and recent reward gains, which show whether those updates have improved task performance. A Bayesian Thompson Sampling controller uses these signals to allocate rollouts across tasks during GRPO training. We evaluate PAC under two settings: a multi-level reasoning setting and a multi-domain reasoning setting. PAC improves sample efficiency and final performance: it reaches comparable validation scores with fewer rollout steps and achieves higher final averages than random sampling and advantage-based curriculum baselines in both settings. These results show that jointly tracking advantage signals and actual reward gains yields an effective online curriculum for LLM post-training.

</details>


### [136] [Q-Strata: Hierarchical Bit Allocation for Mixed-Precision Quantization of Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30564)
*Deokjae Lee,Sihun Chu,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Strata，一种针对Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)大语言模型的双层混合精度量化（MPQ）分配器。其内层用廉价代理为每个专家块在不同预算下缓存Pareto前沿候选集，外层则在装配好的量化模型上通过模型级目标为每个块设置一个预算，从而捕获跨块耦合效应。在Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上，Q-Strata在低位宽场景下相比uniform-bitwidth GPTQ、MxMoE和GEMQ取得了更低的WikiText2困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的混合精度量化面临巨大的分配空间——每个MoE块中的每个专家都包含线性层，分配空间的规模远超稠密模型。现有方法要么在均匀逐块预算下进行块内分配，要么通过加性代理跨块分配，均未直接在耦合各块的模型级目标上进行优化，导致量化质量损失难以最小化。

Method: Q-Strata采用双层（bi-level）分配框架：内层使用廉价代理对每个块内的分配方案进行排序，并在细粒度预算间隔上为每块缓存一个Pareto前沿候选集；外层将搜索空间缩减为每块一个预算（而非为每个线性层分配位宽），在装配好的量化模型上直接优化模型级目标，从而捕获加性代理所遗漏的跨块耦合效应。

Result: 在Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite三个模型上，Q-Strata在低位宽条件下一致地实现了比uniform-bitwidth GPTQ以及现有最优MoE MPQ方法（MxMoE、GEMQ）更低的WikiText2困惑度。

Conclusion: 通过将MoE MPQ问题分解为块内候选生成与块间预算分配的层级结构，Q-Strata有效解决了大规模分配空间和跨块耦合问题，在低位宽场景下显著优于现有方法，证明了模型级目标优化相比加性代理在MoE量化分配中的优越性。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization (MPQ) assigns a different bitwidth to each linear layer of a large language model (LLM) to minimize the quantization-induced quality loss under a fixed budget, but Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models contain these layers in every expert of every MoE block, so the allocation space grows far larger than in a dense model. Existing methods either allocate within each block under a uniform per-block budget, or allocate across blocks through an additive proxy, and neither directly optimizes a model-level objective over the choices that couple the blocks. We propose Q-Strata, a bi-level allocator that ranks within-block assignments with a cheap proxy and allocates across blocks with a model-level objective evaluated on the assembled quantized model. Its inner stage caches a Pareto frontier of candidates per block over finely spaced budgets, leaving the outer stage to set one budget per block instead of a bitwidth for every linear layer. With the search reduced to one budget per block, the outer stage optimizes this model-level objective directly, capturing the inter-block coupling that additive proxies miss. On Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct, Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite, Q-Strata consistently achieves lower WikiText2 perplexity than uniform-bitwidth GPTQ and the state-of-the-art MoE MPQ methods MxMoE and GEMQ in the low-bit regime. The code is available at https://github.com/snu-mllab/Q-Strata/tree/main.

</details>


### [137] [Collapsibility of Performance Metrics in Clinical Predictive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30568)
*João Matos,Ben Van Calster,Richard D. Riley,Paula Dhiman,Gary S. Collins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究考查了预测性AI中15个常用性能指标的可折叠性，发现AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差和Nagelkerke R²五个指标不可折叠，其余十个指标可折叠。AUC的不可折叠性源于其分解为组内和跨组AUC项，整体值可能超出亚组AUC范围，对公平性评估产生误导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群层面的预测性AI性能评估可能掩盖亚组间的性能差异。公平性评估通常依赖亚组性能分析，但某些性能指标是非可折叠的（non-collapsible），即总体性能值不等于亚组特定值的加权平均，这可能导致公平性评估出现偏差。

Method: 研究考察了15个性能指标的可折叠性，两种途径：将每个指标表示为各分层特定值的线性组合（证明可折叠性），或对不可折叠的指标提供受辛普森悖论启发的反例作为形式化反证。

Result: 五个指标（AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差、Nagelkerke R²）被证明不可折叠；十个指标（O:E比值、logloss、Brier分数、准确率、F1分数、真阳性率、真阴性率、阳性预测值、阴性预测值、净收益）被证明可折叠。AUC的不可折叠性因其分解为组内和跨组AUC项，整体AUC可能落在亚组AUC范围之外。

Conclusion: 性能指标的非可折叠性对报告、模型评估和公平性评价有重要影响。它可能产生亚组与整体性能之间的虚假差异，误导公平性评估。明确承认和报告性能指标的可折叠性属性，可提高公平性评估的可解释性和透明度。

Abstract: Background: Population level assessments of predictive artificial intelligence (AI) can conceal performance disparities across subgroups. Fairness evaluations commonly rely on performance analyses across subgroups. However, some performance metrics are non-collapsible, meaning that the overall population performance value does not equal the weighted average of subgroup specific values.
  Objective: To examine the collapsibility properties of commonly reported performance metrics in predictive AI, with a focus on the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC, also known as c-statistic).
  Methods: We investigate the collapsibility of 15 performance metrics, either by expressing each metric as a linear combination of its stratum specific values or, where non-collapsible, by providing a counterexample inspired by Simpson's paradox as a formal disproof.
  Results: Five performance metrics (AUC, calibration intercept, calibration slope, expected calibration error, and Nagelkerke R^2) are shown to be non-collapsible, and ten (O:E ratio, logloss, Brier score, accuracy, F1-score, true positive rate, true negative rate, positive predictive value, negative predictive value, and net benefit) are shown to be collapsible. The AUC is shown to be non-collapsible because it decomposes into within- and cross-group AUC terms when subpopulations coexist, such that its overall value may fall outside the range of subgroup specific AUCs.
  Conclusions: Non-collapsibility of performance metrics has important consequences for reporting, model appraisal, and fairness evaluation. It can generate spurious differences between subgroup and overall performance, which may mislead fairness evaluations. Explicitly acknowledging and reporting the collapsibility properties of performance metrics improves both the interpretability and transparency of fairness assessments.

</details>


### [138] [The Safety Relay in Roleplay Jailbreaks: A Component-Resolved Causal Analysis of Harm Recognition and Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30585)
*Md Mokarram Chowdhury,Ernie Chang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法,系统研究了角色扮演越狱如何逆转大语言模型的拒绝行为。研究发现:成功攻击在请求处保留有害-良性区分,但拒绝关联表达在答案起始处减弱(称为"安全中继衰减");完整的角色扮演构建和场景框架对拒绝逆转有因果关系;这些效应与模型正常拒绝有害请求的内部结构大部分共享,场景框架保留了较小的、模型依赖的成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被训练为遵循指令的同时拒绝有害请求,而越狱攻击利用这一平衡来诱导模型输出本应拒绝的内容。角色扮演越狱尤其令人担忧:有害请求可以隐藏在由角色、场景和任务组成的角色扮演包装中,模型却可能遵从。作者希望通过机制可解释性来理解这一上下文如何逆转拒绝行为,以及哪些元素促成了这种逆转。

Method: 在两个基准、三个模型家族和四个自编包装上,比较配对的有害与良性请求(有/无角色扮演包装)。通过以下方法追踪隐藏状态:从请求到最终提示状态的隐藏状态对比;通过受控反事实隔离包装操作;在保留的评估请求中对激活方向进行干预;对有效方向进行几何分解。

Result: 三个发现:(1)成功攻击在请求处保留有害-良性区分,但拒绝关联表达在答案起始处减弱——安全中继衰减;(2)围绕请求构建完整角色扮演以及场景框架有因果贡献,移除相关激活变化可恢复拒绝;(3)这些效应大部分共享内部结构,大部分修复可由与正常拒绝对齐的组件复现,场景框架保留较小的、模型依赖的成分。

Conclusion: 研究解释了角色扮演如何在保留有害证据的情况下产生遵从行为,并确定了未来防护的具体目标:维持从危害识别到拒绝的连接。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to follow instructions while refusing harmful requests. Jailbreaks exploit this balance to elicit content a model would ordinarily reject. Roleplay jailbreaks are especially concerning: the harmful request can remain visible inside a roleplay wrapper made of a persona, scenario, and task, yet the model may comply. We use mechanistic interpretability to determine how this context reverses refusal and which elements contribute to the reversal. Across two benchmarks, three model families, and four authored wrappers, we compare matched harmful and benign requests with and without this wrapper. We trace hidden-state contrasts from the request to the final prompt state, isolate wrapper operations through controlled counterfactuals, intervene on their activation directions in held-out evaluation requests, and decompose effective directions geometrically.
  Our analysis yields three findings. (1) Successful attacks retain the measured harmful-versus-benign distinction at the request, while its refusal-associated expression weakens where the answer begins, a pattern we call safety-relay attenuation. (2) Constructing the complete roleplay around the request and framing it within the scenario contribute causally: removing the associated activation changes restores refusal. (3) These effects largely share internal structure, and most repair is reproduced by components aligned with the model's ordinary refusal of harmful requests without roleplay; scenario framing retains a smaller, model-dependent component. Together, these findings explain how roleplay can produce compliance despite retained evidence of harm and identify a concrete target for future safeguards: maintaining the connection from harm recognition to refusal.

</details>


### [139] [State of Health Estimation using Convolutional and Bidirectional LSTM Neural Networks tuned by Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30593)
*Panagiotis Eleftheriadis,Foivos Georgios Kyrgios,Sonia Leva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合CNN与BiLSTM的混合深度学习框架，并集成贝叶斯优化的超参数调优算法，用于电池健康状态（SOH）估计。通过引入中间全连接层并进行多特征组合（容量、电压、ICA、DVA）的系统评估，在三个公开数据集上验证了方法的有效性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池SOH估计是电池管理系统中的关键任务，现有深度学习方法在多特征融合和网络结构设计方面存在不足，需要一种更高效、更准确的SOH估计框架。

Method: 采用CNN与BiLSTM的混合架构，集成贝叶斯优化进行超参数调优；评估三种深度学习架构（独立循环模型、CNN-RNN架构、带中间FC层的CNN-RNN组合）；通过结合容量、电压、ICA和DVA的多种特征组合进行系统性评估，确定最优输入表示。

Result: 带中间FC层的CNN-RNN组合模型在三类架构中表现出最高的预测精度；该方法通过MAE、RMSE和FLOPs三个指标进行评估，并在三个公开数据集上验证（两个外部来源，一个作者自建）。

Conclusion: 提出的混合深度学习框架结合贝叶斯优化和系统性的特征工程，能够有效提升电池SOH估计的精度，且通过多数据集验证确保了方法的泛化性和可复现性。

Abstract: In this research, a novel framework is proposed for the SOH estimation, which employs a hybrid deep learning architecture of a concatenation of a Convolution Neural Network (CNN) and a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) Neural Network (NN) with the integration of Bayesian Optimization-based hyperparameter tuning for the network. Three different deep learning architectures are being evaluated: standalone recurrent models, CNN-RNN architectures and CNN-RNN combinations enhanced with intermediate Fully Connected (FC) layers. Among the three, the model with the intermediate FC layers demonstrated the highest predictive accuracy. A comprehensive feature engineering approach combines capacity (Q), voltage (V), Incremental Capacity Analysis (ICA), and Differential Voltage Analysis (DVA), with systematic evaluation of multiple combinations to identify the optimal input representation. To validate the proposed method, three publicly available datasets were utilized, ensuring reproducibility of the results, two from external sources and one developed by the author of this study using a unique experimental setup. The comparison study was performed using the Mean Absolute Error (MAE), the Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) and the FLoating-point OPerations (FLOPs) as evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [140] [PLC-DPO: Posterior Label Correction in Noisy and Ambiguous Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30597)
*Boryeong Cho,Sumyeong Ahn,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLC-DPO提出了一种后验标签校正方法，通过将每对偏好数据分类为"干净"、"翻转"或"平局"三种情况来路由训练信号，利用校准的策略-参考模型边际作为在线证据，自动纠正监督方向和强度，在57个数据集-模型-基准组合中取得了最优平均胜率（60.5 vs 55.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化（DPO）假设所有观测到的偏好都是可靠的，但真实数据中常存在反转、弱或模糊的标签，导致有害的策略更新。现有方法大多只是过滤可疑样本，缺乏主动纠正监督方向和强度的机制。

Method: 提出PLC-DPO框架，核心是将每个偏好对的路由决策（干净/翻转/平局）建立在校准的策略-参考模型边际这一在线证据之上。通过边际信号判断偏好标签的可靠性，分别采取保留原信号、翻转信号或降级为平局处理的校正动作，从而重构训练信号的监督方向与强度。

Result: 在57个数据集-模型-基准组合中，PLC-DPO获得最佳平均胜率60.5%，超过次优方法的55.5%；在注入噪声、平局压力测试、人类分歧分析及自我确认诊断中均表现稳定，能有效区分翻转对与弱方向对。

Conclusion: 将噪声偏好学习从被动过滤重新定义为主动校正监督方向与强度的任务，PLC-DPO通过稳健的路由机制显著提升了DPO在真实噪声数据下的对齐性能，且路由决策具有可解释性和稳定性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) simplifies alignment through pairwise comparisons but assumes all observed preferences are reliable. Real data often violates this assumption, leading to reversed, weak, or ambiguous labels that cause harmful policy updates. To address this, we propose Posterior Label Correction DPO (PLC-DPO) to robustly optimize preferences by routing each pair's training signal as a clean, flip, or tie case. The key idea is to use the calibrated policy-reference margin as online evidence to take appropriate correction actions. This reframes noisy preference learning as actively correcting supervision direction and strength rather than merely filtering suspicious examples. Across 57 dataset-model-benchmark cells, PLC-DPO obtains the best mean win rate against DPO (60.5 vs. 55.5 for the next-best method). Injected-noise and tie stress tests, human disagreement analysis, and self-confirmation diagnostics further show that the routing remains stable and distinguishes flipped from weakly directional pairs.

</details>


### [141] [MolLedger: An Additive Graph Neural Network with Chemically Grounded ADME Attributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30636)
*Christina X. Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MolLedger，一种具有内置原子级可解释性的图神经网络架构，用于ADME性质预测。该模型通过将预测结果表示为每个原子得分之和实现精确可解释性，且不牺牲预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在药物发现中，ADME性质优化至关重要，机器学习模型常用于预测这些性质，但模型预测的可解释性是一个挑战。现有方法难以在保持性能的同时提供对化学性质忠实可靠的解释。

Method: MolLedger是一种图神经网络架构，其预测输出是逐原子分数的加和。通过全局上下文向量为加性头提供足够上下文信息，使加性框架在不损失性能的前提下获得精确的可解释性；同时引入辅助损失将原子分数锚定到化学性质，使归因结果更具化学保真度。

Result: 实验表明MolLedger在保持与现有方法相当性能的同时，能够产生对预测性质变化更合理、更符合化学含义的解释。分子对案例研究证明其归因结果显著优于其他可解释性方法。

Conclusion: MolLedger通过架构设计实现了内在的可解释性，在ADME预测中既能保持高性能，又能提供忠实于化学性质的原子级归因，是药物发现中可解释AI的有力工具。

Abstract: Optimizing absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion (ADME) is an important part of small molecule drug discovery. Many machine learning models have been built to predict ADME properties to facilitate this optimization process, but explaining model predictions is challenging. We propose a new graph neural network architecture with built-in meaningful per-atom attributions. Our model MolLedger outputs predictions that are the sum of per-atom scores. MolLedger's additive framework obtains exact interpretability at no cost to performance because the global context vector gives the additive head enough context to produce good per-atom scores. Furthermore, MolLedger produces attributions that are more faithful to chemical properties than other interpretability methods because the auxiliary loss in MolLedger anchors the atom scores to chemical properties. Our case studies comparing interpretations from multiple methods on molecular pairs reveal that MolLedger is much better at producing sensible explanations for predicted property changes.

</details>


### [142] [Three Steps at a Time: Learning Representations from Action Sequences in Contrastive RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30640)
*Michal Korniak,Kamil Dybek,Benjamin Eysenbach,Marco Bagatella,Michał Bortkiewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将对比强化学习（CRL）从单步动作扩展到动作块（action chunks），在18个离线环境和11个在线环境中分别获得+31.7%和+93.1%的大幅性能提升。研究发现，动作块带来增益的主要原因并非传统的非马尔可夫策略建模或多步回报传播，而是动作块比单步动作携带更多关于目标的信息，从而显著改善评论家的表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督强化学习方法在状态和动作表征学习上取得了显著成果，但一个关键未解决的问题是动作应在何种时间尺度上进行建模。标准方法仅建模单步动作，可能忽略了更长时域中动作之间的相关性和其对目标达成的信息量。

Method: 将对比强化学习（CRL）从单步动作扩展为在动作块（action chunks）上操作，在在线和离线基准上评估该扩展的性能；并通过实证分析探究动作块带来增益的机制，逐一检验已有的两种解释（非马尔可夫时序扩展策略、无偏多步回报传播），最终揭示其核心机制在于动作块携带更多关于目标的信息，改善了评论家的表征。

Result: 在18个离线环境和11个在线环境中分别获得+31.7%和+93.1%的性能提升。实证研究发现，关于动作块增益的传统解释（刻画非马尔可夫扩展策略、传播无偏多步回报）在CRL中仅部分适用；真正的核心原因是动作块比单步动作包含更多关于目标的信息，可度量地提升了评论家的表征质量。

Conclusion: 动作块化是对比强化学习的一种有效且普适的扩展方向，其增益机制在CRL上下文中与传统观点不同，根本在于动作块增强了评论家对目标信息的表征能力，从而显著提升了算法的学习效率和性能。

Abstract: While self-supervised approaches to reinforcement learning have achieved strong results by learning representations of states and actions, a key open question is the time scale over which actions should be modeled. Departing from the standard formulation relying on single-step actions, we extend contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL), a prototypical self-supervised method, to operate over action chunks, and find that this results in large, pervasive gains across established offline and online benchmarks: +31.7% and +93.1% across 18 and 11 environments respectively. While action-chunking-driven gains are generally explained through the ability to model non-Markovian, temporally extended policies, and to propagate unbiased multi-step returns, interestingly, we find that these arguments only partially apply to CRL. Our empirical studies suggest that, in the context of CRL, an action chunk carries more information about the goal than a single action, measurably improving the critic's representations, and rendering the algorithm significantly more effective.

</details>


### [143] [Season-Aware Hybrid Convolutional-Transformer for Antarctic Sea Ice Concentration Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30654)
*Danyang Li,John Taylor,Thang Bui,Quanling Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合卷积-Transformer框架用于月度南极海冰密集度（SIC）预测，结合卷积编码提取空间特征、因子化自注意力建模时空依赖，并引入两种季节性先验机制（月份感知位置编码和季节性时间偏置）来注入周期信息，在分类和回归指标上均优于卷积和循环基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极海冰密集度预测面临复杂空间结构、长程时间依赖和强季节性变化的多重挑战。传统基于卷积的模型擅长捕捉局部空间模式，但难以建模长期时间演化，因此需要更强大的时空建模框架。

Method: 该方法构建混合卷积-Transformer框架：1）卷积编码器提取局部空间特征；2）因子化自注意力（factorised self-attention）建模时空依赖关系；3）月份感知位置编码（month-aware positional encoding）将日历月份信息注入token表示；4）季节性时间偏置（seasonal temporal bias）促使注意力关注周期性相关的历史状态。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在分类和回归指标上均优于卷积和循环基线模型；消融实验进一步验证了两种季节性先验机制在短时和长时预测中均能带来稳定且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结合卷积结构、注意力机制和周期性先验信息，能够有效提升南极海冰密集度预测的准确性和鲁棒性，为极地海洋预报任务提供了有价值的建模思路。

Abstract: Antarctic sea ice concentration (SIC) forecasting is an important yet challenging task due to the coexistence of complex spatial structure, long-range temporal dependencies, and strong seasonal variability. Conventional convolution-based models are effective at capturing local spatial patterns, but often have limited ability to model long-term temporal evolution. To address these challenges, we build on a hybrid Convolutional-Transformer forecasting framework for monthly Antarctic SIC forecasting. This framework combines convolutional encoding for spatial feature extraction with factorised self-attention for spatio-temporal dependency modelling. We further introduce two seasonal prior mechanisms: a month-aware positional encoding that injects calendar-month information into the token representation, and a seasonal temporal bias that encourages attention to periodically related historical states. Experimental results show that the proposed framework achieves better performance than convolutional and recurrent baselines across both classification and regression metrics. Ablation studies further indicate that the seasonal prior mechanisms provide consistent additional gains in both short- and long-horizon prediction. These results demonstrate the value of combining convolutional structures, attention mechanisms, and periodic prior information for Antarctic SIC forecasting.

</details>


### [144] [CoMPASS: Collaborative Molecular Property Prediction via Adaptive Small-Large Model Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30674)
*Wentao Li,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Leyi Zhao,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMPASS是一个检索校准的小-大模型协作框架，以图注意力网络（GAT）作为预测锚点，通过检索局部相关训练分子、向LLM提供注意力基础的证据，并通过共识感知门控将其建议转换为有界修正，从而在可纠正的不确定性区域提升预测精度，同时在高置信区域限制LLM干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测需要在统计可靠性和化学推理之间取得平衡。GNN在标注数据上可直接校准但受训练数据覆盖范围限制；LLM能比较分子证据并阐述化学推理，但作为独立定量预测器不可靠。核心挑战是确定LLM何时应以多大程度影响校准模型。

Method: CoMPASS框架包含四个关键组件：（1）以GAT作为预测锚点；（2）检索局部相关的训练分子；（3）向LLM提供注意力基础的证据（attention-grounded evidence）；（4）通过agreement-aware gate将LLM建议转换为有界修正（bounded correction）。

Result: 在六个分类基准和两个回归基准上，CoMPASS在可纠正的不确定性区域显著提升了GAT锚点的性能，同时在高置信区域限制了LLM干预。消融实验表明性能提升来源于验证校准的检索和有界融合，而非单纯依赖于提示工程。

Conclusion: 生成式推理应通过基于证据的、受控的修正来增强校准预测，而非直接替换模型输出。这表明小-大模型协作的正确范式是"锚定预测+受控修正"，而非"端到端替代"。

Abstract: Accurate molecular property prediction requires both statistical reliability and chemical reasoning. Graph neural networks can be calibrated directly on labeled assays but remain limited by the coverage of their training data. Large language models (LLMs) can compare molecular evidence and articulate chemical rationales, yet are unreliable as standalone quantitative predictors. The central challenge is therefore to determine when an LLM should influence a calibrated model and by how much. Here we present CoMPASS, a retrieval-calibrated framework for small-large model collaboration. CoMPASS retains a graph attention network (GAT) as the predictive anchor, retrieves locally relevant training molecules, provides attention-grounded evidence to an LLM, and converts its proposal into a bounded correction through an agreement-aware gate. Across six classification and two regression benchmarks, CoMPASS improves the GAT anchor in regions of correctable uncertainty while limiting LLM intervention in high-confidence regimes. Ablations show that the gains arise from validation-calibrated retrieval and bounded fusion rather than prompting alone. These results suggest that generative reasoning should augment calibrated prediction through evidence-grounded, controlled corrections rather than direct output replacement. Code is available at https://github.com/littlepeachs/CoMPASS.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Materials Properties from Scarce Labels and Unlabeled Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30682)
*Wentao Li,Yizhe Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Leyi Zhao,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SemiMat，一个用于半监督材料性质回归的受控基准，以及MatRank，一种基于可靠性加权的连续伪标签不确定性目标函数。SemiMat统一了标签/未标签晶体输入、图骨干接口、验证集检查点选择、保留测试报告、归一化MAE（NMAE）和跨六个稀缺标签任务、四个图骨干和五个预定义分割的方法排名摘要。在24个骨干-任务组合中，单一固定的MatRank目标取得了最低的总体保留测试NMAE（0.896）和最佳平均方法排名（2.208），并在组件、OOD和生成池诊断中证明了其可靠性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学中，从稀缺标签和未标记晶体中学习材料属性是实现数据驱动材料发现的核心挑战。现有的半监督方法缺乏标准化的评估协议，导致结果难以比较和复现，因此需要构建一个受控基准来公平评估半监督材料属性回归方法。

Method: 论文提出两大部分：(1) SemiMat基准框架，统一了标签/未标签晶体输入格式、图骨干接口、仅使用验证集的检查点选择、保留测试集报告、NMAE指标以及跨任务/骨干/分割的方法排名汇总协议；(2) MatRank目标函数，通过从标签锚点构建伪目标，依据局部可靠性和弱预测一致性加权，一致地训练弱/强图视图，并引入排序信号，使未标记晶体同时塑造属性值和候选排序。

Result: 在保留的24个骨干-任务组合中，单一固定MatRank目标获得最低的总体保留测试NMAE（0.896）和最佳平均方法排名（2.208）。组件消融、分布外和生成池诊断结果识别了增益可靠的具体场景，同时也揭示了仍需进一步筛选评估的领域。

Conclusion: SemiMat提供了标准化且可复现的半监督材料属性回归评估框架，MatRank展示了在低标签场景下通过可靠性加权伪标签和排序信号提升回归性能的有效性。该工作为材料发现中的半监督学习设立了基准，并明确了其可靠性增益的适用边界。

Abstract: Learning materials properties from scarce labels and unlabeled crystals is a central challenge for data-driven materials discovery. We present SemiMat, a controlled benchmark for semi-supervised materials property regression, and MatRank, a reliability-weighted objective for continuous pseudo-label uncertainty. SemiMat fixes labeled and unlabeled crystal inputs, graph-backbone interfaces, validation-only checkpoint selection, held-out test reporting, normalized MAE (NMAE), and method-rank summaries across six scarce-label tasks, four graph backbones, and five predefined split runs. MatRank builds pseudo-targets from labeled anchors, weights them by local reliability and weak-prediction agreement, trains weak and strong graph views consistently, and adds ranking signals so that unlabeled crystals shape both values and candidate order. Across the retained 24 backbone-task blocks, one fixed MatRank objective gives the lowest aggregate held-out test NMAE (0.896) and best average method rank (2.208). The component, OOD, and generated-pool diagnostics identify where the gain is reliable and where further screening evaluation remains necessary. Code is available at https://github.com/littlepeachs/SemiMat.

</details>


### [146] [Liquid Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30695)
*Yiheng Jiang,Yuanbo Xu,Yongjian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Liquid Gated Attention (LGA)，一种无求解器的并行时序算子，通过将观测时间间隔参数化为输入驱动的门控机制，在隐藏状态演化中引入连续时间归纳偏置，并以快速权重联想记忆的形式实现隐状态演化，支持时间维度上的并行计算。基于LGA构建的LFormer模型在六个任务、十六个数据集（最长17,984步）上展示了长程依赖建模、细粒度状态跟踪和稀疏噪声观测下的轨迹重建能力，以线性扩展效率达到与最先进的离散时间和连续时间基线相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列常呈现出不规则采样和长时间跨度的特点，需要模型在任意间隔上捕捉连续时间动态，且不能带来过高的扩展成本。现有方法存在明显不足：离散时间方法将可变时间间隔坍缩为静态位置步长，丢失时序信息；依赖求解器的连续时间模型保留时序结构但依赖顺序积分，无法并行化；无求解器的近似方法虽避免了积分成本，但没有任何一种方法将观测时间间隔与输入驱动的状态调制相结合。

Method: LGA的核心创新在于：(1) 用观测时间间隔参数化输入驱动的门控机制，从而引入连续时间的归纳偏置；(2) 将隐藏状态演化形式化为快速权重联想记忆，借助矩阵结合律实现非因果编码中的并行计算，并通过前缀扫描（prefix scan）实现因果编码中的并行计算，两种模式下均达到序列长度的线性时间复杂度；(3) 引入序列级归一化来约束累积时间衰减，确保长时程优化的稳定性。在此基础上构建LFormer作为连续时间表示学习的模块化骨干架构。

Result: 在六个任务、十六个数据集上（最长序列达17,984步）的实验表明，LFormer具备长程依赖建模、细粒度状态跟踪以及从稀疏和噪声观测中重建轨迹的能力，在性能上与现有的最先进离散时间和连续时间基线相当，同时保持线性扩展效率。

Conclusion: LGA通过无求解器的并行设计，成功将连续时间动态建模与并行计算效率统一起来，填补了现有连续时间模型在可扩展性上的空白。LFormer验证了这一设计在实际任务中的有效性和通用性，为大规模长时程连续时间序列建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world time series often exhibit irregular sampling and extended temporal horizons, requiring models to capture continuous-time dynamics across arbitrary intervals without prohibitive scaling costs. Discrete-time methods collapse variable time intervals into static positional steps; solver-dependent continuous-time models preserve temporal structure but rely on sequential integration, precluding parallelization; and solver-free approximations avoid this cost yet none couples observed time intervals with input-driven state modulation. We propose Liquid Gated Attention (LGA), a solver-free parallel temporal operator. By parameterizing an input-driven gating mechanism with observed time intervals, LGA introduces a continuous-time inductive bias and formulates hidden state evolution as a fast-weight associative memory, enabling parallel computation across the temporal dimension. Using matrix associativity in non-causal encoding and a prefix scan in causal encoding, LGA attains linear temporal complexity in sequence length in both modes. A sequence-level normalization bounds cumulative temporal decay for stable long-horizon optimization. Building on LGA, we instantiate LFormer, a modular backbone for continuous-time representation learning. Across six tasks and sixteen datasets spanning up to 17,984 steps, LFormer demonstrates long-range dependency modeling, fine-grained state tracking, and trajectory reconstruction from sparse and noisy observations, while delivering competitive performance against state-of-the-art discrete-time and continuous-time baselines with linear scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [147] [Learning Dynamics of Logits Debiasing for Long-Tailed Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30699)
*Yue Cheng,Jiajun Zhang,Xiaohui Gao,Weiwei Xing,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从学习动力学视角重新审视长尾半监督学习（LTSSL），对logits去偏机制进行了理论刻画，并提出基于梯度动态重分配的原则性动态剪枝框架DyTrim。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾分布在半监督学习中普遍存在，伪标签偏向多数类导致泛化性能下降。尽管已有大量LTSSL方法，但它们隐式地使logits去偏的机制尚不清晰，缺乏理论理解。

Method: 推导了logits更新的逐步分解，证明预测被反映标签先验的类不平衡偏差所主导；利用任务无关基线图像的logits作为累积偏差指标，证明其收敛到类先验。基于此统一视角，提出DyTrim框架：在标注数据上进行类别感知剪枝，在未标注数据上进行基于置信度的软剪枝，从而重新分配梯度预算。

Result: 提供了DyTrim降低类偏差并改善泛化的理论保证；在标准LTSSL基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同架构和方法上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: DyTrim通过原则性的梯度预算重分配有效降低类不平衡偏差，为LTSSL方法的logits去偏机制提供了统一的理论理解，并在实验中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Long-tailed distributions are prevalent in real-world semi-supervised learning (SSL), where pseudo-labels tend to favor majority classes, leading to degraded generalization.
