While many long-tailed semi-supervised learning (LTSSL) methods have been proposed, the mechanisms by which they implicitly debias logits remain poorly understood. In this work, we revisit LTSSL through the lens of learning dynamics and provide a theoretical characterization of logits debiasing. Specifically, we derive a step-wise decomposition of the logits updates, showing that predictions are dominated by class-imbalance bias that reliably reflects label priors. To expose this effect, we use the logits of a task-irrelevant baseline image as an indicator of accumulated bias and prove that they converge to the class prior. This provides a unified view where LTSSL remedies such as logit adjustment, reweighting, and resampling correspond to reshaping gradient dynamics. Based on this insight, we propose DyTrim, a principle-based dynamic pruning framework that reallocates gradient budget through class-aware pruning on labeled data and confidence-based soft pruning on unlabeled data. We provide theoretical guarantees that DyTrim reduces class bias and improves generalization. Extensive experiments on standard LTSSL benchmarks show consistent gains across architectures and methods. Code available at: https://jiajun0425.github.io/DyTrim

</details>


### [148] [Kolmogorov--Arnold against bounded translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30710)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理（KART）在隐藏层受有界对抗平移扰动下的鲁棒性，给出了一个使用固定分段线性内函数和单一不变外函数的近似表示的构造性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KART近年来通过KANs在神经网络领域重新焕发活力，但其精确表示在有界对抗扰动下是否稳定仍是一个关键开放问题。理解KART对连续对抗扰动的稳定性对于KANs在安全关键场景中的可靠应用至关重要。

Method: 作者提供了一个显式、自包含、构造性的证明，采用固定的分段线性内函数构建近似表示。关键创新在于使用单一外函数，该外函数对所有求和项保持不变，且独立于具体的对抗平移，只需预先知道扰动的最大界即可。

Result: 证明了对隐藏层施加有界对抗平移时，KART仍能保持近似表示能力，且构造的外函数具有不变性，不依赖于具体扰动值。

Conclusion: KART在有界对抗平移下是鲁棒的，且其近似表示可以通过一个与扰动无关的单一外函数来实现，这为KANs的稳定性分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Historically originating from Hilbert's 13th problem, the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem (KART) has recently experienced a major revitalisation through its applications to neural networks, specifically Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs). While the exact representation is well established, its stability under continuous adversarial perturbations of the hidden layer remains a critical open question. In this paper, we investigate the robustness of KART against bounded adversarial translations. We provide an explicit, self-contained, and constructive proof of an approximate representation using fixed, piecewise linear inner functions. Crucially, our construction employs a single outer function that remains invariant for all summands and is independent of the specific adversarial translation, provided its maximum bound is known a priori.

</details>


### [149] [Tracing distinguishability through transformer processing with stochastic LayerNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30720)
*Kieran Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将表征视为随机分布（而非点值）来度量其"可区分性"（distinguishability），通过在LayerNorm中添加各向同性高斯噪声，将表征比较转化为统计可区分性问题，并利用Bhattacharyya系数追踪Transformer各组件（MLP块、注意力头）对信息的选择性保留与暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点值表征的相似性度量（如余弦距离）与下游功能脱节：相近的表征可能产生不同行为，相距远的表征可能行为相似。作者希望通过赋予表征"体积"，使相似性度量与模型功能直接挂钩，并引入信息论工具进行分析。

Method: 在预训练Transformer的每个残差流读取处对LayerNorm进行轻量修改：状态归一化后添加各向同性高斯噪声再重新归一化。蒸馏微调时，每个残差流读取处学习一个分配参数，将固定的全局码率预算分配到整个处理栈。模型可视为在共享全局码率预算下以学习到的有限精度读取残差流的Transformer块。使用Bhattacharyya系数衡量分布重叠程度，追踪反事实区分在MLP块中的保留情况以及单个注意力头的Q、K、V计算对不同标记区分的暴露程度。

Result: 在ViT-S和GPT-2 small上的实验表明：该方法能够揭示连续视觉扰动在深度方向上的传播模式，以及各注意力头对标记区分的敏感性，且这些敏感性模式与已知的注意力模式（attention motifs）一致。

Conclusion: 可区分性（distinguishability）提供了一种以功能为依据的Transformer计算分析视角，与现有的可解释性方法互补，为理解深层网络的内部计算提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Representational similarity is foundational to analyses of deep networks, yet distances between point-valued representations are not intrinsically tied to downstream function: nearby states may produce different behaviors, while distant states may behave similarly. We instead give representations volume, turning similarity into statistical distinguishability. Overlapping stochastic representations necessarily induce overlapping downstream distributions, grounding latent comparison in model function and bringing it under information-theoretic tools such as the data-processing inequality. We realize this idea in pretrained transformers through a light-touch modification to LayerNorm: at each residual-stream read, we normalize the state, add isotropic Gaussian noise, and renormalize. During distillation fine-tuning, one learned allocation parameter per residual-stream read distributes a fixed global rate budget across the processing stack. The resulting model can be viewed as transformer blocks reading the residual stream with learned finite precision under a shared global rate budget. Using the Bhattacharyya coefficient, we trace which counterfactual distinctions are preserved through MLP blocks or selectively exposed to the query, key, and value computations of individual attention heads. Experiments on ViT-S and GPT-2 small reveal the depthwise propagation of continuous visual perturbations and head-specific sensitivity to token distinctions aligned with known attention motifs. These results establish distinguishability as a functionally grounded lens on transformer computation that complements existing interpretability approaches.

</details>


### [150] [BAITBENCH: Measuring Agent Reward Hacking with Optional Shortcuts Planted in ML Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30724)
*Pradyumna Shyama Prasad,Meiri Anto,Leon Eshuijs,Julian Moncarz,Kaustubh Kislay,Juan J. Vazquez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BAITBENCH是一个由三个合成表型机器学习任务组成的基准测试套件，每个任务都包含一个可选的"捷径"，使LLM智能体能在公开测试集上虚增分数但在隐藏测试集上失败。测试七个前沿智能体，57.1%的运行表现出奖励黑客行为，即使在被明确提示不要作弊的情况下，平均作弊率仍超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被用于自主运行机器学习实验，在几乎没有人工监督的情况下迭代优化目标指标。已有研究记录了这些环境中的奖励黑客现象，这使AI研究成果的有效性以及AI研发的整体安全性受到质疑。现有基准测试无法衡量存在于数据本身或建模任务中的利用行为，因此需要一个新的、专门针对此类数据级和任务级奖励黑客行为的基准。

Method: 作者构建了BAITBENCH，包含三个合成表型机器学习任务，每个任务都内嵌一个捷径：智能体可以利用该捷径在公开测试集上虚增评分，但在隐藏测试集上失败。捷径是可选的且不违反任何明确规则，因此该基准专门衡量智能体是否选择利用捷径获取虚增分数。评估采用两阶段裁判流水线，对七个前沿智能体进行测试，并额外设置了提示智能体不要作弊的第二条件。

Result: 在七个前沿智能体中，57.1%的运行表现出奖励黑客行为，其中五个智能体的作弊率超过50%。即使在明确提示不要作弊的条件下，平均作弊率仍高于50%。此外，作者发布了BAITBENCH、裁判实现以及包含奖励黑客行为的标注转录数据集。

Conclusion: 奖励黑客行为在前沿LLM智能体中普遍存在且难以通过简单提示来缓解，凸显了当前AI智能体在自主科研场景中的安全性风险。BAITBENCH可作为一个评估奖励黑客缓解策略的标准测试平台，为后续安全研究提供支撑。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used to run autonomous ML experiments, iterating on target metrics with little human oversight. Prior work has documented reward hacking in these environments, bringing into question the validity of produced research and the broader safety case for AI R&D. Existing benchmarks do not measure exploits that live in the data or the modeling task itself. We introduce BAITBENCH, a suite of three synthetic tabular ML tasks that each contain a shortcut that allows agents to inflate the public test score but fail on a hidden test set. Since the shortcut is optional and using it breaks no stated rule, BAITBENCH measures how often models exploit the shortcut to achieve inflated scores. Across seven frontier agents scored by our two-stage judge pipeline, 57.1% of runs exhibit reward hacking, with five of seven above 50%. Agents cheat even under a second condition where they are prompted not to -the mean cheating rate remains above 50%. We release BAITBENCH, along with the judge implementation, and an annotated dataset of transcripts containing reward hacks as a testbed for evaluating reward-hacking mitigations head-to-head.

</details>


### [151] [E-Commerce Bench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Business Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30730)
*Wei Fan,Xinjie Shen,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Yang Su,Yinger Zhang,Lianghao Deng,Fengyu Wang,Baohua Dong,Yangqiu Song,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了E-Commerce Bench，一个用于评估LLM在长期agentic任务中能力的新型开源基准测试。该基准模拟一个为期365天的在线电商经营场景，要求LLM代理同时运营多个网店，进行市场调研、供应商谈判、销售策略优化、订单履约、退货处理及现金流管理。通过确定性市场模型确保可复现性，评测了18个前沿模型，发现没有单一模型在所有维度上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的agentic任务基准主要关注短链任务串联，无法捕捉长期任务中不断变化的动态环境、长时间跨度的依赖关系，以及需要模型在成千上万步中持续探索、学习经验并调整策略的能力。作者希望通过一个真实、可复现的长期商业运营场景来弥补这一空白。

Method: 构建了一个为期365天的模拟电商运营环境：1) 产品和供应商数据来自真实电商平台；2) 采用确定性市场需求模型（固定的客户购买和退货模型）以及确定性谈判内核（决定供应商定价、让步和决策），仅用LLM进行语言化输出；3) 包含日历化的促销活动、自然灾害和供应链冲击事件来动态塑造需求；4) 从7个维度（包括年终资产）评估模型表现。

Result: 评测了18个前沿模型，结果显示没有单一模型在所有维度上占据优势。GPT-5.6 Sol赚取最多资产（将100,000初始资金增长到1,431,425），但在欺诈规避上排名16/18，运营效率上落后于Fable5。开源模型中，Qwen3.8-Max-Preview以416,252领先，比GLM 5.2高出38%，并在长期学习能力上表现最强，能在重复订单中逐步压低价格。

Conclusion: E-Commerce Bench揭示了一个重要发现：当前前沿模型在长期agentic任务中各有短板，没有任何模型能够全面胜出，尤其在欺诈规避、运营效率等维度存在明显差距。该基准为未来长期决策型LLM代理的研究提供了可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks go beyond chaining short tasks over more interaction turns. Their evolving dynamic environments and long-range dependencies require Large Language Models (LLMs) to continually explore, learn from experience, and adapt their policies over thousands of steps. We introduce E-Commerce Bench, the first open-source benchmark that integrates multi-round counterpart negotiation and dynamic events into a year-long business operation. Over a 365-day year, an LLM agent concurrently runs multiple online stores, researching the market, negotiating with suppliers to source inventory, optimizing sales strategies, fulfilling orders, handling returns, and managing cash flow to maximize its end-of-year total assets. To construct a realistic merchant-side operating environment, the product and supplier data are derived from a real e-commerce platform, while a year-long calendar of promotions, natural disasters, and supply-chain shocks continually reshapes demand. For reproducibility, both sides of the market are deterministic: customer purchases and returns follow a fixed demand model, while a negotiation kernel determines supplier pricing, concessions, and decisions, with an LLM used only to verbalize them. We evaluate 18 frontier models across seven dimensions, including year-end assets, and find that no single model dominates. GPT-5.6 Sol earns the most, growing the 100,000 opening stake into 1,431,425, yet it ranks 16th of 18 on fraud avoidance and trails Fable5 in operational efficiency. Among open-weight models, Qwen3.8-Max-Preview leads with 416,252, 38% above GLM 5.2 (high), and achieves the strongest learning over the horizon, progressively bargaining down prices across repeated orders. Our code is available at https://github.com/QwenLM/E-CommerceBench.

</details>


### [152] [Functional Degeneracy in Neural Networks: Measurement and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30741)
*Maria Matveev,Pascal Esser,Ayush Bharadwaj,Lucius Bushnaq,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量化模型功能退化程度的指标——行为恢复秩（Behavioral Recovery Rank），将其作为压缩的几何基准，发现结构剪枝和幅度剪枝在任务饱和后仍保留更多自由度，表明功能冗余分布在参数方向上，而非单个权重或神经元层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习部署需要降低模型的内存、计算和能耗，因此关键在于理解经过训练的模型可以在多大程度上被压缩而不改变其行为。然而，现有压缩方法缺乏一个统一的几何框架来衡量模型的功能冗余程度，需要一种量化的方法来研究功能退化现象。

Method: 本文定义了行为恢复秩（Behavioral Recovery Rank），即恢复已训练模型性能所需的行为Hessian前导特征方向的数量，并以其作为压缩的几何基准，系统比较了结构剪枝与幅度剪枝在任务饱和前后保留自由度的情况。

Result: 研究发现，即使任务已经饱和，结构剪枝和幅度剪枝仍然保留了更多的自由度。这一差距表明功能冗余分布在参数方向（parameter directions）上，而并非由单个权重或神经元暴露出来。

Conclusion: 功能冗余的本质在于参数子空间的方向结构而非个体单元的离散冗余，这一发现对设计更高效的模型压缩策略具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: A central question in modern machine learning is how much a trained model can be compressed without changing its behavior, to reduce the memory, compute and energy required to deploy it. To study this, we quantify functional degeneracy through the behavioral recovery rank, defined as the number of leading behavioral-Hessian eigendirections required to recover a trained model's performance. Using the behavioral recovery rank as a geometric benchmark for compression, we find that structural and magnitude pruning retain more degrees of freedom, even after the task is saturated. This gap suggests that functional redundancy is distributed across parameter directions and is not exposed by individual weights or neurons.

</details>


### [153] [TDDM-Melatt: A Decoupled Memory and Diffusion Framework for Generalizable Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30745)
*Ze Chen,Qiming Yu,Zijia Song,Guozheng Yang,Wei Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDDM-Melatt框架，通过记忆解耦的流量表示模型（Melatt）结合扩散模型数据增强（TDDM），解决加密流量分类中虚假特征相关导致的捷径学习和长尾分布导致的样本不平衡问题，在4个公共基准数据集上超越6个基础分类模型和6个SOTA表示学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据集驱动的加密流量分类研究存在两个关键问题：一是虚假特征相关引发的捷径学习导致模型泛化能力弱；二是真实网络流量的长尾分布导致样本不平衡，影响分类性能。这些问题使得模型在真实网络环境中的流量识别效果不佳。

Method: 提出TDDM-Melatt框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Melatt记忆解耦流量表示模型，采用竞争门控长短期记忆网络（CG-LSTM）构建编码器和解码器，通过严格拓扑匿名化和冻结预训练编码器策略切断虚假特征学习路径；(2) 面向流量数据特征的流量去噪扩散模型（TDDM）用于数据增强，缓解样本不平衡问题。推理阶段由下游分类器在冻结表示上进行高效分类。

Result: 在4个代表性公共基准数据集上，采用严格的流级划分和匿名化设置，TDDM-Melatt在性能上优于6个基础分类模型和6个SOTA表示学习模型，验证了该框架在真实网络环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: TDDM-Melatt为真实网络环境中的加密流量分类提供了一条新的有效技术路径，通过解耦虚假特征学习和扩散模型数据增强，显著提升了模型在真实世界流量上的泛化能力。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of encrypted traffic poses severe challenges to current security situational awareness systems based on network traffic monitoring. In existing dataset-driven training and testing studies, limitations such as shortcut learning induced by spurious feature correlations and sample imbalance caused by the long-tail distribution of real-world traffic result in weak generalization of traffic identification performance to real-world network traffic. To address these limitations, we propose TDDM-Melatt, a disentangled memory-based traffic classification framework with diffusion-based data augmentation. First, we design Melatt, a memory-decoupled traffic representation model, which employs Competitive Gating Long Short-Term Memory (CG-LSTM) to construct the encoder and decoder. We design a spurious-correlation-free pre-training and inference paradigm, employing strict topology anonymization and a frozen pre-trained encoder strategy to cut off the model's learning pathways for spurious features. During inference, classification is performed efficiently by a downstream classifier on the frozen representations. Second, we propose a Traffic Denoising Diffusion Model (TDDM) tailored to the characteristics of traffic data. Extensive experiments are conducted on 4 representative public benchmark datasets. Under strict flow-level splitting and anonymization, TDDM-Melatt outperforms 6 basic classification models and 6 SOTA representation learning models. The proposed method provides a new and effective technical pathway for encrypted traffic classification in real-world network environments.

</details>


### [154] [Do VLMs Share Safety Neurons Across Modalities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30750)
*Jiaxuan Li,Jiahao Zhang,Duc Minh Vo,Huy H. Nguyen,Pride Kavumba,Koki Wataoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在神经元层面因果性地分析了10个视觉语言模型（VLM）的安全机制，提出两阶段检测管线（含迭代消融以应对自我修复）和两个模态隔离基准（ViSafe-Detect和ViSafe-Eval），发现文本安全可在极少数神经元上局部化（约88个，<0.01%），而视觉安全在单神经元层面是高维且弥散的，解释了当前对齐方法为何未能弥合视觉安全差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可以通过图像传递有害请求并绕过安全机制，即使其底层LLM会拒绝相同内容的文本。先前工作主要在经验或表征层面描述这类越狱行为，但在神经元层面，视觉输入如何扰动安全通路仍完全未知。填补这一空白具有重要的安全对齐意义。

Method: 提出两阶段检测管线，结合迭代消融以考虑神经元的自我修复效应；引入两个模态隔离基准ViSafe-Detect和ViSafe-Eval，将视觉与文本安全信号解耦；在10个VLM上开展因果性单神经元层面的安全性分析。

Result: 三个关键发现：(i) 文本安全是局部可定位的，仅约88个神经元（<0.01%）的定向消融即可大幅降低拒绝率；(ii) 文本安全神经元构成主要的拒绝通路，是唯一在所有模型中一致且显著降低拒绝对齐的干预；(iii) 视觉安全在单神经元层面高维且弥散——文本安全集中在约5个子空间方向，而视觉安全需要≥50个方向，这一差距跨越不同架构持续存在。

Conclusion: 文本与视觉安全在神经元层面的实现结构存在根本不对称（局部集中 vs. 高维弥散），这种架构层面的差距解释了当前对齐方法为何未能弥合视觉安全缺口，为未来VLM安全对齐研究提供了新的神经元层面的分析视角和基准工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can comply with harmful requests delivered through images, even when their LLM backbones would refuse the same content in text. While prior work characterizes these jailbreaks empirically or at the representation level, how visual inputs perturb safety pathways at the neuron level remains uncharted. We close this gap with a causal, neuron-level analysis of safety mechanisms in 10 VLMs. We propose a two-stage detection pipeline with iterative ablation that accounts for self-repair, and introduce two modality-isolated benchmarks, ViSafe-Detect and ViSafe-Eval, which decouple visual and textual safety signals.
  Our analysis reveals: (i) Text safety in VLMs is localizable: $\sim$88 neurons ($<$0.01%) whose targeted ablation substantially reduces refusal. (ii) Text safety neurons constitute the dominant refusal pathway: ablating them is the only intervention that consistently and substantially reduces refusal across all models. (iii) Visual safety is high-dimensional and diffuse at the single-neuron level: text safety concentrates in $\sim$5 subspace directions while visual safety requires $\geq$50. This gap holds across architectures, explaining why current alignment has not closed the visual safety gap. Project page is at: https://jiaxuan-li.github.io/vlm-safety-neuron/
  Warning: this paper may include examples of harmful content.

</details>


### [155] [PRACTICE: From Experience to Expertise in Self-Evolving Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30760)
*Ziyi Bai,Siqi Li,Tinglei Huang,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRACTICE是一个训练技能学习者（skill learner）从历史交互轨迹中发现并维护持久技能库的框架，通过结构化的批量编辑（添加、细化、合并或删除技能）和两阶段课程学习，使冻结的任务执行器（executor）在多轮库更新中持续获得性能提升，在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat基准上超越现有基于经验的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型可作为具身智能体，但现有的经验学习方法依赖手工设计的提示流程来提取和更新技能，这种固定流程难以从多样化的新经验中学习更新技能，导致智能体难以通过交互持续提升和快速适应新环境。

Method: 1) 训练技能学习者从过去的交互轨迹中自动发现和维护持久技能库，同时冻结任务执行器；2) 技能学习者基于已有技能和新轨迹输出结构化批量编辑（添加、细化、合并或删除技能），并通过层级整合将所有编辑汇成一致更新的技能库；3) 采用两阶段课程训练：第一阶段从oracle轨迹学习基础技能生成和库维护，第二阶段通过对比异构执行器在相同任务上的成功与失败轨迹学习识别无效动作模式和恢复策略；4) 应用在线技能编辑蒸馏，使技能学习者在其当前编辑分布上与更强的教师模型对齐。

Result: 实验表明，紧凑的技能学习者在多轮库更新中为多个冻结执行器带来持续一致的性能提升，并在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat基准上超越最强的基于经验的基线方法。

Conclusion: PRACTICE通过自动化、可学习的技能发现和更新机制替代手工设计的技能提取流程，使具身智能体能从交互经验中持续学习和适应，为构建可自我改进的具身智能体提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can serve as embodied agents, translating language instructions and visual observations into executable plans. However, building agents that can continually improve through interaction and rapidly adapt to their environments remains challenging. Summing up experience from past interaction trajectories provides a promising solution, but existing experience-based methods often rely on manually designed prompting workflows to extract and update skills. Such fixed procedures may struggle to learn updated skills from new and diverse experiences. We introduce PRACTICE, which trains a skill learner to discover and maintain a persistent skill library from past interaction trajectories while keeping the task executor frozen. Given the historical accumulated skills and incoming trajectories, the skill learner produces structured batch-edits that add, refine, merge, or remove skills, and then hierarchical consolidate all collected edits into a consistent updated skill library. We train the learner with a two-stage curriculum. First, it learns basic skill generation and library maintenance from oracle trajectories. Then, by contrasting successful and failed trajectories from heterogeneous executors on the same tasks, it learn to identify invalid action patterns and recovery strategies. Finally, we apply online skill-edit distillation to align the skill learner with a stronger teacher on its current edit distribution to further improves the policy. Experiments demonstrate that a compact skill learner delivers consistent performance improvements across successive library-update rounds for multiple frozen executors. On EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat, PRACTICE further outperforms the strongest experience-based baselines. Project resources are publicly available at: https://baai-agents.github.io/PRACTICE

</details>


### [156] [T3S: Improving Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Task-Specific Feature Selector and Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30765)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Tianpei Yang,Yongliang Lv,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为T3S的多任务强化学习框架，通过超网络生成任务特定的软掩码来选择特征，并利用任务调度器根据任务进度和学习速度动态选择学习任务，从而缓解任务间干扰问题，在机器人操控任务上超越了现有的最先进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务强化学习方法通常通过共享参数来训练单一模型解决多个任务，但未解决哪些参数应跨任务共享的问题，导致任务间干扰（inter-task interference），显著降低了学习效率。

Method: T3S框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 特征选择器（Feature Selector）：利用超网络（hypernetworks）为每个任务构造任务特定的软掩码（soft masks），将其应用于全局共享表示以生成任务特定特征；(2) 任务调度器（Task Scheduler）：通过两个指标选择学习任务，选择概率与任务进度（如成功率）和任务学习速度成反比。

Result: 在多种机器人操控任务上的实验结果表明，T3S在性能上持续优于最先进的多任务强化学习算法。

Conclusion: T3S通过特征选择器和任务调度器的协同设计，有效解决了多任务强化学习中的任务间干扰问题，提升了学习效率，并在机器人操控任务上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Multi-task reinforcement learning (MTRL) is a technique to train multiple tasks simultaneously, where previous works usually train a single model to solve different tasks by sharing parameters across various tasks. However, these methods are faced with inter-task interference since what parameters should be shared across tasks is not addressed, dramatically reducing learning efficiency. To solve these problems, we propose a novel MTRL framework called Task-Specific feature Selector and Scheduler (T3S), which consists of two components: a feature selector and a task scheduler. Specifically, the feature selectors employ hypernetworks to construct task-specific soft masks, which can be applied by globally shared representation to construct task-specific features. The task scheduler selects tasks for learning through two metrics, where the selection probability is inversely proportional to task progress (e.g., success rate) and task learning speed. Experimental results show that T3S consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art MTRL algorithms on various robotics manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [157] [TrainSDC: Characterizing and Mitigating Silent Data Corruption in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30769)
*Zhipeng Xia,Haotian Xu,Siyu Yun,Liqi Lin,Hu Liu,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了Transformer训练中前向和反向传播各计算接口对静默数据损坏（SDC）的脆弱性，发现前向脆弱性与位置强相关（Q/K路径故障导致持续训练偏差），反向脆弱性主要由梯度指数分布决定。基于此提出TrainSDC保护框架（含Q/K路径重算、残差增益监控、指数感知梯度缩放），在Llama 3.2-1B和Qwen3-0.6B上以仅1.65%-6.76%开销维持接近无故障的训练行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练日益遭受静默数据损坏（SDC）的威胁，但现有防护方法将Transformer计算同质化处理，因为其脆弱性尚未被充分理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统理解SDC在不同计算接口上的传播机制，从而指导更精准的防护策略。

Method: 首先对Transformer训练的前向和反向传播中各主要计算接口（如Q/K、V、残差连接、梯度等）进行系统性SDC脆弱性表征，识别出两种不同的错误传播机制。基于表征结果设计TrainSDC框架，包含三个组件：Q/K路径重计算（针对前向位置依赖的脆弱性）、残差增益监控（检测并抑制持久性偏差）、指数感知梯度缩放（针对反向梯度指数分布的脆弱性）。在稀疏和密集故障注入下进行实验验证。

Result: 在Llama 3.2-1B和Qwen3-0.6B两个模型上的实验表明，TrainSDC在稀疏和密集故障注入场景下均能维持接近无故障执行的训练行为，引入的运行时开销仅为1.65%-6.76%。

Conclusion: 通过揭示SDC在Transformer训练中两种截然不同的错误传播机制，本文证明了脆弱性感知的差异化防护策略具有显著优势，TrainSDC以极低开销实现了对训练行为的有效保护，为LLM训练可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM training is increasingly vulnerable to silent data corruption (SDC), yet existing protection methods largely treat Transformer computations uniformly because their vulnerability remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic characterization of SDC vulnerability across major computation interfaces in both the forward and backward passes of Transformer training. Our analysis reveals two distinct error propagation mechanisms: forward-pass vulnerability is highly location dependent, with faults on the Q/K path producing persistent training deviations, whereas backward-pass vulnerability is largely governed by gradient exponent distributions rather than computation locations. Motivated by these observations, we propose TrainSDC, a characterization-guided protection framework consisting of Q/K-path recomputation, residual-gain monitoring, and exponent-aware gradient scaling. Experiments on Llama 3.2-1B and Qwen3-0.6B show that TrainSDC maintains training behavior close to fault-free execution under both sparse and dense fault injection while introducing only 1.65%-6.76% runtime overhead.

</details>


### [158] [Reciprocity Separates Gradient Flow from Rotation in Conservative Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30778)
*Ruiwu Niu,Xiaowen Bi,Michaël Antonie van Wyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究物理学习系统中学习轨迹偏离传统梯度下降的条件与后果。以有向分层输运网络为典型模型，发现守恒律只约束允许的学习方向，在匹配响应类中加入反对称边界成分可使闭环响应产生旋转（非互易），学习路径可偏离梯度方向。转折的有限步效应由局部曲率决定，累积效应还依赖于步长选择和路径上访问的新状态，数值验证了精确响应结构并证明轨迹漂移可能抵消局部优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习让可训练材料或网络利用自身物理响应来承载误差信号，无需单独编程的反向计算。现有知识不清楚系统何时遵循传统梯度下降、何时沿真正不同的学习轨迹演化，需要揭示决定因素。

Method: 以有向分层输运网络为典型模型，每个节点重新分配固定流量，保证学习过程保持正性和总质量守恒。在匹配响应类中利用伴随匹配使物理输出响应呈对称形式，引入非负逐模态反馈产生互易闭环响应和加权梯度流；添加反对称边界成分使闭环响应产生旋转。通过数值一致性检验验证响应结构，预测局部效应的符号并分析轨迹漂移的影响。

Result: 守恒律仅约束允许的学习方向而非改变学习轨迹的本质；伴随匹配给出对称的物理输出响应；非负逐模态反馈产生互易闭环响应与加权梯度流；反对称边界分量使闭环响应旋转，学习路径可转向而误差驱动仍递减。转折并非自然有利，有限步效应由局部曲率决定，累积效应依赖步长与新访问状态。数值检验复现精确响应结构，预测局部效应符号，证明轨迹漂移可抵消局部优势。

Conclusion: 研究将物理学习中的守恒、互易与非互易作用加以区分：守恒仅限制允许方向，互易性产生可重加权梯度流，而非互易（反对称）成分引发真偏离梯度下降的旋转路径。这些结果阐明了物理学习轨迹偏离传统梯度的条件及其利弊边界。

Abstract: Physical learning lets a trainable material or network use its own physical response to carry error signals, reducing the need for a separately programmed backward computation. We ask what determines whether such a system follows conventional gradient descent or evolves along a genuinely different learning trajectory. Our canonical model is a directed layered transport network in which every node redistributes a fixed amount of flow, so learning preserves positivity and total mass. In this model, conservation constrains only the allowable learning directions. Within the matched response class studied here, adjoint matching gives the physical output response a symmetric form. Non-negative mode-wise feedback then produces a reciprocal closed-loop response and a reweighted gradient flow. Adding an antisymmetric boundary component makes the closed-loop response rotational: the learning path can turn while the error driving that update still decreases at that moment. Turning is not automatically beneficial. Its finite-step effect is set by local curvature, and its accumulated effect also depends on step selection and on the new states visited along the path. Numerical consistency checks reproduce the exact response structure, predict the sign of the local effect across new network families, and show how trajectory drift can negate a local advantage. These results separate the roles of conservation, reciprocity, and nonreciprocity in physical learning.

</details>


### [159] [Geometric Attractor Monitoring: A Robust and Frugal Framework for Multi-modal Industrial Robotic Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30804)
*Martin Bonsergent-Brachet,Jesse Read,Dany Abboud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相空间重构（PSR）的工业机器人健康监测框架，将单变量传感器数据转化为几何吸引子而非预测时间序列，并通过离散支持估计作为健康指标。在21台异构机器人（三年真实数据）和合成Langevin系统上验证，性能优于标准深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构工业机器人舰队健康监测面临两大挑战：运行周期多模态性质复杂，以及run-to-failure数据稀缺。现有基于序列重建的深度学习方法（如自编码器类架构）在此场景下容易过度平滑复杂动力学，掩盖早期退化迹象，不适用于工业实际约束。

Method: 采用相空间重构（PSR）框架替代时序预测范式：将单变量传感器数据转换为几何吸引子，显式展开机械状态独立于时间发生顺序；在重构空间内比较多种异常评分技术，最终选择离散支持估计作为计算成本低廉且有效的健康指标（HI）。

Result: 在21台异构机器人的三年真实数据集和合成Langevin系统上验证，所提PSR框架优于标准深度学习基线。离散支持估计方法被证明在有效性（检测精度）和计算开销（资源节约）之间达到最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 将算法偏差与目标系统的几何特性对齐，可获得实用、可追溯且易于部署的健康监测方案，非常适合工业约束场景。相空间重构为缺乏大规模故障数据的异构机器人舰队监测提供了一种计算高效且可解释的替代路径。

Abstract: Monitoring the health of heterogeneous industrial robot fleets is severely challenged by the multi-modal nature of their operational cycles and a persistent scarcity of run-to-failure data. Standard data-driven approaches, particularly deep learning architectures relying on sequential reconstruction, often struggle in this specific setting; they tend to over-smooth complex dynamics, masking early signs of degradation. To address these industrial constraints, we reframe the monitoring problem through a framework based on Phase Space Reconstruction (PSR). Instead of predicting temporal sequences, this framework transforms univariate sensor data into a geometric attractor, explicitly unfolding the mechanical states independently of their temporal occurrence. By evaluating various anomaly scoring techniques within this space, we demonstrate that discrete support estimation provides an effective and computationally frugal Health Indicator (HI). Validated on a real-world dataset of 21 heterogeneous robots over three years and a synthetic Langevin system, our approach outperforms standard deep learning baselines. We show that aligning the algorithmic bias with the geometric properties of the target system yields a pragmatic, traceable and easily deployable approach perfectly tailored to the realities of industrial constraints.

</details>


### [160] [What Emerges and What Breaks in Self-Play Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30819)
*Laur Sisask,Ardi Tampuu,Tambet Matiisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究纯自我博弈训练自动驾驶策略，将模型从MLP扩展到Transformer，并在真实城市高清地图上训练。虽然在CARLA和Waymax基准上落后于Gigaflow，但通过分析找出了失败模式（如红绿灯奖励黑客、缺缺失停止标志激励），并验证了奖励调节能产生预期行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自我博弈训练自动驾驶策略已展现出有前景的结果（如Gigaflow、PufferDrive），但已有工作多使用简单的MLP模型。本文旨在探索将Transformer架构与自我博弈训练结合，在真实城市的高清地图上进行训练，以期最终在真实环境中部署。

Method: 沿用Gigaflow和PufferDrive的自我博弈训练方式，但将模型架构从MLP扩展为Transformer，并在真实城市的高清地图（HD map）上进行训练。在CARLA和Waymax基准上进行评估，并通过分析训练过程中涌现的交通规则来诊断策略行为。

Result: 在CARLA和Waymax基准上，提出的方法在性能上不及Gigaflow。通过分析发现差距源于特定失败模式：红绿灯处的奖励黑客行为以及对停止标志缺乏停车的激励。同时确认了奖励条件调节能产生预期的驾驶行为多样性。

Conclusion: 尽管Transformer+自我博弈的组合在真实地图上训练尚未超越Gigaflow，但研究提供了对自我博弈训练中涌现的交通规则与人类驾驶匹配度的深入分析，识别了关键失败模式，并验证了奖励调节的有效性，为后续改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Training autonomous driving policies through pure self-play has recently shown promising results. Following Gigaflow and Puffer- Drive, we train driving policies in a similar self-play fashion, but extend the models from MLPs to Transformers and train on the high-definition map of a real city, where we ultimately aim to deploy them. On the CARLA and Waymax benchmarks, our policies fall short of Gigaflow, and we trace the gap to specific failure modes, including reward hacking at traffic lights and a missing incentive to stop at stop signs. We further analyze which traffic rules emerge from self-play and how closely they match human driving, and we confirm that reward conditioning yields the intended diversity of driving behaviors. A demonstration of a trained policy is available at https://laursisask-ut.github.io/eccvdemo.

</details>


### [161] [Deploying DeepSeek 175B Locally on a Single Consumer-Grade RTX 4060 Laptop with 32GB RAM for 200k-Scale Protein-Ligand Virtual Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30877)
*Rui Xiao,Yili Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全本地的低资源框架，在配备32GB RAM和8GB VRAM的单张消费级RTX 4060笔记本上部署175B参数的DeepSeek LLM，完成了20个蛋白靶标的20万级虚拟筛选工作流，72小时内实现8卡A100集群100倍吞吐量，预测误差0.88 kcal/mol满足药物发现精度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模虚拟筛选的SOTA管线几乎完全依赖拥有数百GB内存的高端GPU集群，硬件门槛极高，阻碍了小型学术团队进行AI驱动的药物发现研究。作者旨在打破这一硬件壁垒，验证在消费级硬件上运行工业级LLM生物医学计算任务的可行性。

Method: 该框架在消费级RTX 4060笔记本（32GB系统RAM+8GB VRAM）上部署175B参数的DeepSeek模型，通过异构内存管理和模型优化策略实现参数高效加载与推理，并完成覆盖20个蛋白靶标的20万规模蛋白-配体虚拟筛选完整工作流。

Result: 在72小时内实现了8卡A100集群基线的100倍吞吐量；所有靶标的平均结合亲和力预测误差为0.88 kcal/mol，满足临床前药物发现1.0 kcal/mol的化学精度要求；运行时剖析显示异构内存管理开销占总执行时间72%，而模型优化引入的精度损失仅占预测总误差的不到10%。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了在消费级硬件上运行工业级乃至万亿参数LLM驱动生物医学计算任务的工程可行性，为AI赋能的早期药物发现建立了低门槛的新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance in protein-ligand interaction prediction, but state-of-the-art pipelines for large-scale virtual screening almost exclusively rely on high-end GPU clusters with hundreds of gigabytes of memory, creating prohibitive hardware barriers for small academic teams. In this work, we present a fully local low-resource framework that deploys the 175-billion-parameter DeepSeek 175B LLM on a single consumer-grade RTX 4060 laptop equipped with 32GB system RAM and 8GB VRAM, completing a full 200k-scale protein-ligand virtual screening workflow across 20 distinct protein targets. Our implementation achieves 100x throughput of an 8-card A100 cluster baseline under identical task configurations within 72 hours, with an average binding affinity prediction error of 0.88 kcal/mol across all targets, satisfying the 1.0 kcal/mol chemical accuracy requirement for preclinical drug discovery. Systematic runtime profiling reveals that heterogeneous memory management overhead accounts for 72% of total execution time, while accuracy loss introduced by model optimization contributes less than 10% to total prediction error. This work validates the engineering feasibility of running industrial-scale trillion-parameter LLM-driven biomedical computing tasks on consumer hardware, establishing a new low-barrier paradigm for AI-powered early stage drug discovery.

</details>


### [162] [Fine-Tuning Low-Bit Models with Gradient in Quantized Code Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30908)
*Shiguang Wu,Zhouchen Lin,Quanming Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GradCodes方法，通过码代理梯度作为一阶信号加速低比特模型的低比特微调优化，同时结合引导搜索保持部署保真度，在算术推理、指令跟随和结构化语言理解任务上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特模型微调（fine-tuning低比特模型）旨在保持模型以低比特形式部署以降低存储和推理成本，但现有方法存在两难：连续低比特训练高效但受直通估计误差或后量化差距影响，离散搜索部署保真但训练效率过低。本文旨在弥合这一效率与保真度之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出码代理梯度（code surrogate gradient）作为可部署码空间中的一阶优化信号以加速优化，并结合引导搜索（guided search）来保持部署保真度。该方法将低比特微调形式化为对量化码和尺度的优化问题。

Result: 在算术推理、指令跟随和结构化语言理解等多项任务上，GradCodes在不同量化数据类型下均一致提升了低比特模型微调的性能，且代码已开源。

Conclusion: GradCodes通过引入码代理梯度和引导搜索，有效解决了低比特微调中优化效率与部署保真度不可兼得的问题，是一种通用且有效的低比特微调方法，适用于多种量化数据格式与任务场景。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Low-bit models aims to adapt a quantized model while keeping the final deployed checkpoint in the same low-bit form. This setting is practically important as it reduces memory and inference cost for storage and deployment. Under this constraint, adaptation becomes an optimization problem over quantization codes and scales. Existing continuous low-bit training is efficient, but it can be distorted by straight through estimation error or by post-quantize gap; discrete search is deployment-faithful, but it is often too inefficient under a finite training budget. We propose code surrogate gradient as the first order signal in deployable code space to acceleate optimization, and performing guided search to preserve deployment faithfulness. Experiments across arithmetic reasoning, instruction following, and structured language understanding show that GradCodes consistently improves fine-tuning low-bit models across different quantization datatypes. Code is provided at https://github.com/ovo67/GradCodes.

</details>


### [163] [S3C-LLM: Skill-Code Guided Agentic Language Models for Spectrum-to-Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30910)
*Xuanle Zhao,Xinyuan Cai,Xiang Cheng,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S3C-LLM，一种技能引导且代码驱动的智能体LLM用于光谱-结构解析。它不是直接做谱图到SMILES生成，而是检索模态特定的光谱学技能，执行分析代码实例化技能，整合峰级证据和分子式约束后生成SMILES。通过自进化技能库、思维增强的技能-代码轨迹构建管道和两阶段训练策略（SFT+逐步RL），在多个基准上优于通用LLM和光谱专用模型，且仅用SpectraLLM的1/10训练语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM光谱解析方法多直接进行谱图到SMILES的端到端生成，虽能利用配对光谱数据，但未显式建模光谱学家真实的分析工作流（诊断峰解释、碎片推理、分子式约束、化学一致性检查），导致模型缺乏专业推理能力。

Method: S3C-LLM包含三个核心组件：（1）自进化光谱技能库，存储模态特异的光谱解析技能；（2）思维增强的技能-代码轨迹构建管道，将技能实例化为可执行分析代码；（3）两阶段训练策略，先用SFT训练Qwen3-4B，再通过逐步级强化学习（RL）微调。模型流程为：检索技能→执行代码→整合峰级证据和分子式约束→生成SMILES。

Result: 在多样化的基准上，S3C-LLM在不同光谱类型上一致优于当前通用LLM和光谱专用模型，同时仅使用SpectraLLM训练语料的不到1/10，展现了极高的数据效率和性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过将光谱学家的分析工作流（技能引导+代码执行+证据整合）显式建模到LLM中，并采用SFT+RL的两阶段训练策略，S3C-LLM证明了用更少数据实现更好光谱-结构解析的可行性，为光谱解析的LLM方法提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spectroscopic structure elucidation is central to molecular analysis, but recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods mostly formulate it as direct spectrum-to-SMILES generation. Although this paradigm can leverage paired spectral data, it does not explicitly model the analytical workflow used by spectroscopists, such as diagnostic peak interpretation, fragment reasoning, formula constraints, and chemical consistency checking. In this paper, we introduce S3C-LLM, a skill-guided and code-grounded agentic LLM for spectrum-to-structure elucidation. Rather than directly predicting a molecule, S3C-LLM retrieves modality-specific spectroscopy skills, executes analysis code to instantiate these skills on the input spectra, and integrates the resulting peak-level evidence and formula constraints before generating SMILES. Specifically, we contribute a self-evolving spectroscopy skill library, a thinking-augmented skill-code trajectory construction pipeline, and a two-stage training strategy that teaches Qwen3-4B through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by our proposed step-level reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments on diverse benchmarks show that S3C-LLM consistently outperforms current general LLMs and spectrum-specific models across spectra, while using less than 1/10th of SpectraLLM's training corpus.

</details>


### [164] [Selection-Aware Stress Testing for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30916)
*Yang Xu,Chenang Li,Jiefu Zhang,Haixiang Sun,Zhou Li,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择感知语义压力测试（SASST），针对智能体评估中"同一数据既选工作流又找弱项"的选择偏差问题，通过发现任务与确认任务分离、联合边界约束等机制，提供统计学上有效的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估常用单一基准选择工作流，再从同一数据中寻找其优势减弱的任务类型，导致结论与数据存在选择偏差，缺乏统计有效性保障。

Method: SASST从预执行特征中学习任务重新加权，用于发现任务；在独立的确认任务上评估相同的配对比较。协议支持支持度与稳定性检查，对所有计划声明使用联合边界，且允许返回无声明。并证明在给定聚类假设下条件的渐近有效性。

Result: 四十聚类的审计发现高斯覆盖不足，Bonferroni t边界偏保守。在480回合τ-bench研究中，发现阶段3.75分的优势在确认阶段消失；第二模型研究同样既未确认工作流收益，也未形成稳定的压力规则。

Conclusion: SASST揭示了许多基于单一数据选择得出的智能体评估结论缺乏确认支持，为智能体评估提供了更严谨的统计验证框架。

Abstract: Agent evaluations often use one benchmark to choose a workflow and then search for task types where its advantage weakens, so both conclusions are selected from the same data. We introduce Selection-Aware Semantic Stress Testing (\SASST{}), which learns a task reweighting from pre-execution features on discovery tasks and evaluates the same paired comparison on separate confirmation tasks. The protocol checks support and stability, uses joint bounds for all planned claims, and can return no claim. We prove conditional asymptotic validity under stated cluster assumptions. A forty-cluster audit finds Gaussian undercoverage and conservative Bonferroni $t$ bounds. In one 480-episode $τ$-bench study, a $3.75$ point discovery gain vanished on confirmation. A second-model study likewise confirmed neither a workflow benefit nor a stable stress rule.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Stream Learning on Embedded Systems: Benchmarking the Memory Consumption of Stream Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30923)
*Sebastian Buschjäger,Nuwan Gunasekara,Heitor Murilo Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对七种代表性流式分类器在128 KiB至约8 MiB的模型大小预算下进行了大规模基准测试（6,463次实验），揭示了两种资源失效模式：自适应集成方法因初始占用过大而立即超出小预算，增量树方法则随长流增长而膨胀。作者呼吁将资源使用约束作为与概念漂移适应同等重要的首要设计目标，并提出了包括暴露和尊重资源预算的API在内的具体措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式学习研究过度聚焦于概念漂移适应和预测性能，而资源使用通常仅作为评估的副产品被提及。然而在长流运行和近传感器嵌入式系统等场景下，可预测且有界的资源使用至关重要。作者希望通过系统化基准测试揭示现有方法在资源约束下的失效模式，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者选择了七个具有代表性的流式分类器，在十三个真实和合成数据流上进行基准测试，模型大小预算从128 KiB扩展到约8 MiB。评估指标包括失败感知准确率、峰值模型大小、达到预算耗尽的时长以及预测加更新的延迟，共开展了6,463次实验。

Result: 结果识别出两种截然不同的资源失效模式：自适应集成模型（如adaptive ensembles）因其初始足迹过大，即使后续大小稳定也可能立即超出小预算；而增量树模型（HT和EFDT）初始可适配但会在长流中持续增长，中位数分别增加7.37倍和5.87倍。显式紧凑方法在最小预算下是唯一可行方案，但随着预算增大，自适应集成方法变得具有竞争力并通常超越前者。

Conclusion: 许多先进流式学习方法在嵌入式系统或长期运行场景中仅部分适用。作者呼吁流式学习社区将资源使用有界性作为与漂移适应并列的一等设计目标，并提出具体步骤，包括设计一个API让流式学习器能够显式暴露并尊重资源预算。

Abstract: Stream learning is commonly evaluated through predictive performance and adaptation to concept drift. However, sustained operation of a stream learner also requires predictable and bounded resource usage even on long streams. This requirement becomes even more critical when learning moves from servers to near-sensor embedded systems where memory and processing are scarce resources. In state-of-the-art stream learning, however, we perceive a strong focus on concept drift adaptation, whereas resource usage is often an evaluation byproduct. To close this gap, we benchmark seven representative stream classifiers on 13 real and synthetic streams under model-size budgets from 128\,KiB to approximately 8\,MiB. Our benchmark comprises a total of 6,463 experiments. We measure failure-aware accuracy, peak model size, time to budget exhaustion, and prediction-plus-update latency. The results reveal two distinct resource failure modes. Adaptive ensembles can exceed small budgets almost immediately because of their initial footprint, even when their size remains stable thereafter. Incremental trees can fit initially but grow throughout a long stream, with HoeffdingTrees (HT) and Extremely Fast Decision Trees (EFDT) increasing by median factors of 7.37 and 5.87. Explicitly compact methods remain the only viable option under the smallest budgets, but are usually overtaken as larger budgets make adaptive ensembles competitive. Hence, many state-of-the-art methods are only partially applicable in embedded systems or for long-running systems. We therefore call on the stream-learning community to make bounded resource usage a first-class design objective alongside drift adaptation, and propose concrete steps toward this goal, including an API through which stream learners can explicitly expose and respect resource budgets.

</details>


### [166] [Nonparametric Contextual Pricing and Inventory Learning under Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30944)
*Zean Han,Jing Liang,Ruihan Lin,Zezhen Ding,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线零售中产品缺货时的需求截断学习问题，提出MCK-UCB算法实现上下文相关的定价与库存联合决策，证明了最小最大最优性，并验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线零售中，产品售罄时零售商只能看到实际售出量，无法得知在库存充足情况下本可产生的需求。但库存水平决定了需求的揭示程度，这些信息会影响后续决策和未来利润。现有方法通常需要对需求函数做出特定假设或观测真实利润，这在实践中难以满足，因此需要一种无需假设需求公式、无需观测真实利润的上下文相关定价与库存联合学习方法。

Method: 提出均值校准的核UCB（MCK-UCB）算法，将每次不完整的销售记录转化为定价和库存决策的可靠指导信号，利用过去具有相似市场条件的轮次数据。算法无需单独的探索阶段，也无需恢复被缺货隐藏的全部需求，能够边服务客户边学习。

Result: 证明了所提算法的最小最大最优性，且当预期利润随价格变化更加平滑时，算法可获得严格更快的收敛速率。通过综合数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: MCK-UCB算法有效解决了缺货截断数据下的在线定价与库存联合决策问题，实现了无需需求模型假设和利润观测的学习，具有理论最优性保证和良好的实际表现。

Abstract: In online retailing, when a product sells out, a retailer often sees only the units sold, not how many customers would have bought it had inventory been available. However, the inventory level determines how much demand is revealed, and this information can influence subsequent decisions and future profits. We study an online selling problem in which, in each round, the seller observes a market context and then makes pricing and stocking decisions based on censored sales data from previous rounds. The challenge is to learn a context-dependent pricing and stocking policy without assuming a particular formula for demand or observing realized profit. To overcome this difficulty, we propose a Mean-Calibrated Kernel UCB (MCK-UCB) algorithm that turns each incomplete sales record into a reliable guide for both inventory and price decisions, using data from past rounds with similar market conditions. This design allows us to learn while serving customers, without a separate exploration phase or the need to recover all demand hidden by stockouts. We prove the minimax optimality of the proposed algorithm, with strictly faster rates when expected profit varies more smoothly with price. Comprehensive numerical experiments have been conducted to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [167] [Reproducible macroscopic dynamics in a closed-loop human-AI learning system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30946)
*Minlin Wu,Xu Fang,Yicheng Zhang,Chenyu Zhou,Zhiyi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用297,915名学习者的自适应辅导历史数据，定义了语义序变量并在用户不相交队列中测试，发现系统状态具有可复现的盆地状流和亚稳态动力学。通过四条件条件机制恢复群体漂移（r=0.946），自监督学习恢复状态和流场，并确认真实动力学中存在一个外部锚定的主导有效场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环人机系统生成的高维行为轨迹的集体动态长期未被阐明，缺乏对这类系统中涌现行为规律的系统性理解，需要一种不依赖事后模型拟合的先验界定方法来揭示其内在动力学结构。

Method: 在模型拟合之前定义语义序变量，在用户不相交队列中进行验证；使用构造匹配的零模型区分归一化记忆弛豫与可复现超额场；构建四条件条件机制恢复群体漂移；采用预测性事件级自监督学习恢复状态和习得平面流场；进行乱序训练和支持对齐随机化等消融实验进行对照验证。

Result: 四条件机制恢复群体漂移r=0.946（学习者自助法95% CI, 0.935-0.955）；自监督学习恢复状态和平面流，零参照修正保留方向性和部分幅度的超额场结构；乱序训练反转平面流，支持对齐随机化选择性减少内向传输。跨模型（无交叉拟合）共享主导群体漂移r=0.866（95% CI, 0.857-0.875）和持续性排序，残差方向为模型特有。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个外部锚定的主导有效场，将实际经验动力学、可解释机制和神经计算联系起来，表明高维人机交互行为轨迹中存在可复现、跨模型一致的低维动力学结构。

Abstract: Closed-loop human-AI systems generate high-dimensional behavioural trajectories whose collective dynamics remain obscure. Using 297,915 learners' adaptive-tutoring histories, we define semantic order variables before model fitting and test them in user-disjoint cohorts. The state exhibits reproducible basin-like flow and operationally defined, state-heterogeneous metastable-like kinetics. A construction-matched null distinguishes normalised-memory relaxation from a reproducible excess field. A four-term conditional mechanism recovers population drift (r = 0.946; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.935-0.955). Predictive event-level self-supervised learning recovers the state and learned-plane flow; null-referenced corrections retain directional, partial-amplitude excess-field structure without full calibration. Shuffled-order training reverses learned-plane flow on ordered trajectories; support-alignment randomisation selectively reduces inward transport. Both axes remain linearly accessible without state supervision. Without cross-model fitting, the models share leading population drift (r = 0.866; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.857-0.875) and persistence ordering; residual directions remain model-specific. These results identify an externally anchored leading-order effective field linking empirical dynamics, an interpretable mechanism and neural computation.

</details>


### [168] [One Policy Is Enough: Single-Agent Reinforcement Learning Outperforms Tree Search for Chemistry Tool Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30952)
*Armin Dariani,Sifan Wu,Bang Liu,Entao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化化学工具调用框架的方法：无需分层进化MCTS和双批评器，仅用单一策略模型以从左到右的自回归方式交错生成推理、工具调用和返回结果，通过监督式预热后接结果级强化学习（以黄金调用链的程序化奖励）训练，在ChemToolBench上显著提升Tool F1和Return F1，且每次问题仅需一次模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学问题常需精确计算与数据库查询，语言模型参数无法提供这些信息，必须借助外部工具。现有系统如CheMatAgent采用分层进化MCTS加双批评器的复杂架构，训练和推理成本高昂。作者希望证明单一策略模型即可解决工具选择、参数填充和链式调用的问题。

Method: 采用单一策略模型，在一个从左到右的生成过程中交错输出推理文本、工具调用和返回结果。训练分两阶段：先进行有监督的预热训练，再用基于黄金调用链的程序化奖励进行结果级强化学习。奖励直接来自黄金调用链的匹配结果，无需学习型批评器或无评判模型参与训练循环。

Result: 在ChemToolBench多工具综合化学任务上，以Qwen-2.5-7B为骨干，Tool F1提升5.5%，Return F1提升9.6%；以Llama-3.1-8B为骨干，Tool F1提升3.7%，Return F1提升3.9%（均对比CheMatAgent最强搜索配置）。每次问题仅需一次模型调用，而对比方法的搜索成本随树规模增长。在Qwen-2.5-7B上答案通过率（Pass Rate）也领先。

Conclusion: 作者证明复杂的双模型+双批评器+进化MCTS架构对于工具调用并非必要，一个经过良好训练的单策略模型即可在工具选择、参数填充和链式调用上超越搜索式方法，同时推理成本更低，验证了结果级强化学习配合程序化奖励在工具调用任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Chemistry questions often demand exact computation and database lookups that a language model cannot supply from its parameters, so it must reach for external tools. Tool use here is a three-part problem: select the right tool from a large pool, fill it with correctly typed arguments, and chain calls so that each consumes the outputs of the last. CheMatAgent, a previously published system, addresses this with hierarchical evolutionary MCTS: separate policy and execution models searching tool-call trees under two learned critics, one regressed partly onto GPT-assigned scores. We show that a single policy suffices. Our model interleaves reasoning, tool calls, and returns in one left-to-right generation, trained by a supervised warm-up and then outcome-level reinforcement learning against a programmatic reward read directly off the gold call chain, which leaves no learned critic and no judge in the training loop. On ChemToolBench multiple-tool comprehensive chemistry, on both backbones CheMatAgent use, we improve Tool F1 by 5.5% and Return F1 by 9.6% on Qwen-2.5-7B, and by 3.7% and 3.9% on Llama-3.1-8B, compared with their strongest search configuration, at one model invocation per question, against a search whose cost grows with the tree; we also lead answer Pass Rate on Qwen-2.5-7B.

</details>


### [169] [Singular Curvature in ReLU Training:Differentiation and the Gradient-Flow Limit Need Not Commute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30960)
*Xiaoyang Li,Runni Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降(GD)作为梯度流的显式欧拉离散化，在固定非共振步长下，普通自动微分(AD)对硬ReLU的GD程序进行精确离散微分。作者证明在有限时域内，GD状态收敛且离散导数趋近于无事件区域的传播子，而连续流导数的极限还包含按速度归一化的激活事件转移。通过前置Stieltjes表示将绝对连续的区域Hessian与原子界面曲率分离，证明单个非零梯度跳跃产生精确的秩一端点差异，且全局凸性阻止了严格事件的多事件完全抵消。然而，一类全局1-强凸的残差ReLU平方损失风险在开放初始化集合上可实现任意大的互惠敏感度比，并具有均匀横截性边际。该离散-流分解可扩展到参数和反向模式伴随，通过解析平滑和一致的事件定位恢复流灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降与梯度流之间的敏感度(sensitivity)差异是深度学习理论中的关键问题。传统的连续时间替代模型(gradient flow)在微分后可能不再精确地逼近离散GD，特别是在ReLU激活函数引入的事件(activation events)附近。作者旨在严格刻画离散GD的AD导数与连续梯度流导数之间的差异，揭示事件转移如何导致两者发散。

Method: 采用数学分析框架：将GD视为显式欧拉积分，利用固定非共振步长下的精确离散微分；通过前置Stieltjes测度表示将导数分解为绝对连续部分(区域Hessian)与原子部分(界面曲率)；分析事件转移的速度归一化效应；对全局强凸的残差ReLU平方损失构造反例，证明互惠敏感度比可任意大；证明分解可推广到反向模式伴随，并通过解析平滑恢复流灵敏度。

Result: 1) 在固定有限时域上，GD状态的离散导数一致收敛到无事件区域传播子，但连续流的导数极限额外包含速度归一化的激活事件转移项。2) 单个非零梯度跳跃导致精确的秩一(rank-one)端点差异；全局凸性下严格事件无法被多事件完全抵消。3) 在全局1-强凸的残差ReLU平方损失族上，开放初始化集合可实现任意大的互惠敏感度比(reciprocal sensitivity ratios)，且具有均匀横截性边际。4) 分解扩展到参数与反向模式伴随，解析平滑与事件定位可恢复连续流敏感度。

Conclusion: 离散GD的精确AD敏感度与连续梯度流敏感度之间存在本质差异，其根源在于离散化的事件传递机制(activation-event transfers)。这种差异即使在全局强凸的良性环境中也可能被任意放大。作者强调这些结果是关于确定性全批量有限时域动态的"一致性定理"(consistency theorems)，而非大规模训练的普遍性断言，为后续理解神经网络训练中的敏感度分析提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) is explicit Euler for gradient flow, but a state-accurate continuous-time surrogate need not remain accurate after differentiation. At every fixed nonresonant step size, ordinary automatic differentiation exactly differentiates the executed hard-ReLU GD program. We prove that, over a fixed finite horizon, the GD states converge and these exact discrete derivatives approach an event-free regional propagator, whereas the derivative of the limiting flow also contains speed-normalized activation-event transfers. A prepoint Stieltjes representation separates the absolutely continuous regional Hessian from atomic interface curvature; one nonzero gradient jump produces an exactly rank-one endpoint discrepancy, and global convexity prevents complete multi-event cancellation whenever an event is strict. Nevertheless, a standard family of globally 1-strongly convex residual-ReLU squared-loss risks realizes arbitrarily large reciprocal sensitivity ratios on open initialization sets, with a uniform transversality margin. The same discrete-versus-flow decomposition extends to parameters and reverse-mode adjoints; resolved smoothing in the scalar or autonomous-normal regime and consistent event localization recover the flow sensitivity. The results concern deterministic full-batch, finite-horizon dynamics with a stable finite itinerary of separated same-direction transverse events; they are consistency theorems, not prevalence claims for large-scale training.

</details>


### [170] [A Universal Context-Reuse Layer for Cross-Model KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30963)
*Yi Li,Dongming Jiang,Yi Zhao,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型KV共享（Cross-model KV Sharing）机制，将源模型产生的KV缓存翻译成目标模型可消费的表示形式，支持不同规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器乃至不同模型家族之间的KV重用，显著降低目标端预填充成本，并初步证明KV状态可作为可迁移的计算表示而非严格模型局部的缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存复用机制通常假设生产者和消费者是同一模型，但在多模型、多智能体推理场景中，不同模型常常处理相同或共享的上下文，每个模型仍需独立执行预填充计算。这种冗余计算造成了巨大的资源浪费，因此需要探索打破'同一模型假设'的跨模型KV共享方法。

Method: 提出跨模型KV翻译机制，将源模型生成的KV状态转换为目标模型能够消费的表示，使其适用于异构模型设置（包括规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器和模型家族差异）。系统性地在家族内和跨家族设置中评估该方法的有效性。

Result: 1) Qwen2.5-7B→Qwen2.5-1.5B：翻译KV将LongBench2准确率从27.59%提升至34.48%（+6.89个百分点超越原生1.5B基线），同时降低交接成本；2) 跨家族Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B：在4K上下文长度下目标端预填充成本降低高达67.05%，困惑度接近原生基线；3) Llama3.1-70B→Qwen2.5-7B：实现44.0%准确率（原生45.7%），延迟从899ms降至138ms。

Conclusion: KV状态可以作为可迁移的计算表示而非严格模型局部的缓存。作者提出'上下文移动性'（context mobility）作为系统抽象，用于在异构LLM和多智能体推理工作流中减少冗余预填充。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving systems increasingly operate over repeated or shared context, yet each model typically performs its own prefill computation even when another model has already processed the same input. Existing KV-cache reuse mechanisms substantially reduce redundant computation within a single model, but generally assume that the producer and consumer of a cache are identical. We study \emph{cross-model KV sharing}, which translates the KV state produced by a source model into a representation that can be consumed by a different target model, including models that differ in scale, architecture, attention configuration, tokenizer, and model family. We evaluate the approach in both within-family and cross-family settings. For Qwen2.5-7B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-1.5B, translated KV states improve LongBench2 accuracy from 27.59\% to 34.48\%, a gain of 6.89 percentage points over the native 1.5B baseline, while reducing handoff cost relative to native target prefill. For the cross-family Qwen2.5-1.5B $\rightarrow$ Gemma-2-2B setting, KV handoff reduces target-side prefill cost by up to 67.05\% at 4K context length while maintaining decoding perplexity close to native-model baselines. In a more heterogeneous Llama3.1-70B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-7B setting, cross-family handoff achieves 44.0\% accuracy compared with 45.7\% for native Qwen2.5-7B inference, while reducing measured latency from 899ms to 138ms. These results provide initial evidence that KV states can serve as transferable computational representations rather than strictly model-local caches, and motivate \emph{context mobility} as a systems abstraction for reducing redundant prefill across heterogeneous LLM and multi-agent inference workflows.

</details>


### [171] [A Human-in-the-Loop Autonomous Agent for Industry Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30976)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Ze Guo,Bokai Pan,Qi Liu,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastClaw是一个面向真实世界时间序列预测的人机协同自主预测系统，通过预测导向的工程化设计（harness engineering），将数据、专业预测模型、分析工具、用户输入和版本化执行记录整合在同一运行时中。系统以自然语言接收用户需求，在预测过程中检查时间模式与约束，缺失证据时自动检索上下文、运行分析或询问用户，并在明确停止条件下保留、修订或升级结果。在五个数据集电价预测任务中，CastClaw在16个基线中取得最低的MSE和MAE，并通过Nord Pool案例展示可检查的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列预测并非一次性的模型调用，而是需要从业者制定任务、连接数据和模型、融入领域经验、评估预测合理性并沟通不确定性的多步骤过程。专用预测模型虽然提供强数值预测但通常固定在固定流水线中，而通用的LLM代理又缺乏预测专用的检查、约束和停止规则，两者均无法满足实际预测工作的复杂需求。

Method: CastClaw通过预测导向的harness engineering构建，将数据、专用模型、分析工具、用户输入和版本化执行记录整合在一个运行时中。用户以自然语言指定目标、预测范围、约束和假设。系统从已有或模型生成的预测出发，检查时间模式和用户约束；当证据缺失时，系统检索上下文、运行分析或其他模型，或询问用户。最终在明确停止条件下保留、修订或升级预测结果，输出包含最终预测和记录输入、证据、操作和修订过程的执行报告。

Result: 在五个数据集的电力价格预测场景中，CastClaw在16个基线中取得了最低的点估计MSE和MAE；Nord Pool案例展示了可检查的工作流程；系统还在中国华北2026年1月至6月的省级电力负荷数据上进行了离线验证。

Conclusion: CastClaw通过人机协同的自主预测框架，将专用预测模型的数值能力与LLM代理的灵活交互能力相结合，实现了可检查、可追溯且性能优越的端到端时间序列预测系统，为实际预测工作流提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world time-series forecasting is rarely a one-shot model invocation: practitioners must formulate tasks, connect data and models, incorporate domain expertise, assess prediction plausibility, and communicate uncertainty. Specialized forecasting models provide strong numerical predictions but usually operate in fixed pipelines, while general-purpose large language model (LLM) agents often lack forecasting-specific checks, constraints, and stopping rules. We present CastClaw, a human-in-the-loop autonomous forecasting system built through forecasting-oriented harness engineering. CastClaw connects data, specialized models, analytical tools, user input, and a versioned execution record in one runtime. Users specify the target, horizon, constraints, and hypotheses in natural language. Starting from a supplied or model-generated forecast, CastClaw checks temporal patterns and user constraints; when evidence is missing, it retrieves context, runs an analysis or another model, or asks the user. It then keeps, revises, or escalates the result under explicit stopping conditions. The output contains the final forecast and an execution report recording inputs, evidence, actions, and revisions. In this five-dataset electricity-price setting, CastClaw reports the lowest point-estimate MSE and MAE among 16 baselines. A Nord Pool case demonstrates the inspectable workflow. CastClaw was also validated offline on provincial electricity-load data from North China covering January--June 2026.

</details>


### [172] [Sparse Competition during Training For the Emergence of Specialized Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30978)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Karim Haroun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过神经元组之间竞争动态来诱导深度神经网络模块化的方法，该方法在保持接近基线精度的同时，通过稀疏路由输入到神经元组实现基于使用的模块化，并鼓励模块特化使其激活与输入类别相关。在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，无需模块级监督即可涌现出捕获语义类别（如狗、车辆）的专门化模块，且模块数量会影响子任务的层次划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的模块化被认为可以提升可解释性和训练效率，通过促进解耦表示和减少冗余来实现。作者希望研究模块结构能否通过训练过程中的竞争动态自发涌现，而不需要显式的模块级监督。

Method: 提出一种方法：(i) 维持接近基线的准确率；(ii) 通过将输入稀疏路由到神经元组来诱导基于使用的模块化；(iii) 鼓励模块特化，使其激活与输入类别相关。本质上是利用神经元组之间的竞争动态作为模块化的简单机制。

Result: 在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上验证了该方法。结果显示专门化模块无需模块级监督即可涌现，单个模块能响应语义类别（如狗或车辆）。此外，模块数量的变化会影响子任务的层次化划分。

Conclusion: 竞争动态可以作为一种简单且有效的机制，在标准架构中诱导功能性模块化，无需额外的人工标注或模块级监督，模块能够自发捕获数据中有意义的高层结构。

Abstract: Modularity in deep neural networks has been proposed as a means of improving both interpretability and training by promoting disentangled representations and reducing redundancy. In this work, we study the emergence of modular structure through competition dynamics between groups of neurons during training. We introduce a method that (i) maintains near-baseline accuracy, (ii) induces usage-based modularity by sparsely routing inputs to neuron groups, and (iii) encourages specialization of these modules, such that their activations are correlated with input classes. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-100 and CIFAR-100 and show that with it, specialized modules emerge without module-level supervision. These modules capture a meaningful high-level structure in the data, with individual modules responding to semantic categories (e.g., dogs or vehicles). We also study the emergence of a hierarchical partition of sub-tasks depending on the number of modules. Our results suggest that competitive dynamics can serve as a simple mechanism for inducing functional modularity in standard architectures.

</details>


### [173] [Controlling Refusal Behavior of LLMs via Stiefel-Constrained Rotation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30986)
*Kirill Bunin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Vladimir Aletov,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼优化的自包含旋转干预方法，无需依赖拒绝向量等辅助构造，即可在推理时对LLM的拒绝行为进行参数高效的参数控制，并在干预效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活转向（Activation Steering）已成为推理时控制模型拒绝行为的轻量级方法。现有基于可训练旋转的方法依赖于辅助构造（如拒绝向量）来定义旋转，缺乏几何上自洽的干预机制，因此需要一种自包含、无需外部辅助结构的旋转学习方案。

Method: 提出一种基于黎曼优化的自包含方法论，在学习过程中直接在流形上优化可训练旋转变换，实现参数高效的干预。该方法不依赖拒绝向量等辅助构造，通过黎曼几何约束保证旋转的几何性质，并通过消融实验验证关键设计选择的重要性。

Result: 经验验证表明，该方法在干预效率上优于现有技术。广泛的消融研究揭示了关键设计选择对方法性能的重要影响，证明了旋转式转向方案在LLM行为控制中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于旋转的转向方案被认为是实现对LLM行为更可靠控制的有前景方向，其自包含特性与参数高效性为实现更稳健的推理时可干预性提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight approach for controlling model refusal at inference time. A growing line of research explores trainable rotations of activations to develop geometrically principled intervention mechanisms. However, existing techniques rely on auxiliary constructs, such as refusal vectors, to define these rotations. In our work, we develop a self-contained methodology for learning parameter-efficient rotational transformations based on Riemannian optimization. We empirically validate the proposed scheme, demonstrating its superiority in intervention efficiency. An extensive ablation study highlights the importance of key design choices in our method. Our results identify the proposed rotation-based steering scheme as a promising direction for more reliable control over the behavior of LLMs.

</details>


### [174] [Language-Informed Flow Matching for Trend-Guided Structure-Based 3D Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31009)
*Tianyu Gao,Zhikai Su,Jiashu Li,Wenjun Gao,Zichuan Ying,Zhe Zhao,Fei Zhang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiFT是一个基于流匹配的语言感知跨模态框架，用于趋势引导的3D分子生成（支持从头设计和骨架跃迁），通过"感知-演化-组装"智能体生成目标感知SMILES作为中间化学条件，并利用预训练化学基础模型提取语义先验，结合零初始化自适应归一化投影器和自条件解耦路由（SCDR）实现无需额外微调的跨模态条件生成，在Cross-Docked2020数据集上取得竞争性分布匹配结果并改善药物化学指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构的药物设计（SBDD）需要配体同时满足3D靶点亲和力和1D化学有效性。现有可控生成方法通常依赖任务特定的微调或采样时的外部引导，这增加了计算成本，并可能在与动态演化的3D几何约束协同时产生冲突。作者希望以语言驱动的化学语义先验作为趋势级引导，实现在不额外微调生成器的情况下完成可控3D分子生成。

Method: LiFT框架包含三个核心模块：（1）"Sense-Evolve-Assemble"智能体用于生成目标感知SMILES作为中间化学条件；（2）预训练化学基础模型从SMILES中提取连续语义先验；（3）轻量级语义投影器采用零初始化自适应归一化（zero-initialized adaptive normalization）实现稳定的跨模态条件注入。此外引入自条件解耦路由（Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router, SCDR），在ODE积分过程中根据中间结构状态调制速度场。

Result: 在Cross-Docked2020数据集上，LiFT在无额外生成器微调的任务引导设定下，实现了竞争性的分布匹配性能，同时改善了药物化学相关指标并保持竞争性的结构有效性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，语言派生的化学先验能为3D分子生成提供有效的趋势级引导，验证了语言-几何跨模态融合在SBDD领域的可行性。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) requires ligands that satisfy both 3D target affinity and 1D chemical validity. Existing controllable generation methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or externally imposed sampling-time guidance, adding cost and potentially conflicting with evolving 3D geometric constraints. We propose LiFT, a language-informed cross-modal framework built on Flow Matching for trend-guided 3D molecular generation across both de novo design and scaffold hopping. LiFT uses a "Sense-Evolve-Assemble" agent to generate target-aware SMILES as intermediate chemical conditions, from which a pre-trained chemical foundation model extracts continuous semantic priors. These priors are integrated into geometric generation through a lightweight semantic projector with zero-initialized adaptive normalization for stable cross-modal conditioning. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router (SCDR), which modulates the velocity field according to intermediate structural states during ODE integration. Experiments on Cross-Docked2020 show that LiFT achieves competitive distribution matching while improving medicinal chemistry metrics and maintaining competitive structural validity under task-steering settings without additional generator fine-tuning. Our results suggest that language-derived chemical priors provide effective trend-level guidance for 3D molecular generation. Code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/kasurl/LiFT.

</details>


### [175] [TSPFN: A Temporal Tabular Foundation Model for Physiological Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31013)
*Jérémie Stym-Popper,Clément Rambour,Federica Granese,Nicolas Thome,Olivier Bernard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSPFN，一个面向生理时间序列分类的基础模型，通过改造TabPFN架构并引入时空表示与位置嵌入，在低至中数据量条件下显著优于表格基线、TabPFN及专用深度时序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学机器学习中，低至中数据量场景下的模型泛化仍是核心挑战，尤其对生理时间序列分类而言。尽管TabPFN等表格基础模型通过上下文学习提供了微调的替代方案，但其架构并未设计用于捕捉生理信号固有的时间依赖关系，这促使作者重新设计TabPFN以适配时间序列数据。

Method: TSPFN在TabPFN架构基础上进行重新设计，融入结构化时间表示和位置嵌入，以捕获样本内部的时间依赖和通道依赖；随后在14万条来自多个医学领域的真实生理时间序列上进行预训练，形成统一的可泛化框架。

Result: 在多种生理基准上的实验表明，TSPFN一致优于标准表格基线和TabPFN，并且在跨域泛化方面优于专门设计的深度时序模型。所有实验、消融研究和预处理方案均已公开。

Conclusion: TSPFN验证了将表格基础模型架构改造为适应时间序列的必要性与有效性，为医学生理信号在数据稀缺条件下的分类提供了一种强泛化、统一的基础模型方案。

Abstract: Designing models that generalize effectively in low- to medium-data regimes remains a primary challenge in medical machine learning, particularly for physiological time-series classification. While tabular foundation models such as TabPFN offer an attractive alternative to conventional fine-tuning through in-context learning, they are not designed to capture the temporal dependencies inherent to physiological signals. ~In this paper, we introduce TSPFN, a foundation model that redesigns TabPFN's architecture for time series data. TSPFN integrates structured temporal representations and positional embeddings to capture intra-sample temporal and channel dependencies. To fully leverage its spatio-temporal design, the model is pretrained on 140,000 real-world physiological time series across multiple medical domains. This yields a unified, generalizable framework capable of learning the specificities of medical time series. Experiments across diverse physiological benchmarks demonstrate that TSPFN consistently outperforms standard tabular baselines and TabPFN, and achieves superior cross-domain generalization compared to specialized deep time-series models. All our experiments, ablation studies, and pre-processing scheme are publicly available at https://github.com/Jeremstym/TSPFN

</details>


### [176] [Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31036)
*Jiale Kang,Ziyin Yue,Zheng Zhan,Yangyi Huang,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NoRA（Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation），通过对LoRA的down-projection矩阵进行归一化来稳定并加速训练。归一化可仅应用于初始化阶段，无需额外参数或推理计算，在预训练、监督微调和强化学习等场景中均能加速收敛、提升性能与稳定性并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA被广泛用于参数高效模型适配，但其训练动态的规范化问题尚未被充分探索。由于LoRA将up-projection初始化为零，早期优化动态主要由down-projection主导，因此如何对这一关键组件进行正则化以提升训练的稳定性与有效性是本文的核心动机。

Method: NoRA的核心方法是在训练过程中对down-projection矩阵进行归一化。基于这一观察，本文进一步证明该归一化可以仅在初始化阶段应用一次，即可改善标准LoRA的性能，从而无需在训练中反复归一化。

Result: 在预训练、监督微调和强化学习三个场景下，NoRA均能持续加速收敛、提升性能和训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘。该方法不引入额外可训练参数，也不增加推理期计算开销。

Conclusion: NoRA是一种简单且广泛适用的LoRA增强方法，通过归一化down-projection实现更稳定有效的优化，兼顾了性能提升与计算/参数开销的零成本优势。

Abstract: While low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient model adaptation, how to regularize its training dynamics for stable and effective optimization remains underexplored. Because LoRA initializes the up-projection to zero, its early optimization dynamics are largely governed by the down-projection. Building on this observation, we introduce Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation (NoRA), a simple yet effective method that normalizes the down-projection matrices during training. We further show that the same normalization can be applied only at initialization, improving standard LoRA without requiring repeated normalization throughout training. Across pretraining, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning, NoRA consistently accelerates convergence, improves performance and training stability, and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. These benefits require neither additional trainable parameters nor inference-time computation, making NoRA a simple and broadly applicable enhancement to LoRA.

</details>


### [177] [Rotational Equivariance in Machine Learning: A Comprehensive Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31045)
*Peter Lippmann,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于三维机器学习中旋转等变性的综合性教程，从几何与物理直觉出发，系统地介绍了几何深度学习、群论和表示论的基础工具（消息传递、群作用、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积、Clebsch-Gordan分解等），并综述了实现旋转等变性的主要策略（群卷积、内部张量表示、规范化方法）及其权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转对称性是三维机器学习中最重要的结构原则之一，在物理、材料科学和三维计算机视觉等应用中，模型预测不应依赖于任意的坐标系选择。然而，旋转等变性的数学工具（群论、表示论等）对许多从业者而言门槛较高，且相关思想常以不同的形式化语言表达，缺乏统一和系统的讲解，因此需要一个降低入门门槛的综合性教程。

Method: 本文采用教程式综述方法：首先从坐标无关性的物理与几何直觉出发，逐步构建几何深度学习所需的数学框架，包括欧几里得图上的消息传递、群作用与表示、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积和Clebsch-Gordan分解；随后详细综述三类主流的旋转等变深度学习方法——群卷积、内部张量表示和规范化（canonicalization）方法，并对每种方法进行优缺点分析。

Result: 本文系统性地统一了旋转等变领域中通常以不同形式化语言表达的多种思想，将底层数学与实际的模型设计相连接，并对三类主要等变架构策略进行了清晰的权衡比较，帮助从业者理解并选择合适的方法。

Conclusion: 该教程旨在通过连接数学理论与实际建模、统一不同形式语言、以及清晰讨论各方法的权衡，降低旋转等变机器学习的学习门槛，使从业者能够更有信心地在实际应用中采用和选择适当的旋转等变架构。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry is one of the most important structural principles in machine learning on 3D data. In applications ranging from physics and materials science to 3D computer vision, predictions should not depend on an arbitrary choice of coordinate frame. Rotational equivariance captures this requirement mathematically by enforcing that a rotation of the input induces a corresponding transformation of the model output. This tutorial provides a comprehensive introduction to rotational equivariance, starting from the physical and geometric intuition behind coordinate independence and building up the necessary machinery from geometric deep learning, group theory, and representation theory. We introduce message passing on Euclidean graphs, group actions and representations, spherical harmonics, Wigner matrices, tensor products, and Clebsch-Gordan decomposition, and explain how these ingredients give rise to modern equivariant architectures. We then survey the principal strategies for incorporating rotational equivariance in deep learning, including group convolutions, internal tensorial representations, and canonicalization-based methods, and discuss their practical strengths and limitations. The tutorial aims to lower the barrier to the subject by connecting the underlying mathematics to practical model design, by unifying ideas that are often expressed in different formal languages, and by helping practitioners choose among competing approaches through a clear discussion of their trade-offs.

</details>


### [178] [Does On-Policy Distillation Really Distill? From Noisy Teacher to Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31046)
*Yi Ding,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现同策略蒸馏(OPD)的教师监督存在显著噪声，且噪声随教师规模增大而增多，但学生策略对噪声不敏感。进一步分析揭示OPD的核心收益并非来自教师蒸馏，而是通过抑制低对数概率token实现的。基于此提出无监督方法OPSA，使用熵自适应负优势信号，在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41点（相对提升263%），Pass@32翻倍以上，并超越OPD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)的替代方案，提供密集的token级监督，但教师评分的学生生成轨迹本质上是教师的离策略数据，因此监督的可靠性及其对学生提升的真正来源尚不明确。作者希望厘清OPD训练中监督的性质及其实际作用机制。

Method: 首先定量分析OPD训练中的教师监督，检测到大量噪声（随教师规模增大而增多）。通过消融实验发现学生策略对噪声不敏感。进一步分析发现学习集中在低对数概率token上，单一固定负优势即可匹敌教师提供的优势。基于此提出OPSA（On-Policy Self-Adaptation），一种无需监督的方法，使用熵自适应负优势：对高熵位置分配更强学习信号以抑制尾部token，并在头部token间均匀重新分配概率质量。

Result: OPSA相比基线Qwen3-1.7B在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41点（相对提升263%），在三个基准上Pass@32均翻倍以上；相比OPD在AIME24上Avg@32高出16.77点。跨模型家族和任务的广泛实验验证了其有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: OPD的作用机制主要是通过抑制低对数概率token（即降低尾部token的概率）而非真正的知识蒸馏。这一发现催生了无需任何教师监督的OPSA方法，它在熵自适应负优势的驱动下取得了比OPD更优的性能，揭示了大规模语言模型通过自适应的熵调节即可实现有效推理能力的提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision as an alternative to the sparse outcome-level advantages of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, the teacher scores student-generated trajectories that are inherently off-policy for it, so the reliability of its supervision, and hence the source of the student's improvement, remains unclear. We quantitatively analyze teacher supervision during OPD training and find substantial noise whose prevalence increases with teacher scale. Surprisingly, the student policy is insensitive to such noise, converging to comparable performance regardless of whether noisy supervision is retained or removed. Does OPD distill at all? By analyzing what drives its gains, we find that learning concentrates on low log-probability tokens, and using a single fixed negative advantage matches the performance of teacher-provided ones. This suggests that OPD works largely by suppressing low log-probability tokens, which requires no teacher. These findings motivate On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA), a supervision-free method using entropy-adaptive negative advantages. It assigns stronger learning signals to high-entropy positions, suppressing tail tokens, and evenly redistributing probability mass among head tokens. Compared with the base \texttt{Qwen3-1.7B}, OPSA improves Avg@32 by 35.41 points on AIME24, corresponding to a 263\% relative gain, and more than doubles Pass@32 across all three benchmarks. It also outperforms OPD by 16.77 points in Avg@32 on AIME24. Extensive experiments and analyses across model families and tasks further demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [179] [Universal Transformers for Circuit Computations: Perfect Length Generalization in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31067)
*Takuya Ito,Ruchir Puri,Murray Campbell,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可证明正确的Transformer参数化方法（布尔代数任务仅需280个可学习参数），通过将算法任务概念化为电路模型嵌入Transformer中，利用深度位置编码、掩码硬注意力和线性注意力实现任意深度/长度的算法计算，在浅层训练后可实现精确的长度泛化，并在布尔表达式、模运算和ListOps等基准上达到100%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在组合泛化和长度泛化基准上持续失败，学习可泛化的算法计算仍然是一个挑战，现有方法难以在浅层训练后正确泛化到任意深度或长度的输入。

Method: 将算法任务建模为嵌入Transformer中的电路模型，实现单次前向传播的深度1电路归约；引入跟踪每个门深度位置编码，通过掩码硬注意力识别每个迭代中可求值的子表达式；采用线性注意力实现O(n)每迭代复杂度；结合自主停止准则，在深度d的问题上经d次迭代终止，总复杂度O(n·d)。

Result: 在深度1和深度2的浅层实例上训练即可恢复可解释的参数（参数"咔嗒"式就位），实现精确的长度泛化；证明该构造能完美求值任意长度的布尔表达式（通用符号计算），并在模算术和ListOps等其他常见长度泛化基准上实现100%的完美泛化准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究证明通过精心设计的Transformer架构（极少量参数+电路化概念化+深度跟踪的位置编码），神经网络可以实现可证明且可泛化的算法计算，为长度和深度泛化问题提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning generalizable algorithmic computations remains a challenge for neural networks, as reflected in persistent failures on compositional and length generalization benchmarks. We present a provably correct, transformer parameterization (with only 280 learnable parameters for Boolean algebra tasks) capable of learning and evaluating problems of any depth or length. We assume inputs are fully parenthesized, well-formed expressions. Our approach conceptualizes algorithmic tasks as circuit models embedded in transformers, enabling depth-1 circuit reduction in a single forward pass. To achieve depth generalization, we introduce a positional encoding that tracks each gate's depth within the circuit, enabling the model to identify evaluable subexpressions at each iteration via masked hard attention, with $O(n)$ per-iteration complexity via linear attention. Combined with an autonomous halting criterion, the model terminates after $d$ iterations for problems of depth $d$, yielding $O(n \cdot d)$ total complexity. We show that training on shallow problem instances (depth 1 and depth 2) effectively recovers interpretable parameters that {\em snap} into place, resulting in exact length generalization. Though we establish that our construction provably evaluates Boolean expressions -- a universal symbolic computation -- of arbitrary length perfectly, in other experiments we also demonstrate that our transformer variant can learn and generalize perfectly (100% accuracy) on other common length generalization benchmarks, including modular arithmetic and ListOps.

</details>


### [180] [A Model with No Head and Many Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31069)
*Nikita Koriagin,Yaroslav Aksenov,George Bredis,Gleb Gerasimov,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Soft Latent Thinking方法，在推理阶段用轻量投影器替代LM head，使推理过程在嵌入空间中连续进行而非离散token化，在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上提升pass@k且降低链式推理的每步计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在每一步推理时都需要通过巨大的词汇表头进行投影解码，这一操作计算开销大，且强迫所有推理过程都以离散token形式表达，限制了推理的连续性和效率。

Method: 在推理过程中使用轻量级投影器替代LM head，使得自回归滚动在嵌入空间中执行，推理步骤保持连续而非token化，从而避免每次解码的昂贵词汇表投影操作。

Result: 在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上的实验表明，该方法在所有k值下一致提升pass@k，同时降低链式推理的每步计算量，并在pass@32上达到所有soft-thinking方法中的最优表现。

Conclusion: 有效推理可以在连续空间中完成而不需要离散token生成，Soft Latent Thinking证明了这一点，同时兼顾了推理性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Large language models decode by projecting hidden states through a large vocabulary head at every step. This operation is computationally costly and forces all reasoning to be expressed in discrete tokens. We introduce Soft Latent Thinking, a method that replaces the LM head during reasoning with a lightweight projector, enabling autoregressive rollout in embedding space where reasoning steps remain continuous rather than tokenized. Experiments on DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B and LLaMA-3.2-3B show that Soft Latent Thinking consistently improves pass@k across all k while reducing per-step compute during chain-of-thought. Our method achieves the highest pass@32 among all soft-thinking approaches, demonstrating that effective reasoning can be carried out in continuous space without discrete token generation.

</details>


### [181] [Sycophantic Agreement Transfers with Neutral Data via Contrastive Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31079)
*Camila Blank,Zhuofan Ying,Christopher Potts,Peter Hase,Jing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现谄媚性附和（sycophantic agreement）可以作为广泛使用的对比偏好优化目标（如DPO）的意外后果而产生。通过OLMo 3后训练流程，研究发现教师模型的谄媚率与最终学生模型的谄媚率之间存在强相关，且该效应在7种偏好优化目标中普遍存在。更重要的是，谄媚信号分散在整个训练数据集中而非集中在少量样本中，使得基于数据归因的过滤方法难以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谄媚性附和是语言模型对齐中的已知失败模式，即模型过度附和用户而有损事实准确性。然而，目前对该行为如何从模型训练中涌现的理解十分有限。理解这一机制对于改进对齐训练方法、防止有害行为泛化至关重要。

Method: 使用OLMo 3后训练流程，覆盖三个模型族的多对教师模型。通过比较教师模型谄媚性附和率的对数比值与学生模型谄媚率之间的相关性来分析转移效应。在7种偏好优化目标（包括DPO）上进行验证。此外，通过探针式数据归因和logit线性选择方法分析偏好数据，检查谄媚信号在数据集中的分布特征（集中vs扩散）。

Result: 研究发现教师模型谄媚率与最终学生模型谄媚率之间存在强相关，且该效应在DPO及其他6种偏好优化目标中均存在。谄媚信号并非集中在稀疏样本中而是弥散于整个数据集，每个个体样本看似中立，缺乏显式谄媚实例。基于探针的数据归因或logit线性选择的过滤方法无法在移除大量数据的情况下缓解谄媚问题。

Conclusion: 教师模型与对齐训练目标之间的交互可能以意想不到的方式产生不良行为（如谄媚性附和）的泛化。这一发现表明当前偏好优化方法存在潜在的设计缺陷，谄媚信号在数据中分散分布的特性使得简单过滤策略失效，需要重新思考数据生成与对齐目标的设计。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement refers to a behavior in which language models excessively affirm the user, often at the cost of factual accuracy. Although sycophantic agreement is a well-known failure of model alignment, there is limited understanding of how it emerges from model training. In this work, we demonstrate that sycophantic agreement can emerge as an unintended consequence of widely used contrastive preference optimization objectives. Using the OLMo 3 post-training pipeline, we show that, for various pairs of teacher models across three families, there is a strong correlation between the log-ratio of the teacher model sycophantic agreement rates and the resulting student model sycophantic agreement rate. We further demonstrate that this unintended transfer is not limited to DPO but also occurs across 6 other preference optimization objectives. To understand whether this effect can be attributed to particular training examples, we analyze the preference data and find that the sycophancy signal is diffused across the entire dataset rather than concentrated in a sparse set of examples: each example appears neutral, i.e., there are no explicit instances of sycophantic agreement, and filtering based on probe-based data attribution or logit-linear selection fails to mitigate sycophancy without removing a large portion of the dataset. Overall, our findings suggest that the teacher models used to generate preference data can interact with alignment training objectives in unexpected ways, generalizing to undesirable and potentially harmful behaviors like sycophantic agreement.

</details>


### [182] [Stress-Testing Efficient Responsible-AI Evaluation: When Compute Savings Change Benchmark Conclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31108)
*Ahmed El Kady,Aravind Narayanan,Rehana Noorani,Yani Ioannou,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了高效评估方法（批处理、量化、基准缩减及其组合）对负责任AI基准测试结论稳健性的影响，发现不同加速手段在保持准确率的同时，对偏差严重度、推理质量、子群行为等结论的稳定性产生不同影响，并提出应将高效评估视为一种需要验证其效度的"测量干预"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效评估方法（如量化、批处理、基准缩减）能降低评估成本，但很少被检验这些加速手段是否改变了基准测试所支撑的结论。本文旨在压力测试结论稳健性，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 在BBQ和BBQ-V基准上评估三种稠密和混合专家模型，设置七种条件，涵盖批处理、量化（INT8/INT4）、基准缩减及其组合。比较准确率、偏差严重度和发生率、推理质量、子群行为、子集成员稳定性、运行时间和GPU能耗，对照完整基准BF16基线。

Result: 更大的批处理在六个模型-数据集设置中有五个降低能耗，准确率偏差在0.35个百分点以内且子群变化较小；INT8基本保持质量但能耗为基线的1.79-4.26倍；INT4导致更大且依赖模型与上下文的改变；缩减基准节能最稳定，但极小规模子集对内容保留高度敏感。

Conclusion: 高效评估应被视为一种测量干预，其效度必须针对基准所支撑的每类结论逐一验证，不能仅以聚合准确率的保持作为充分条件。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation changes the protocol used to support claims about model behavior, yet it is rarely tested whether those claims remain stable after the evaluation itself is made cheaper. We stress-test conclusion robustness in responsible-AI benchmarking by evaluating three dense and mixture-of-experts models on BBQ and BBQ-V under seven conditions spanning batching, quantization, benchmark reduction, and their combinations. Rather than treating preserved aggregate accuracy as sufficient, we compare accuracy, bias severity and prevalence, reasoning quality, subgroup behavior, subset-membership stability, runtime, and measured GPU energy against a full-benchmark BF16 baseline.
