<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作记忆是异质的，不同类型的记忆对象具有不同的语义角色、尺寸、保留周期和表征方式。现有研究虽已开始探索考虑这种异质性的记忆管理机制，但仍缺乏对语义异质性如何塑造编码智能体工作记忆管理与评估的系统性研究。

Method: 基于55条归档的编码智能体轨迹数据，分析语义不同类型工作记忆对象的保留和压缩行为差异；提出两种语义感知策略：对象感知压缩策略（object-aware compression policy）和基于检索的策略（retrieval-based policy）；在真实系统中进行重放实验以揭示名义token预算未能捕获的服务限制。

Result: 发现语义不同的工作记忆对象表现出不同的保留和压缩行为；校准增益不一定迁移到保留任务；等量token预算不意味着等量的上下文传递或管理成本；真实系统重放揭示了名义预算未反映的服务限制。

Conclusion: 语义结构对智能体工作记忆管理至关重要，评估记忆管理策略不能仅看名义token预算，应至少从四个层面考量：存储状态（stored state）、传递上下文（delivered context）、管理工作（management work）和任务/过程结果（task or process outcome）。

Abstract: Agent working memory is heterogeneous. Objects such as instructions, artifacts, tool outputs, and agent-generated state play different semantic roles and exhibit different size, retention, and representation profiles. Recent work has begun to explore memory-management mechanisms that account for such heterogeneity. This work focuses on semantic heterogeneity and studies how it should shape the management and evaluation of working memory in coding agents. Across 55 archived coding-agent trajectories, we find that semantically different working-memory objects exhibit distinct retention and compression behavior. This heterogeneity motivates semantically informed memory management. We study two semantically informed strategies: an object-aware compression policy and a retrieval-based policy. Their evaluation shows that calibration gains may not transfer to held-out tasks, and that equal token budgets do not imply equal delivered context or management cost. A real-system replay further exposes serving limits that nominal budgets alone do not capture. Together, these results show why semantic structure matters for agent working memory and why evaluating memory-management strategies requires more than a nominal token budget. We organize these lessons into four levels: stored state, delivered context, management work, and task or process outcome.

</details>


### [383] [Wrong Prediction, Right Answer: Recovering Evidence from Collapsed LLM Sequence Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31068)
*Qiyao Yan,Chenpeng Wang,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，大语言模型在推理任务上的失败常被归因于能力缺失，但实际上可能是输出阶段的"表达瓶颈"而非内部推理能力不足。通过仅拟合两个参数、用最少25个无标签样例的简单加性校正，就能在多个基准上恢复9-34个准确率点，且跨模型泛化。这表明许多零样本推理缺陷实际上是表达失败掩盖了完好的内部逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，当一个LLM在推理任务上失败时，研究者默认其缺乏底层推理能力。但作者指出这混淆了"真正缺乏推理能力"与"输出阶段的后期瓶颈"两种截然不同的情况。他们观察到隐藏状态的探测器能正确解码答案，即使原生序列评分因结构偏差而完全崩溃，这揭示了需要重新审视推理失败归因的研究动机。

Method: 首先通过隐藏状态探测证明存在"读出差距"（readout gap）：即隐藏状态已包含正确推理但序列输出失败。然后设计诊断协议，引入一种最小化的、无需目标标签的加性校正方法，仅拟合两个参数，在最少25个无标签样例上进行校正。随后验证该方法在多个模型（Qwen3.5、OLMo-2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B）上的跨模型迁移效果，并与保排列置换基线进行对比。

Result: 仅拟合两个参数、使用25个无标签样例即可恢复9-34个准确率点；该方法成功迁移到OLMo-2-1B和Llama-3.1-8B；恢复出的决策在词汇重叠无法解决的困难样本上仍然有效，且显著优于保计数的排列基线。

Conclusion: 许多表面上的零样本推理缺陷本质上是表达失败（expression failure），掩盖了完整无损的内部逻辑。这一发现强烈建议对基准评估结果应采用更谨慎、更狭窄的解释，不能简单将输出失败等同于能力缺失。

Abstract: When a large language model fails a reasoning task, it is often assumed to lack the underlying capability. However, this conflates a genuine absence of reasoning with a late-stage output bottleneck. We observe a consistent readout gap across diverse reasoning benchmarks: hidden-state probes successfully decode correct answers even when native sequence scoring completely collapses due to structural biases. To test whether instance-specific logic survives this collapse, we introduce a diagnostic protocol using a minimal, target-label-free additive correction. Fitting just two parameters on as few as 25 unlabeled examples recovers 9--34 accuracy points for Qwen3.5 models, transferring successfully to OLMo-2-1B and Llama-3.1-8B. Crucially, these recovered decisions persist on hard instances unresolved by simple lexical overlap and significantly exceed count-preserving permutation baselines. Our results show that many apparent zero-shot reasoning deficits are expression failures masking intact internal logic, urging a narrower interpretation of benchmark evaluations.

</details>


### [384] [Scaling Large Reasoning Models beyond Human Supervision: A Path toward Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31075)
*Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu,Yuhan Liu,Hengyu Liu,Yonggang Zhang,Kainan Cao,Zizhuo Zhang,Chenxin Li,Ruibin Yuan,Jiahao Pan,Jiankai Sun,Zhenyuan Zhang,Yibo Li,Yunlong Lin,Jing Xiong,Sida Lin,Bo Han,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）在人类监督逐渐退出学习循环后如何继续改进的问题，提出了从L0到L5的五级爬升框架，分别从奖励轴和经验轴两个维度分析学习过程，并围绕策略能力、反馈保真度和经验质量三个互补对象进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已在数学和代码等可自动检查结果的领域显著提升推理能力，但将其扩展到开放性和智能体任务仍面临困难：可靠的奖励难以获取，直接的人类监督无法跟上模型生成经验的规模和复杂度。因此需要研究LRMs在人类监督减少后如何持续自我改进。

Method: 提出双维分析框架：奖励轴追溯从逐实例人工判断到可复用验证器乃至无需人类反馈的奖励的演进；经验轴考察从人工策划的任务和环境到自生成课程、构建环境和自主共同进化的进展。通过五级阶梯L0-L4连接两轴，识别学习过程中仍处人类控制的部分。

Result: 揭示了日益自主的奖励和经验生成带来的风险，包括奖励欺骗、反馈漂移、课程崩溃和环境错误，并建立了围绕策略能力、反馈保真度和经验质量三个对象进行评测的体系。

Conclusion: 该分析为当前超越人类监督扩展LRMs的方法提供了结构化梳理，明确了发展自我维持学习系统以迈向超智能的过程中所面临的开放问题，并维护GitHub仓库持续追踪最新进展。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have shown that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can substantially improve reasoning in mathematics and code, where outcomes can be checked automatically. Extending this progress to open-ended and agentic tasks remains difficult because reliable rewards are harder to obtain and direct human supervision cannot keep pace with the scale and complexity of model-generated experience. This paper studies how LRMs can continue to improve as human supervision gradually recedes from the learning loop. We examine two connected dimensions of this problem. The reward axis traces the development from per-instance human judgments to reusable verifiers and rewards that operate even without human feedback. The experience axis examines how learning can progress from human-curated tasks and environments toward self-generated curricula, constructed environments, and autonomous co-evolution. We connect these dimensions through a five-level ladder from L0 to L4 that identifies which parts of the learning process remain under continued human control. Our analysis further highlights the risks introduced by increasingly autonomous rewards and experience generation, including reward hacking, feedback drift, curriculum collapse, and environment errors. Consequently, we also provide the evaluation around three complementary objects: policy capability, feedback fidelity, and experience quality. This analysis provides a structured account of current approaches to scaling LRMs beyond human supervision and the open problems involved in developing self-sustaining learning systems toward superintelligence. Furthermore, we maintain a continuously updated \href{https://github.com/visitworld123/Awesome-Scaling-LRM-Beyond-Human-Supervision}{GitHub repository} to track the latest advances.

</details>


### [385] [Reconciling Process Supervision with Outcome-Based Credit in Agentic Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31077)
*Jingxiao Yang,Wangjie Gan,Yingxuan Zhuang,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TASPO通过将特权监督转化为基于结果的动作级信用分配，弥补了细粒度监督与细粒度信用之间的鸿沟，在三个智能体基准上相比GRPO提升10.6%并具有更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习为语言模型智能体提供验证反馈，但将轨迹级优势均匀分配给所有决策，在长程交互中产生粗粒度信用分配。虽然基于策略的自蒸馏可以通过训练时特权信息提供更细粒度的监督，但细粒度监督并不等于细粒度信用——特权信息引起的似然变化描述了额外信息如何改变策略偏好，却无法直接决定可执行动作应如何继承验证后的任务结果，形成了监督-信用差距。

Method: TASPO将特权监督转化为基于结果的动作信用：1)从验证成功的经验中构建适用于决策的特权信息；2)在可执行动作层面聚合特权信息引起的似然偏移；3)将相对动作支持转化为原始轨迹优势上正的、有界的、保均值的权重。验证结果决定更新方向和平均尺度，特权信息仅用于在动作间重新分配信用。

Result: 在三个智能体基准上，TASPO相比GRPO提升10.6%，对未见任务具有更好的泛化能力。进一步分析表明TASPO减少了监督失配，动作级信用分配稳定了策略优化过程。

Conclusion: TASPO有效弥合了监督与信用之间的差距，通过将特权监督转化为基于结果的动作级信用分配，既保留了验证结果的语义强度，又获得了细粒度的信用分配，为语言模型智能体提供了另一种有前景的视角。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides verified feedback for language-model agents, but assigns trajectory-level advantage uniformly to all decisions, yielding coarse credit over long-horizon interactions. On-policy self-distillation offers finer supervision by re-evaluating sampled behavior with privileged information (PI) available only during training. However, fine-grained supervision is not necessarily fine-grained credit: PI-induced likelihood changes describe how additional information alters policy preference, but do not directly determine how an executable action should inherit the verified task outcome. This creates a supervision-credit gap. Privileged signals may be irrelevant to the current interaction state, operate at a token granularity misaligned with executable decisions, and lack the outcome semantics required for reinforcement. We introduce TASPO, which converts privileged supervision into outcome-grounded action credit. TASPO constructs decision-applicable PI from verified successful experience, aggregates PI-induced likelihood shifts at the executable-action level, and converts relative action support into positive, bounded, mean-preserving weights on the original trajectory advantage. Thus, the verified outcome determines the update direction and average scale, while PI only redistributes credit across actions. Across three agentic benchmarks, TASPO improves over GRPO by 10.6\% and generalizes better to unseen tasks. Further analysis indicates that TASPO reduces supervision mismatch and that action-level assignment stabilizes the policy optimization process. These findings offer the community another interesting perspective.

</details>


### [386] [Token-Efficient Data Reasoning Agents via Adaptive Structuring of Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31082)
*Milad Rezaei Hajidehi,Qitong Wang,Stratos Idreos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"智能体数据破解"（Agentic Data Cracking）方法：在LLM智能体推理过程中，作为推理的副产品自适应且投机性地将非结构化数据转化为结构化存储，使后续查询无需重新打开文档即可快速回答，在保持准确率的同时大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI的核心挑战是在非结构化数据上部署LLM智能体回答复杂问题，但当前每次查询都需要反复打开大型文档、消耗高达百万级token，成本高昂。虽然预先结构化数据可以大幅降低推理成本（如FanOutQA上28倍差距），但预先结构化所有内容并不可行，因为文档包含的潜在结构远超实际工作负载所需，且有用的结构和文档在查询到来前是未知的。

Method: 提出"智能体数据破解"方法：结构化的过程是自适应的（由观察到的查询决定何时结构化及关注什么）且投机的（超越当前问题）。当智能体打开文档回答问题时，一个破解子代理（cracking sub-agent）从已加载的上下文中分叉出来，以边际成本提取接地结构（grounded structure），服务于未来的相关查询。随着时间推移，越来越多的查询完全由结构化数据覆盖，无需打开文档即可回答。

Result: 在FanOutQA基准上，仅在每个测试问题扩展一个相关问题的情况下，破解方法将成本降低53%，同时保持准确性。推理理想预结构化存储比非结构化推理便宜28倍，且问题越分散多文档，差距呈数量级扩大。

Conclusion: 智能体数据破解是面向非结构化数据的下一代智能体推理数据基础设施的第一步：在模型之下构建一个共享基座，让推理过程中已付出代价获取的知识得以积累，使智能体推理的成本接近RAG水平。

Abstract: Valuable data remains embedded in unstructured sources: web pages, reports, contracts, filings, earnings calls, and PDFs. The big bet in enterprise AI is deploying LLM agents that reason over this data to answer complex questions for every knowledge worker. Agents can do this today, but at prohibitive cost. Each question repeatedly opens large documents to recover scattered evidence, consuming up to a million tokens. However, if the data were already structured, the same question would reduce to a cheap database lookup. For example, on FanOutQA benchmark, reasoning over an ideal pre-structured store is 28X cheaper, and the gap grows to orders of magnitude as questions fan out over more documents. Yet structuring everything in advance is not viable: documents hold vastly more possible structure than any workload will use, and the useful structure and documents are unknown until queries arrive. We propose agentic data cracking, a method that structures unstructured data adaptively and speculatively as a byproduct of reasoning itself. Structuring is adaptive because observed queries decide when it happens and what matters, and speculative because it goes beyond the current question. Whenever the agent opens a document to answer, a cracking sub-agent forks from the already-loaded context at marginal cost and extracts grounded structure likely to serve related future queries. Over time, an increasing share of queries is fully covered by structured data and answered without opening a document, keeping agentic accuracy at close to RAG cost. On FanOutQA, extended with merely one related question per test question, cracking cuts cost by 53% while preserving accuracy. Agentic data cracking is a first step toward next-generation data infrastructure for agentic reasoning over unstructured data: a shared substrate beneath the model where knowledge that reasoning already paid to uncover accumulates.

</details>


### [387] [Cross-Regional Grapevine Cold Hardiness Prediction via Learned Multimodal Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31097)
*William Solow,Paola Pesantez-Cabrera,Markus Keller,Lav Khot,Sandhya Saisubramanian,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迁移的木本植物抗寒性预测框架，通过学习区域特定的潜在嵌入表示，结合文本描述和有限历史观测，实现了对新区域和新品种的零样本与少样本迁移预测，在北美六个区域的数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗寒性预测模型（生物物理、混合、深度学习）在局部数据上精度高但具有场地特异性，难以推广到新区域和品种；抗寒性数据稀缺且缺乏原则性的迁移方法，限制了在数据匮乏地区的实际应用。

Method: 提出一个抗寒性预测框架，通过学习可迁移的潜在表征来捕捉区域特异性变异，具体做法包括：(1) 利用可学习的嵌入（learned embeddings）编码区域差异；(2) 通过品种和种植区域的文本描述推断未见区域的嵌入（零样本迁移）；(3) 利用有限的历史观测推断嵌入（少样本迁移）。

Result: 在北美六个区域的数据集上进行实验，所提方法在预测准确性和向数据匮乏区域的迁移效果上均显著优于现有的最先进抗寒性预测方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了抗寒性预测的跨区域迁移问题，通过潜在表征和嵌入推断机制，显著提升了在数据稀缺新区域的预测能力和实际可用性。

Abstract: Accurate daily predictions of cold hardiness in woody plants are critical in regions where freezing temperatures can damage dormant buds and reduce seasonal yield. Existing biophysical, hybrid, and deep learning models have shown high predictive accuracy when trained on local data but remain largely site-specific. The limited availability of cold hardiness data, coupled with the lack of principled methods for transferring cold hardiness predictions to new regions and cultivars, has limited the broader adoption and practical utility of these approaches, particularly in data-scarce regions. To address these limitations, we propose a cold hardiness prediction framework that learns a transferable latent representation by capturing region-specific variation through learned embeddings. To enable prediction in previously unseen regions, we infer embeddings from (1) text descriptions of the cultivar and growing region, and (2) limited historical observations, supporting both zero-shot and few-shot transfer. Experiments on datasets from six regions across North America demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cold hardiness prediction methods, yielding more accurate predictions and substantially improving transfer to data-scarce regions.

</details>


### [388] [BLOOM-WILT: Logit Tilting for Behaviour Elicitation in Automated LLM Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31105)
*Adrians Skapars,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLOOM-WILT，一个无需训练成本的完整审计流水线，通过自动审计器在多轮对话中建模并自适应地调整目标模型的解码分布，高效诱发部署模型中罕见的自然多轮行为实例。在4个目标模型和8种行为上，WILT在32个设置中的30个击败基线审计器，并推翻了先前模型安全排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的语言模型常出现评测中几乎无法触发的行为，因为部署产生的交互量远超任何评估能模拟的数量级。自动化审计器虽然测试成本低且灵活，但缺乏优化压力导致采样效率低下，难以高效诱发罕见行为。

Method: WILT从输入和输出两侧协同优化：(1) 输入侧，审计器模型跨轮次修正对话策略，从先前评分的交互中学习；(2) 输出侧，利用目标模型自身的分布，以诱发提示为条件自适应地对解码重新加权，使行为相关生成在未提示时同样概率的候选中被优先采样。

Result: 在4个目标模型和8种行为的评估中，WILT在32个设置中的30个击败基线审计器，并推翻之前的模型安全排名。在诱发Qwen3.5-4B的自我伤害鼓励行为时，WILT将平均行为出现率从51%提升至100%，在计算量匹配的情况下击败所有移植到同一流水线中的诱发方法，且不将输出概率推低至基线以下。

Conclusion: WILT通过结合输入侧策略学习和输出侧自适应解码重加权，实现了高效、无训练成本、仅需目标模型next-token分布的自动化审计，显著提升罕见行为诱发效率并挑战了既有的模型安全评估结论。

Abstract: Users of a deployed language model routinely encounter behaviours that testing almost never surfaces, since deployment puts the model through orders of magnitude more interactions than any evaluation can simulate. Automated auditors make testing cheap to scale and flexible enough to cover almost any specified behaviour, yet their lack of optimisation pressure makes them sample-inefficient. To address this shortcoming, we introduce BLOOM-WILT, a full auditing pipeline that elicits natural multi-turn instances of rare behaviours, without training cost or access beyond the target's next-token distribution. On the input side, WILT's auditor model revises its conversational strategy across rounds, learning from previous scored interactions. On the output side, WILT adaptively reweights the target's decoding using the model's own distribution conditioned on an elicitation prompt, so that behaviour-relevant generations are sampled ahead of others it finds equally probable when unprompted. We evaluate WILT across 4 target models and 8 behaviours, where it beats the baseline auditor in 30 of the 32 settings and overturns the previous model safety rankings. WILT raises average behaviour presence from 51% to 100% when eliciting self-harm encouragement from Qwen3.5-4B, beating every elicitation method we port into the same pipeline at matched compute, without pushing output probability below the baseline's.

</details>


### [389] [When Does Bigger Help? A Controlled Study of LLM Scale for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31118)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模语言模型（LLM）规模对本体学习（OL）性能的影响，通过对Qwen3.5和Qwen3.6系列的13个稠密和混合专家（MoE）变体以及专有的GPT版本进行受控评估，发现模型参数规模对性能的影响既非单调也非跨任务均匀，模型架构和血统有时比名义参数数量更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规模对本体学习性能的影响尚未得到充分刻画，现有研究缺乏系统的受控评估，难以为本体工程的模型选择提供可靠的实证指导。

Method: 使用OntoLearner检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，对13个模型（涵盖Qwen3.5和Qwen3.6系列的稠密和MoE变体，以及专有GPT版本）进行受控评估。所有模型采用相同的嵌入模型、检索配置、提示模板、解码设置、数据集和评估指标，在四个生物医学和材料科学与工程本体上评估词项类型标注、分类法发现和非分类关系抽取三个任务。

Result: 在稠密Qwen3.5系列中，参数增加主要提升精确率而非召回率，最大增益出现在9B到27B之间。规模效应既非单调也非跨任务均匀：稠密27B模型在词项类型标注上优于更大的稀疏模型，而更大的MoE模型在分类法发现上取得最强的开放权重结果。非分类关系抽取在各规模下仍具挑战性，尤其在材料数据科学本体上。匹配的Qwen变体与专有GPT版本之间的性能差异表明架构和模型血统可超越名义参数数量。

Conclusion: 模型规模本身不足以作为本体学习的模型选择标准，研究为可复现的LLM辅助本体工程提供了实证指导。

Abstract: The effect of Large Language Model (LLM) scale on ontology learning (OL) performance remains insufficiently characterized. We present a controlled evaluation of 13 models spanning dense and Mixture-of-Experts variants from the Qwen3.5 and Qwen3.6 lineages, together with proprietary GPT release variants, using the OntoLearner retrieval-augmented generation pipeline. All models are evaluated with the same embedding model, retrieval configuration, prompt templates, decoding settings, datasets, and metrics on term typing, taxonomy discovery, and non-taxonomic relationship extraction across four biomedical and materials science and engineering ontologies. Within the dense Qwen3.5 lineage, increasing parameter count primarily improves precision rather than recall, with the largest gains occurring between 9B and 27B parameters. However, the effect of scale is neither monotonic nor uniform across tasks and domains. Dense 27B models outperform substantially larger sparse models on term typing, whereas larger Mixture-of-Experts models achieve the strongest open-weight results on taxonomy discovery. Non-taxonomic relationship extraction remains difficult across model scales, particularly for the Materials Data Science ontology. Performance differences across matched Qwen variants and proprietary GPT releases further indicate that architecture and model lineage can outweigh nominal parameter count. These findings show that model size alone is an insufficient selection criterion for OL and provide empirical guidance for reproducible LLM-assisted ontology engineering.

</details>


### [390] [OntoAligner-Ensemble: Voting-Based Fusion across Heterogeneous Ontology Alignment Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31137)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Sören Auer,Peio Popov,Mahsa Sanaei,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OntoAligner-Ensemble，一个模块化且与aligner无关的本体对齐集成框架，通过投票式融合策略和后融合选择策略两阶段过程整合候选对应关系，实验表明集成融合在不同领域任务中稳定提升精确率与召回率的平衡，且异构跨范式集成通常提升精确率，同质LLM集成更常获得更高的整体F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体对齐已从词汇和结构对齐器演进到知识图谱嵌入（KGE）模型和基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法。虽然现代OA框架提供了部署异构对齐器的统一生态系统，但系统性地调和这些互补且有时相互冲突的预测结果的机制仍然相对未被充分探索。

Method: 提出OntoAligner-Ensemble框架，采用两阶段可配置流程：(1) 基于投票的融合策略，(2) 后融合选择策略。框架与对齐器无关，支持OntoAligner中任何能产生候选对应关系的对齐器。实例化时采用了轻量字符串对齐器、KGE对齐器以及由开放权重和API-based LLM驱动的检索增强生成对齐器。

Result: 在来自五个OAEI轨道、涵盖生物医学到超越等价关系的八个基准任务上评估了单个对齐器和集成配置。结果显示集成融合持续改善精确率与召回率之间的平衡，并经常在多样化领域超越独立对齐器。分析还发现异构跨范式集成通常提升精确率，而同质LLM集成更常获得更高的总体F1分数。

Conclusion: 系统化集成学习为本体对齐提供了一种稳健且可复现的策略，同时为不同对齐场景下选择集成构成提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Ontology alignment (OA) has evolved through several methodological paradigms, ranging from lexical and structural aligners to knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models and, more recently, Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches. Although modern OA frameworks provide unified ecosystems for deploying these heterogeneous aligners, mechanisms for systematically reconciling their complementary and sometimes conflicting predictions remain relatively underexplored. We present OntoAligner-Ensemble, a modular and aligner-agnostic framework that combines candidate correspondences through a configurable two-stage process comprising voting-based fusion strategies followed by post-fusion selection policies. The framework supports any aligner implemented within OntoAligner that produces candidate correspondences, enabling diverse alignment paradigms to be integrated through a unified decision process. To demonstrate its effectiveness, we instantiate the framework using representative lightweight string-aligner, KGE-based, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation aligners powered by both open-weight and API-based LLMs. We evaluate individual aligners and ensemble configurations across eight benchmark tasks from five OAEI tracks spanning biomedical to beyond-equivalence. The results show that ensemble fusion consistently improves the balance between precision and recall and frequently outperforms standalone aligners across diverse domains. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that ensemble composition directly affects the precision-recall trade-off: heterogeneous cross-paradigm ensembles generally improve precision, whereas homogeneous LLM ensembles more often achieve higher overall F1-scores. These findings demonstrate that systematic ensemble learning offers a robust and reproducible strategy for OA while providing practical guidance for selecting ensemble compositions under different alignment scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [391] [MERIT: Mitigating Exposure Bias in Generative XMC for User-Interest Propensity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28931)
*Abhinav Mahajan,Arindam Sarkar,Prakash Mandayam Comar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT框架，用于大规模电商平台中用户兴趣类别的匹配（用户-兴趣双向检索）。针对自回归语言模型在教师强制微调下的暴露偏差问题，MERIT采用排列不变的多目标损失（混合金标签和挖掘的难负样本），使生成器暴露于错误前缀中，同时保持教师强制的训练效率。在25万+兴趣类别的专有电商数据集上，MERIT将全局召回率提升至少11.9%，平均Hit@k提升6.1%，生产A/B测试中用户转化率提升0.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模电商平台中，将用户匹配到兴趣类别面临标签空间持续演化、用户兴趣信号稀疏且长尾分布等挑战。自回归语言模型依靠世界知识和语义先验可泛化到极端标签空间，但在教师强制微调下，推理时预测会进入上下文成为后续输出的条件，早期错误会引导后续输出偏向共现标签，导致过度生成相关标签而漏掉真正不相关的兴趣——即暴露偏差问题。

Method: 提出MERIT框架，核心是自纠正目标（self-correction objective）：通过排列不变的多样目标损失，将金标签与挖掘的难负标签混合打乱，使生成器在训练中暴露于错误前缀，同时保持教师强制训练的效率。训练目标将监督集中于分类位置，产生与倾向对齐的隐藏状态，用于训练轻量级打分器，支持双向检索（用户→兴趣和兴趣→用户）。

Result: 在包含25万+兴趣类别的专有电商数据集上，MERIT的全局召回率（global recall）至少提升11.9%，平均Hit@k提升6.1%；在生产环境的A/B测试中，用户转化率提升0.26%。

Conclusion: MERIT通过暴露偏差缓解机制有效提升了生成式方法在大规模动态标签空间下用户兴趣倾向建模的性能，不仅在离线指标上显著优于基线，也在生产环境中带来了可量化的业务转化增益，验证了其在实际电商场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Matching users to interest categories at scale is central to personalized shopping, but the task is challenging in large e-commerce platforms, where label spaces continually evolve and user-interest signals are sparse and long-tailed. Autoregressive language models are appealing because their world knowledge and semantic priors over descriptors generalize across extreme label spaces and accommodate multiple valid label assignments. Yet under teacher-forced fine-tuning, inference-time predictions become part of the conditioning context: early errors steer later outputs toward co-occurring labels, over-generating near-correlates and missing unrelated true interests. We present MERIT, a framework for user-interest propensity modeling that mitigates this exposure bias through a self-correction objective. A permutation-invariant multi-target loss over shuffled mixtures of gold and mined hard-negative labels exposes the generator to erroneous prefixes while preserving the efficiency of teacher-forced training. This training objective concentrates supervision at classification positions, yielding propensity-aligned hidden states powering a lightweight scorer for bidirectional retrieval (interests for users and users for interests). On a proprietary e-commerce dataset with 250k+ interest categories, MERIT improves global recall by at least 11.9% and average Hit@k by 6.1%. In production A/B tests, it achieves +0.26% gain in user conversion.

</details>


### [392] [Book Readership During Movie Releases: An Exploratory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29019)
*Sushobhan Parajuli,Vittoria Vineis,Samira Vaez Barenji,Michael D. Ekstrand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了外部事件（如电影改编上映）对推荐系统中物品相关性的暂时性影响，利用Goodreads数据和电影上映日期匹配，发现电影改编书籍在电影上映月份出现明显的阅读量激增，并评估了现有推荐模型在此期间的排序表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部事件（如电影改编上映）会暂时改变推荐系统中物品的相关性，但历史交互数据无法提前捕捉这些变化。理解这一现象对改进推荐系统在事件驱动场景下的表现具有重要意义。

Method: 使用大规模Goodreads数据集与电影上映日期进行匹配，分析书籍在电影上映前后的阅读量变化，并使用现有推荐模型评估它们对电影改编书籍在上映日期前后的排名表现。

Result: 发现电影改编书籍在电影上映月份出现明显的阅读量激增，且现有推荐模型在此时段对这类书籍的排名表现存在不足。

Conclusion: 外部事件驱动的相关性变化是推荐系统面临的挑战，现有模型未能充分适应这种暂时性的用户兴趣转移，未来需要设计能够捕捉事件驱动兴趣变化的推荐方法。

Abstract: Exogenous events can temporarily change the relevance of items in recommender systems, but these shifts are often not visible in historical interaction data until after users have already responded. In book recommendation, movie adaptations provide a clear example of such events: the release of a movie based on a book can temporarily increase attention to the source text and change its relevance for some readers. We examine this phenomenon using a large Goodreads dataset matched to movie release dates. We find a clear spike in readership around the release month, and then we evaluate existing recommendation models to understand how they rank movie-adapted books around the movie release date.

</details>


### [393] [TAAL: Mitigating Early Beam Pruning in Generative Recommendation via Temporal Autoregressive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29179)
*Lianjie Li,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu,Hongliang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出时间自回归对齐（TAAL）方法，解决生成式推荐中标准下一词预测忽略序列多模态转移、导致真实语义ID（SID）在早期束搜索分支中被不可逆剪枝的问题。通过训练时联合(c1,c2)软目标对齐早期前缀分布，推理时使用点互信息（PMI）校准候选分数，TAAL在三个公开基准上显著提升NDCG@10和完整SID存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准下一词预测不显式覆盖交互序列中的多模态转移；在三个基准上发现91.9%-96.6%的检索失败发生在解码前两步，说明早期束搜索分支的错误剪枝是主要瓶颈。

Method: 1) 训练阶段：构建联合(c1,c2)软目标，利用历史转移信息，通过前向KL散度对齐早期前缀分布。2) 推理阶段：使用点互信息（PMI）校准候选分数，降低全局高频前缀的影响。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Instruments和Yelp上，NDCG@10分别提升39.5%、6.7%和28.6%；完整SID存活率提升3.9%-16.6%。束宽越窄提升越明显，B=5时相对存活率增益达39.4%。

Conclusion: TAAL通过针对早期解码前缀的专门设计，有效缓解了生成式推荐中早期束搜索错误剪枝问题，验证了时间维度的多模态转移对齐对生成式推荐的重要性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation encodes items as hierarchical semantic identifiers (SIDs) and retrieves the next item through autoregressive decoding. Standard next-token prediction, however, does not explicitly cover the multimodal transitions present in interaction sequences, leaving the ground-truth SID vulnerable to irreversible pruning at early beam-search branches. Across three public benchmarks, we find that 91.9\%--96.6\% of retrieval failures occur within the first two decoding steps. We therefore propose Temporal Autoregressive Alignment (TAAL). During training, TAAL constructs a joint $(c_1,c_2)$ soft target from historical transitions and aligns the early-prefix distribution with a forward KL objective. During inference, it calibrates candidate scores with pointwise mutual information (PMI) to reduce the influence of globally frequent prefixes. On Amazon Beauty, Instruments, and Yelp, TAAL improves NDCG@10 over the standard baseline by 39.5\%, 6.7\%, and 28.6\%, respectively, while increasing full-SID survival by 3.9\%--16.6\%. Beam-width analysis further shows that the relative survival gain grows as the beam narrows, reaching 39.4\% at $B=5$.

</details>


### [394] [Database-Augmented RAG for Automated Repair of REST API Misuses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29290)
*Shoei Inoue,Norihiro Yoshida,Erina Makihara,Shiyu Yang,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文评估了RAG（检索增强生成）在REST API误用自动修复中，不同API规范存储配置对修复效果的影响。实验构建了11种RAG数据库组织配置，最高修复率达到88.6%，远超基线方法的54.3%，表明按版本和内容类型组织规范是一种有效设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST API客户端开发中常出现API误用问题，而服务器返回的错误响应往往不足以定位根因，导致开发者需要反复与服务器端沟通。尽管RAG是向LLM提供外部知识的有力手段，但在REST API误用修复场景下，API规范应如何在RAG数据库中组织存储仍是未解问题。

Method: 作者从真实仓库收集REST API误用案例，构建了11种不同数据库结构（不同组织方式）的RAG配置，并与基线方法进行修复率对比评估。这些配置涵盖不同的API规范组织维度，如版本、内容类型等。

Result: 在研究的真实数据集上，基线方法修复率为54.3%，而采用四个数据库的RAG方法最高修复率可达88.6%。实验结果显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 按照API版本和内容类型对规范进行组织，是RAG-based REST API误用修复的有效数据库设计策略，可显著提升自动修复的成功率。

Abstract: Many Internet of Things (IoT) services provide Representational State Transfer (REST) APIs, which require client developers to implement applications that conform to the corresponding API specifications. When client programs contain API misuse, developers debug them based on error responses. However, such responses are often insufficient for identifying the root cause, requiring developers to repeatedly communicate with the server. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a promising approach for providing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, in automated repair of REST API misuses, it remains unclear how specifications should be stored in a RAG database. This study evaluates how different configurations for organizing API specifications affect RAG-based repair of REST API misuse. We constructed 11 RAG configurations with different database structures and compared their repair rates with a baseline method. For evaluation, we used REST API misuse cases collected from real-world repositories. The results show that, in the studied datasets, the baseline method achieved a repair rate of 54.3%, whereas a RAG-based method using four databases achieved a maximum repair rate of 88.6%. These results indicate that organizing specifications according to version and content type can be an effective design choice for RAG-based REST API misuse repair.

</details>


### [395] [Personalized Recommender Systems for Gym Workouts: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29409)
*Roan Rosema,Helma Torkamaan,Masoud Mansoury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将健身推荐从单纯的动作推荐扩展为完整的训练处方推荐（含动作、组数、次数、负荷），并提出一个基于强化学习的框架，包含四种环境（纯动作/完整处方，各有无跳过与有跳过交互），实验表明完整处方建模能获得更高的奖励和用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健身推荐系统仅推荐动作，忽略了组数、次数和负荷等关键训练参数，且未能考虑用户跳过动作等现实行为，限制了系统在实际场景中的应用价值。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的推荐框架，构建四种环境：exercise-only与full-prescription设置，每种又分为有/无skip交互；full-prescription环境同时推荐动作、组数、次数和负荷，skip-enabled环境利用用户跳过行为进行在线个性化。

Result: 在合成用户上的实验表明，完整处方建模相比纯动作推荐能获得更高的奖励和更大的用户参与度，验证了现实训练规划在个性化健身推荐系统中的重要性。

Conclusion: 健身推荐系统应将推荐范围从动作选择扩展至完整的训练处方，并结合用户跳过行为进行自适应调整，以提升系统实用性和用户参与度。

Abstract: Workout recommender systems aim to help gym users complete effective and engaging training sessions. However, recommending exercises alone is insufficient, as a practical system must also determine appropriate sets, repetitions, and training loads, while adapting to user behavior such as skipping exercises. Existing approaches typically consider only a subset of these factors, limiting their applicability in real-world settings. In this paper, we extend workout recommendation from exercise selection to full workout prescription. We propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based framework with four environments: exercise-only and full-prescription settings, each with and without skip-based interaction. The full-prescription environments recommend exercises, sets, repetitions, and load, while the skip-enabled environments use user skipping behavior for online personalization. Experiments with synthetic users show that modeling the full prescription task leads to higher rewards and greater user engagement than exercise-only recommendation, highlighting the importance of realistic workout planning in personalized gym recommender systems.

</details>


### [396] [Agents as Knowledge Integrator and Utilizer in Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29410)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Puzhen Wu,Zewei Liu,Zheng Lin,Jianheng Tang,Jing Yang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了AgentMMRec，一个基于智能体的多模态推荐框架，通过两个协同角色（Integrator Agent和Utilizer Agent）将多模态内容与用户行为知识整合到推荐图结构和模型表示中，在三个Amazon数据集上一致优于现有多模态推荐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统通常直接将视觉和文本特征注入物品表示，或从模态级相似度构建同质图，但生成信号与推荐目标存在语义错位。论文从知识整合角度出发，认为多模态内容应与用户行为一起被解读后再用于构建推荐图或调整排名，以弥合这一语义鸿沟。

Method: AgentMMRec包含两个协同角色：Integrator Agent从训练交互和物品内容中推断行为感知与多模态感知的用户偏好和物品属性，存入可复用的知识记忆；Utilizer Agent消费该记忆来细化模态特定的物品-物品图、构建行为感知的同质图，并在冻结的评估期记忆下对候选列表进行重排序。生成的知识先转化为图结构和模型表示，再用于推荐，而非直接LLM特征增强或纯LLM重排序。

Result: 在三个Amazon多模态推荐数据集上的实验表明，AgentMMRec在Recall和NDCG上一致优于近期多模态基线；在稀疏性和物品冷启动场景下仍然有效，并且其构建的知识可以迁移到现有推荐骨干模型上。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态知识与用户行为协同解读并转化为图结构和模型表示，AgentMMRec有效解决了多模态信号与推荐目标之间的语义错位问题，展现了良好的性能、鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly rely on multimodal recommender systems to rank products, media, and other Web content. Existing methods usually inject visual and textual features into item representations or build homogeneous graphs from modality-level similarity, but the resulting signals can remain misaligned with the recommendation objective. We study this semantic gap from a knowledge-integration perspective: multimodal content should be interpreted together with user behavior before it is used to construct recommendation graphs or adjust rankings.
  We propose AgentMMRec, an agent-based multimodal recommendation framework with two coordinated roles. The Integrator Agent infers behavior- and multimodal-aware user preferences and item properties from training interactions and item content, then stores them in a reusable knowledge memory. The Utilizer Agent consumes this memory to refine modality-specific item-item graphs, construct behavior-aware homogeneous graphs, and rerank candidate lists under a frozen evaluation-time memory. This design differs from direct LLM feature augmentation and pure LLM reranking because the generated knowledge is first converted into graph structure and model representations before recommendation. Experiments on three Amazon multimodal recommendation datasets show that AgentMMRec consistently improves Recall and NDCG over recent multimodal baselines, remains effective under sparsity and item cold-start settings, and can transfer its constructed knowledge to existing backbones.

</details>


### [397] [Content Exploration Beyond the Feed: Creator Supply and the Shared Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29430)
*Yuanyuan Shen,Yiren Yan,Wenjie Li,Chunhui Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究基于大型短视频平台的四项实验，系统分析了预算探索机制对创作者侧和观众侧的因果效应，提出了"语料库效应"及其测量局限，揭示了观众侧A/B测试在语料库流转下的识别边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通过预算探索给新内容初始曝光，再利用早期表现决定后续投放。但现有已发表的预算目标优化文献只度量观众侧消费，忽略了创作者侧的响应（即探索带来的创作供给激励）。此外，观众侧A/B测试因语料库效应（两臂消耗共享内容库导致效应相互抵消）存在系统性测量偏差，导致对探索总价值的估计可能严重失准。

Method: 论文采用四项实验的多重验证策略：(1) 为期八个月的创作者消融实验，对比探索机制与最小探索地板下创作者的发布行为差异；(2) 预算匹配再分配实验，考察将固定探索预算重新分配后创作者参与度的变化；(3) 为期一年的观众消融实验，测量视频观看次数和观看时间的变化；(4) 为期三周的联合分流（co-diverted）实验，让两个实验臂各自拥有独立语料库以规避语料库效应抵消，并配合探索期后的队列分析检测有机增长。

Result: 创作者消融实验显示探索机制使每位创作者发布视频数提高8.55%，至少发布一次的创作者比例提高7.10%。预算匹配再分配在不损害短期内观众侧指标的前提下提升了创作者参与度。观众消融实验中视频观看次数增加1.74%但观看时间下降2.13%。三周联合分流实验无法确定语料库效应的符号方向（验证了理论预测），但成功识别了直接Feed效应并检测到探索结束后的有机增长。

Conclusion: 论文确立了探索对创作者供给的正向因果效应，并揭示了新兴测量两大局限：其一，观众侧A/B测试在共享语料库条件下会抵消语料库效应；其二，独立语料库虽能避免抵消，但语料库周转率w决定了实验周期t内最多只能表达wt比例的最终语料库效应。在语料库路径显著弯曲之前，有效置信区间可能缺乏有限上端点。因此，识别探索总价值需要设计足够长的实验期并保证语料库独立分配。

Abstract: Industrial recommenders give new content initial views through budgeted exploration, then use early performance to decide further delivery. On many short-video platforms, exploration is the primary way new videos reach viewers. Viewer-side tests measure consumption; the published budget objectives we review omit creator response. We analyze four experiments on a major short-video platform. An eight-month creator ablation finds production exploration raises videos posted per creator by 8.55% and creators posting at least once by 7.10% relative to a minimal floor. A budget-matched reallocation raises creator participation with no detectable short-run viewer-side change. A year-long viewer ablation finds 1.74% more video views but 2.13% less view time. A delivered view creates immediate feed value, can trigger organic take-up, and can induce creator supply. Take-up and supply replenish a shared corpus, creating two measurement limits. Viewer-side A/B tests cancel the corpus effect when both arms consume the same corpus. Giving each arm its own corpus avoids cancellation, but turnover still controls the horizon. If the corpus turns over at rate w per posting cycle, a t-cycle experiment expresses at most wt of the eventual corpus effect. More users reduce noise but do not speed turnover. Before the corpus path visibly bends, data cannot distinguish a modest fast effect from an arbitrarily large slow one, so a valid confidence interval may lack a finite upper endpoint. As predicted, the three-week co-diverted experiment cannot determine the sign of the eventual corpus effect. Within the window, it identifies the direct feed effect, and an exploratory cohort analysis detects organic lift after exploration ends. The experiments establish a positive creator response, measure the gross corpus flow visible within three weeks, and show the design and duration needed to identify total value.

</details>


### [398] [The Edge Spectrum of Choice-Derived Item Graphs: Strong and Weak Edges Encode Different Relations in Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29578)
*Keigo Sakurai,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图协同过滤中"边谱"现象：由选择模型生成的item-item图中，强边与弱边编码着本质不同的关系（强边集中于点击项在展示列表中的竞争对手），导致平滑算子与排序梯度存在符号错配。作者证明co-click图天然不具此错配，并通过MIND和EB-NeRD上的实验诊断出：只有边幅感知算子才能恢复预测排序，邻居截断k是语义开关而非稀疏化超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图协同过滤依赖item-item图进行正平滑，但其隐含假设——强边与弱边编码相同的关系——在实践中并不成立。特别是当图的权重来自选择模型时，强弱边编码的是本质不同的关系（边谱现象），这导致基于平滑的推荐算子与排序学习目标之间存在系统性错配，而现有方法未能识别这一结构性问题的根源。

Method: 作者形式化了平滑算子与排序梯度之间的符号错配，并证明co-click图在构造上不会出现该错配。他们通过三个实验验证诊断：(i) 对比drop-in选择派生算子与co-click性能；(ii) 检验统一标量修正（符号翻转、in-slate margin loss）为何失败；(iii) 使用边幅感知算子并按诊断而非调参定位regime边界。同时将诊断转化为可复用的部署前检查协议。

Result: 实验证实：选择派生算子即使索引了结构不同的邻域也无法超越co-click；标量级修正因错配存在于图中而非损失函数中而系统性失败；只有边幅感知算子且regime边界由诊断确定时才能恢复预测的排序。诊断还预测绝对增益在衰减传播通道下较小。

Conclusion: 图平滑在选择派生图上的失败根因是边谱导致的符号错配，而非超参数欠调优。邻居截断k应被视为控制边类型的语义开关。作者将诊断固化为协议，供从业者在部署任何选择派生item侧算子前运行，并公开了代码。

Abstract: Graph collaborative filtering relies on item--item graphs whose edges are used for positive smoothing, under the implicit assumption that stronger edges encode more of the same relation as weaker ones. We show that this assumption fails for a practically important class of graphs: those whose edge weights come from a choice model. On such graphs, strong and weak edges encode qualitatively different relations, which we call an edge spectrum. Specifically, strong edges concentrate on the in-slate competitors of clicked items, exactly the pairs that the within-slate ranking gradient pushes apart, while weak edges do not. We formalize this as a sign mismatch between the smoothing operator and the ranking gradient, and prove that co-click graphs cannot exhibit the same misalignment by construction. This diagnosis explains three empirical observations on MIND and EB-NeRD: (i) drop-in choice-derived operators do not beat co-click, despite indexing structurally distinct neighborhoods; (ii) uniform scalar fixes (sign flip, in-slate margin loss) fail predictably, because the misalignment lives in the graph, not in the loss; (iii) only edge-magnitude-aware operators, with the regime boundary located by the diagnosis rather than by tuning, recover the predicted ordering. The neighbor cutoff $k$ is therefore a semantic switch, not a sparsification hyperparameter. Our claim concerns which interventions fail or succeed and why, not absolute headline gains, which the diagnosis itself predicts to be small under the attenuated propagation channel we observe. We turn the diagnosis into a reusable protocol practitioners can run before deploying any choice-derived item-side operator. Code: https://github.com/kyomusso/Edge-Spectrum-in-CF.

</details>


### [399] [RePair: Turning Retrieval Failures into Counterfactual Hard Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29604)
*Siyi Liu,Xiaorong Zhu,Enjun Du,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了RePair方法，通过挖掘假正例（false positives）作为反事实支架，利用LLM进行最小化局部编辑生成硬正例，并与未编辑版本构成横跨决策边界的硬负对，实现互补的推拉监督，从而比无条件的合成数据增强更高效地提升CLIP式双编码器视觉-语言检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP式双编码器在视觉-语言检索中表现优异，但实际准确率常受限于局部语义差异——排名靠前的近似匹配与真实匹配仅差一个关键细节。现有硬样本挖掘只能选取易混淆的候选样本，无法构造纠正后的对应样本；合成增强虽能生成新样本，但缺乏对实际模型失败模式的感知，仅针对无关的难度维度进行优化。

Method: RePair方法遵循三个原则：有效性（Validity）、最小性（Minimality）和局部性（Locality）。具体包含三个关键步骤：(1) 双向挖掘假正例（bidirectional false positive mining）；(2) 利用LLM引导的反事实编辑（LLM-guided counterfactual editing），对假正例中造成失败的关键残差进行最小化修正，生成同模态的硬正例；(3) 使用局部硬对对比损失（local hard-pair contrastive objective）进行训练，利用修正前后样本跨越决策边界构成互补的推-拉监督信号。

Result: 在Flickr30K和COCO30K基准上，RePair以仅107K合成样本超越受控增强基线，合成样本数量比可比方法少26%-75%，表明失败条件驱动的修复比错误无关的增强具有更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: 失败条件驱动的反事实修复比错误无关的数据增强更高效，RePair验证了基于模型实际失败模式构造硬样本对的范式能在更少合成数据下取得更优性能。

Abstract: Vision-language retrieval with CLIP-style dual encoders achieves strong cross-modal performance, yet practical accuracy often hinges on localized semantic distinctions where top-ranked near misses differ from the true match by a single critical detail. Hard-sample mining can select confusable candidates but cannot construct corrected counterparts; synthetic augmentation can generate novel samples but, without conditioning on actual model failures, targets irrelevant dimensions of hardness. We observe that a top-ranked false positive is a counterfactual scaffold---sharing most of the query's semantics while differing in a localized failure-causing residual. Minimally correcting this residual yields a hard positive of the ground truth in the same modality; the corrected and unedited versions form a hard negative pair that straddles the decision boundary, producing complementary pull--push supervision. We introduce RePair, guided by three principles---Validity, Minimality, and Locality---which mines false positives bidirectionally, applies LLM-guided counterfactual editing, and trains with a local hard-pair contrastive objective. On Flickr30K and COCO30K, RePair outperforms controlled augmentation baselines with only 107K synthetic samples---26\%--75\% fewer than comparable methods---confirming failure-conditioned repair is more data-efficient than error-agnostic augmentation.

</details>


### [400] [ICEGR: An Intent-Coherent End-to-End Generative Retrieval Framework for E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29652)
*Jiayi Tuo,Hehan Li,Dongjun Fu,Xin Lu,Ling Zhuang,Fuwei Zhang,Meifang Li,Peizhi Xu,Hanmeng Liu,Shuanglong Li,Liwei Qian,Yanbiao Ma,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ICEGR，一个意图一致的端到端生成式检索框架，用于电商搜索。该框架通过意图感知的语义ID构建、合成查询增强的统一SFT以及相关性校准的偏好优化三个组件，在生成式检索训练全流程中保持查询意图一致性。离线实验中Recall@20提升21.7%、NDCG@20提升26.6%；在线A/B测试中CTR提升3.52%、订单量提升15.96%、GMV提升7.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索(GR)方法在电商搜索中存在三个关键问题：(1)基于静态商品信息构件的语义ID(SID)无法编码商品-意图关联；(2)仅依赖在线日志的query-to-SID训练导致低曝光商品缺乏真实查询意图监督；(3)面向业务的偏好优化可能偏向热门或高价值商品而非最匹配查询意图的商品，削弱了查询-商品相关性。

Method: ICEGR包含三个核心组件：(1)意图感知SID构建(Intent-Aware SID Construction)：将查询意图信号融入SID构建过程，使SID能捕获超越静态商品信息的搜索意图；(2)合成查询增强统一SFT(Synthetic Query-Enhanced Unified SFT)：将多个SFT任务统一到query-to-SID目标下，并用合成查询增强在线日志的稀疏监督，为低曝光商品提供补充的查询意图监督；(3)相关性校准偏好优化(Relevance-Calibrated Preference Optimization)：将查询-商品相关性与业务信号整合到边际自适应偏好目标中，在保持查询意图的同时支持业务偏好学习。

Result: 离线实验中，ICEGR相比基线在Recall@20上提升21.7%，NDCG@20上提升26.6%。在百度电商搜索中部署为端到端生成式检索路径，A/B测试显示CTR相对提升3.52%，订单量提升15.96%，GMV提升7.53%。

Conclusion: ICEGR通过在生成式检索训练流水线的每个阶段（SID构建、监督微调、偏好优化）一致地整合查询意图，有效解决了现有方法中查询意图不一致的问题，显著提升了检索性能和业务指标，验证了端到端生成式检索在电商搜索场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) is promising for e-commerce search, yet existing methods struggle to maintain query-intent consistency throughout the training pipeline. First, semantic ID (SID) construction based on static product information limits the ability of SIDs to encode product-intent associations. Second, although supervised fine-tuning (SFT) learns product-SID mappings across the catalog, low-exposure products still lack real query-intent supervision because query-to-SID training relies solely on online logs, resulting in poor retrieval performance for these products. Third, business-oriented preference optimization may favor popular or high-value products over those that best match the query intent, weakening query-product relevance. To address these issues, we propose ICEGR, an Intent-Coherent End-to-End Generative Retrieval Framework for E-commerce Search that integrates query intent consistently throughout the GR training pipeline. ICEGR comprises three components: (1) Intent-Aware SID Construction incorporates query-intent signals into SID construction, enabling SIDs to capture search intent beyond static product information; (2) Synthetic Query-Enhanced Unified SFT unifies multiple SFT tasks under the query-to-SID objective and augments sparse supervision from online logs with synthetic queries, providing complementary query-intent supervision for low-exposure products; and (3) Relevance-Calibrated Preference Optimization integrates query-product relevance and business signals into a margin-adaptive preference objective, preserving query intent while enabling business preference learning. Offline results show that ICEGR improves Recall@20 by 21.7% and NDCG@20 by 26.6% over the baseline. Deployed as an end-to-end generative retrieval pathway in Baidu E-commerce Search, ICEGR achieves relative improvements of 3.52% in CTR, 15.96% in order volume, and 7.53% in GMV in an A/B test.

</details>


### [401] [Demand-Side Measurement for Generative Engine Optimization: Constructing and Validating a Million-Persona, Intent-Annotated Buyer Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30023)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin,Daniil Dzemesjuk*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了PersonaGen-1M，一个包含103万个合成买家画像的语料库，覆盖511个行业标签和4种市场情境，包含1940万个结构化行为属性和516万个搜索查询。每个画像带有单一主意图标签（78.3%信息型、17.4%商业型、4.3%交易型）和偏好信息来源字段，用于研究品牌如何进入或未进入生成式引擎（如ChatGPT、Gemini、Perplexity）的答案中列出的品牌短名单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎直接在答案中列出品牌短名单，研究品牌如何进入或未进入该名单需要需求侧数据，即买家在品类中的提问内容、所需信息和信任的来源。现有的百万级画像语料库是为训练数据多样性构建的，既不带分阶段搜索意图标签，也不带偏好来源字段，无法与供给侧推荐测量数据对接。

Method: 从四个公开数据集中提取约4000万条原始画像描述，通过GPU加速的MinHash局部敏感哈希（LSH）和语义去重进行清洗，然后按固定模式丰富信息，构建出103万个合成买家画像。每个画像具备单一primary_intent标签（覆盖其查询集）和preferred_sources字段（命名买家信任的来源类型）。

Result: 生成的语料库包含1,031,732个合成买家画像，覆盖511个行业标签和4种市场情境，携带19,416,821个结构化行为属性（其中5,160,046个为搜索查询）。意图标签中78.3%为信息型、17.4%为商业型、4.3%为交易型。与2026年8月调查的百万级画像语料库相比，只有另一个带来源偏好属性（作为六值媒体渠道枚举），而PersonaGen-1M提供逐画像命名的来源列表与分阶段商业搜索意图标签和附加查询集。

Conclusion: intent字段用于筛选驱动推荐的商业评估型画像（commercial-evaluation personas），preferred_sources字段可与引文溯源数据配对，该配对是主要预期用途，其受控的经验估算属于未来工作。完整语料库仅限非商业研究按需共享，另公开发布分层子集以便协议、模式和验证的可检查性与可复用性。

Abstract: Generative engines such as ChatGPT, Gemini, and Perplexity answer buyer questions directly and name a shortlist of brands inside the answer. Studying how brands enter or fail to enter that shortlist requires demand-side data: what buyers in a category ask, what information they need, and which sources they trust. Existing large persona corpora are built for training-data diversity and carry neither a staged search-intent label nor a preferred-sources field, so they cannot be joined to supply-side recommendation measurements. We built and validated PersonaGen-1M, a corpus of 1,031,732 synthetic buyer personas spanning 511 industry labels and 4 market contexts, carrying 19,416,821 structured behavioral attributes, 5,160,046 of them search queries. Each persona carries a single primary_intent label covering its query set (78.3% informational, 17.4% commercial, 4.3% transactional) and a preferred_sources field naming the source types that buyer would trust. The corpus was built from roughly 40 million raw persona descriptions drawn from four public datasets through GPU-accelerated MinHash LSH plus semantic deduplication, then enriched to a fixed schema. The intent field selects the commercial-evaluation personas whose queries drive recommendation, and the preferred_sources field pairs against citation-provenance data; that join is the primary intended use, and its controlled empirical estimate is future work. Among million-scale persona corpora surveyed in August 2026, one other carries a source-preference attribute, as a six-value media-channel enum; PersonaGen-1M pairs named per-persona source lists with a staged commercial search-intent label and an attached query set. The full corpus is shared on request for non-commercial research; a stratified subset is published openly so the protocol, the schema and the validation can be inspected and reused without asking us.

</details>


### [402] [The Language of the Question Selects the Market: Query Language and Exit IP as Separable Factors in Commercial Recommendations from a Generative Search Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30052)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过234次受控实验（6个重复运行/条件，覆盖4个出口国、6种查询语言），探究生成式搜索界面在回答商业问题时的市场推荐偏差。发现三点：1）顶级推荐不稳定（6次重复中4/6提示词结果变化），且与界面/API无关，是系统固有属性；2）查询语言而非地理位置决定本地供应商是否出现（语言与国家匹配时本地品牌胜出率高，英文查询时爱沙尼亚和土耳其本地品牌0胜出）；3）语言与位置可分离且作用于不同机制——固定语言只改变出口IP可切换推荐市场，同时答案语言不变。少数语言（俄语在爱沙尼亚）处于中间层级。负对照实验显示，某些虽国内有供应商但无国家管制的类别，各语言均无本地品牌出现，说明决定因素是"国家管制"而非"国内供应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索界面回答商业问题时会推荐特定市场的产品，但以往研究未系统分离语言、地理位置和界面类型等变量的影响。作者希望揭示这种推荐偏差背后的因果机制，特别是语言与位置各自独立作用的规律，以及为何某些市场偏好会被触发。

Method: 采用受控探针实验设计：2026年8月29-30日，对ChatGPT已登出网页界面和OpenAI API进行234次运行，覆盖4个出口国家、6种查询语言，每单元格6次完全相同运行。使用了正实验（商业查询）+负对照（第二类别、相同编码工具）的设计，并对比了浏览器界面与API（分别启用/禁用web search）的结果。

Result: 1）推荐不稳定是系统属性：顶级推荐在6个提示词中的4个上、6次重复运行间发生变化，浏览器与API（web search开/关）表现一致。2）查询语言而非位置决定本地供应商是否出现：语言与国家匹配时本地品牌1/24胜出；英文查询时爱沙尼亚和土耳其本地品牌0/6胜出。3）语言与位置作用分离：固定语言仅改变exit IP即可切换推荐市场（柏林发土耳其语、塔林发俄语两对测试均验证）。俄语在爱沙尼亚处在中间层级：4/6次命名爱沙尼亚供应商、6/6全局品牌；对比爱沙尼亚语每次命名本地、英语一次也没有。负对照类别显示各语言均无本地品牌，宣告"国内供应"假设不成立。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索界面的市场推荐由查询语言和出口位置共同决定但机制分离：语言控制供应商是否出现及答案语言，位置控制具体推荐哪个国家的市场。系统推荐不稳定是其固有属性，而非界面层差异。是否存在本地品牌的预测因素不是"国内是否有供应"，而是"类别是否受国家监管"。

Abstract: When a generative search interface answers a commercial question, which market's products it names is decided before the model reasons about the products. We report a controlled probe of 234 runs against the logged-out ChatGPT web interface and the OpenAI API, collected on 29 and 30 August 2026 across four exit countries and six query languages, with six identical runs per cell. Three results. First, the top recommendation is unstable: it changed across six identical runs on four of six prompts, and that rate was identical in the browser interface and in the API with web search both enabled and disabled, so instability is a property of the system and not of the surface. Second, query language, and not location, decides whether local suppliers appear at all. Where the query language matched the country, a global brand won 1 of 24 runs; asked in English on the same connections, local brands took 0 of 6 runs in Estonia and Turkiye. Third, language and location are separable and act on different things: holding the query language fixed and moving only the exit IP moves the market whose brands are named while the answer stays in the query language. We show this on two unrelated pairs, Turkish asked from Berlin and Russian asked from Tallinn, and in both the answer names the resident country's suppliers. A minority language occupies a middle tier: Russian asked from Estonia names an Estonian supplier in 4 of 6 runs and a global one in all six, where Estonian names a local supplier in every run and English names none. A negative control in a second category, coded with the same instrument, shows no language effect at all, and disconfirms our own expectation: that category does have domestic suppliers and none was named in any language, which points the explanation at whether a category is nationally regulated rather than at whether it is nationally supplied.

</details>


### [403] [E-SENS: Exclusion-Sensitive Penalization for Negative-Constraint Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30130)
*Yerang Kim,Jiyoon Myung,Joohyung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: E-SENS是一种无需训练的重新排序方法，用于敏感于否定的检索。它通过提取排除概念的"陷阱查询"（trap query），并将其相似度从原始查询的检索分数中减去，从而在保留相关结果的同时减少被排除概念的检索。在ExcluIR基准上，E-SENS在四个嵌入模型上展示了清晰的召回率-违反率权衡，并在保持召回率的同时减少了陷阱检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强语言模型在用户明确排除某些概念时可能失效，因为被排除的概念仍出现在查询文本中，密集检索器会为其赋予高相似度。现有方法无法有效处理这种否定敏感的检索需求，特别是在"包含一个概念同时排除另一个"或"属于某类别但不同于相近实例"的复杂查询场景下。

Method: E-SENS是一种训练无关的重新排序方法。其核心机制是：为被排除的一方提取一个紧凑的"陷阱查询"（trap query），然后从原始查询的检索分数中减去陷阱查询的相似度，从而实现否定敏感的重排序。

Result: 在ExcluIR基准上，E-SENS在四种嵌入模型上展现了清晰的召回率-违反率（recall-violation）权衡，并在保持召回率的同时有效减少了陷阱检索（trap retrieval）。

Conclusion: E-SENS通过简单的相似度减法策略，无需重新训练即可提升检索系统对否定约束的敏感性，在保留相关文档召回的同时降低被排除概念被检索的概率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented language models can fail to respect negative constraints when the retriever supplies evidence about concepts the user explicitly excluded. Beyond explicit negation, queries may ask for answers that include one concept while excluding another, or for entities that belong to a category but differ from a closely related instance. Because the excluded concept still appears in the query text, dense retrievers may assign high similarity to documents about that concept even when the user asks to avoid it. We introduce E-SENS, a training-free reranking method for negation-sensitive retrieval. E-SENS extracts a compact trap query for the excluded side and subtracts trap-query similarity from the original-query retrieval score. On ExcluIR, E-SENS shows a clear recall-violation trade-off across four embedding models and reduces trap retrieval at recall-preserving settings.

</details>


### [404] [Understanding before verifying: Claim normalization for automated citation verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30145)
*Yifan He,Mengjia Wu,Siming Deng,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出引用归一化（Claim Normalization）方法，通过三种重写策略处理原始引用声明，构建了三阶段的引用归一化引文验证框架（CNCV），包括声明归一化、带接地证据检索和引用分类。在18个分类器的析因实验中，相较传统两阶段框架，CNCV在宏F1上对编码器平均提升12%、对生成式LLM提升10%，且自动归一化声明检索的证据在分类性能上与人工标注证据统计等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段（检索-分类）框架直接处理原始引用声明，忽视了原始声明的复杂性，导致范围不匹配、视角不匹配和命题纠缠三个问题，这些问题增加了检索和分类的难度，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出声明归一化方法，在检索和分类之前对原始引用声明应用三种重写策略，使每个下游模型执行单一、定义明确的任务。基于此方法构建CNCV三阶段框架：声明归一化、带接地证据检索和引用分类。通过析因实验在18个分类器上进行评估。

Result: 与两阶段框架相比，CNCV在宏F1上对编码器平均提升12%，对生成式LLM平均提升10%。改进主要由证据质量驱动，且自动归一化声明检索得到的证据在分类性能上与人工标注证据统计等价。

Conclusion: 声明归一化有效解决了原始引用声明复杂性带来的三个问题，证据质量是性能提升的主导因素，自动归一化方法可达到与人工标注相当的效果。

Abstract: Citation accuracy has been studied for decades because of its importance to research reliability. Content-level citation verification assesses the reliability of scholarly claims. Recent work adopts a two-stage retrieval-classification framework inherited from fact-checking. However, this design overlooks the complexity of the raw citing claim and introduces three issues into the verification system, namely scope mismatch, perspective mismatch, and proposition entanglement. These issues increase the difficulty of retrieval and classification, thereby limiting model performance. Motivated by this gap, we propose claim normalization, which applies three rewriting strategies to the raw citing claim before retrieval and classification, allowing each downstream model to perform a single, well-defined task. Building on this method, we develop Claim-Normalized Citation Verification (CNCV), a new three-stage framework consisting of claim normalization, evidence retrieval with grounding, and citation classification. We evaluate CNCV across 18 classifiers using a factorial experiment on human-annotated citation instances. Compared with the prior two-stage framework, CNCV improves macro F1 by an average of 12% for encoders and 10% for generative LLMs, driven by improved evidence quality, the dominant factor identified in our experiments. Evidence retrieved from automatically normalized claims yields downstream classification performance statistically equivalent to that obtained with manually annotated evidence.

</details>


### [405] [Doc-REFRAG: Rethinking Multimodal Document Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30163)
*Ruofan Hu,Shengyang Xu,Minjie Hong,Xiaoda Yang,Sashuai Zhou,Ke Lei,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DocLongRAG大规模多模态检索增强生成数据集（343K问答对，平均每问关联37.4张图像），并在此基础上提出Doc-REFRAG框架，通过问题引导的视觉token压缩和轻量级RL选择器实现高效精准的多图像RAG，在六个基准上超越十一个强基线，实现最先进的准确率和更低的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG模型主要面向单图像或封闭文档场景，在多图像真实场景下准确率有限；同时处理大量检索图像会产生大量无关视觉token，带来巨大的计算开销。

Method: 提出DocLongRAG数据集（343K问答对，平均37.4张检索图像）；提出Doc-REFRAG框架：采用问题引导的方式将视觉token压缩为粗粒度块，并通过基于轻量级强化学习的选择器有选择性地扩展与问题相关的块。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，Doc-REFRAG超越十一个强基线模型，达到最先进准确率，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: Doc-REFRAG验证了问题引导的视觉token压缩与选择性扩展策略在多图像RAG场景中的有效性，实现了准确率与推理效率的双重提升，为真实多图像文档问答提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge resides in multimodal documents, necessitating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for accurate question answering. However, existing multimodal RAG models are primarily designed for single-image or closed-document settings and exhibit limited accuracy in realistic multi-image scenarios. Moreover, processing numerous retrieved images incurs substantial computational overhead from irrelevant visual tokens. To address these challenges, we introduce DocLongRAG, a large-scale dataset of 343K question--answer pairs, each associated with an average of 37.4 retrieved images to reflect authentic RAG workflows. Building on this dataset, we propose Doc-REFRAG, a question-guided framework that compresses visual tokens into coarse chunks and selectively expands question-relevant ones via a lightweight RL-based selector. Experiments on six benchmarks show that Doc-REFRAG outperforms eleven strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly lower inference latency. Our resources are available at https://github.com/Collab-Gen/Doc-REFRAG.

</details>


### [406] [SetMIR: Multi-Interest Retrieval as Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30251)
*Xiaodong Liu,Congfei Zhang,Hsiang-wei Chao,Siman Wang,Xiao Bai,Tong Zhao,Jingxiao Ma,Wen Zhang,Zhe Liu,Shantanu Aggarwal,Di Huang,William Leach,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SetMIR将多兴趣召回建模为集合预测问题，通过可学习查询、匈牙利匹配和存在性评分，在Snap动态产品广告中减少33%的ANN查询量，同时提升CTR 44%、CVR 51%，并作为新召回源使整体CVR提升3.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中基于嵌入的召回是核心，但单用户嵌入无法充分捕捉用户的多样化兴趣。现有多兴趣召回方法存在两个关键问题：兴趣坍缩（不同嵌入学到相同兴趣）和静态调度（即使某些嵌入不必要，服务时仍使用固定召回预算）。

Method: SetMIR将多兴趣召回视为集合预测问题：用Transformer编码用户行为历史，通过K个可学习查询解码一组用户兴趣，每个兴趣生成一个召回嵌入和一个存在性评分。训练阶段使用匈牙利匹配将目标一对一分配给查询，使匹配的查询学习不同兴趣，存在性头学习哪些查询是活跃的；服务阶段利用存在性评分和查询级非极大值抑制（NMS）只发起活跃且无冗余的ANN查询。

Result: 在Snap动态产品广告（DPA）数据上，SetMIR在所有指标上优于四种学习的多兴趣召回器，同时每次请求减少33%的ANN查询。部署为DPA生产栈的新召回源后，整体CVR提升3.1%，相较于使用相同物品嵌入、ANN索引和召回配额的item-to-item召回源，CTR提升44%，CVR提升51%。

Conclusion: SetMIR通过集合预测范式从本质上解决了兴趣坍缩和静态调度问题，在提升召回性能的同时降低了计算开销，并在真实工业推荐系统中得到验证，证明了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval is at the core of industrial recommender systems, but a single user embedding is often too limited to capture a user's diverse interests. Multi-interest retrieval addresses this by using multiple user embeddings, yet existing methods still suffer from two issues: interest collapse, where different embeddings learn the same interest, and static dispatch, where serving uses a fixed retrieval budget even when some embeddings are unnecessary. We propose SetMIR, which treats multi-interest retrieval as a set prediction problem. SetMIR encodes a user's behavior history with a transformer and uses K learnable queries to decode a set of user interests, each producing a retrieval embedding and a presence score. During training, Hungarian matching assigns targets to queries one-to-one, so matched queries learn distinct interests and the presence head learns which queries are active. At serving time, SetMIR uses presence scores and query-level Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) to issue only active, non-redundant ANN queries. On Snap's Dynamic Product Ads (DPA) data, SetMIR outperforms four learned multi-interest retrievers on every metric while issuing 33% fewer ANN queries per request. Deployed as a new retrieval source in the DPA production stack, SetMIR lifts overall CVR by 3.1%, while lifting CTR by 44% and CVR by 51% over the item-to-item retrieval source with the same item embeddings, ANN index, and retrieval quota.

</details>


### [407] [CAMIE: Co-Engagement-Aware Multimodal Item Embeddings for Snap Dynamic Product Ads Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30255)
*Xiaodong Liu,Siman Wang,Congfei Zhang,Hsiang-wei Chao,Xiao Bai,Wen Zhang,Jingxiao Ma,Zhe Liu,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAMIE，一个面向Snap动态产品广告（DPA）的协同参与感知多模态物品嵌入框架。它基于LLM/MLLM主干，利用原生多模态接口将物品图像与元数据映射到共享嵌入空间，并使用从用户旅程中挖掘的协同参与物品对进行对称的批内InfoNCE目标微调。离线评估中CAMIE在Recall@10上超越最强的商业多模态嵌入模型，并支持从同一检查点提供文本检索；在线部署中，CAMIE作为两个已上线内容型I2I编码器的即插即用替代品，在各项指标上全面超越多模态与文本基线。目前CAMIE已在生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业生产环境中的I2I检索面临两大挑战：一是视觉、文本、多模态编码器各自独立导致检索栈碎片化；二是仅基于内容训练的嵌入与驱动的下游转化的协同参与行为不对齐。这两个问题促使作者设计一个既能统一编码器又能对齐协同参与行为的解决方案。

Method: CAMIE采用LLM/MLLM作为主干，利用其原生多模态接口将物品图像与元数据统一编码到共享嵌入空间；随后从用户旅程中挖掘协同参与的物品对，使用对称的批内InfoNCE目标函数对主干进行微调，从而实现内容表征与协同参与行为信号的对齐。

Result: 离线方面，CAMIE在Recall@10上优于最强的商业多模态嵌入模型，且同一检查点直接提供文本检索服务而质量损失极小。在线方面，CAMIE作为两个已部署内容型I2I编码器的即插即用替代品：相对多模态对照组CTR提升0.390%、CVR提升10.832%；相对文本对照组CTR提升18.958%、CVR提升13.12%；在整体DPA流量上CTR提升0.211%、CVR提升1.911%。CAMIE已成功上线生产。

Conclusion: CAMIE通过统一的多模态主干和协同参与感知的微调策略，有效解决了生产I2I检索中编码器碎片化及内容-行为不对齐的问题，在离线和在线指标上均取得显著提升，并已成功部署至生产环境，验证了其实际价值。

Abstract: Item-to-item (I2I) retrieval is a core primitive in large-scale recommendation and advertising systems. In production Snap Dynamic Product Ads (DPA), I2I retrieval faces two challenges: separate visual, textual, and multimodal encoders fragment the retrieval stack, and content-only training does not align embeddings with the co-engagement behavior that drives downstream conversions. We present CAMIE, a co-engagement-aware multimodal item embedding framework for Snap DPA retrieval. CAMIE builds on LLM/MLLM backbones, using their native multimodal interfaces to represent item images and metadata in a shared embedding space. It then fine-tunes the backbone on co-engaged item pairs mined from user journeys with a symmetric in-batch InfoNCE objective. Offline, CAMIE outperforms the strongest commercial multimodal embedding model on Recall@10 and serves text-only retrieval from the same checkpoint with minimal quality loss. Online, CAMIE serves as a drop-in replacement for two deployed content-based I2I encoders, delivering +0.390% CTR / +10.832% CVR over the multimodal control, +18.958% CTR / +13.12% CVR over the text control, and +0.211% CTR / +1.911% CVR on overall DPA traffic. CAMIE is deployed in production.

</details>


### [408] [PEARL: Front-Loading Relational Chains for Multi-Hop Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30291)
*Subeen Ho,Hyeongu Kang,SeongKu Kang,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PEARL是一个无需训练的框架，通过垂直分区子表编码来提升大语言模型在多表检索中的能力，利用离线生成的多跳查询和预识别连接路径重构检索语料库，在3跳查询上R@2指标最高提升30.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在表格推理方面能力很强，但真实世界数据的碎片化和关系型结构使得检索相关表格成为难题。现有方法依赖整表表示，忽略了由连接（join）关系带来的跨表语义，导致多表检索效果不佳。

Method: PEARL采用垂直分区（vertical partitioning）策略进行子表编码。它离线在预识别的连接路径上生成多跳查询，并将相关列重组为垂直分区的语料库单元，从而增强检索语料库。整个过程无需查询时LLM推理，实现训练-Free的多表检索。

Result: 实验表明PEARL一致优于现有方法，在3跳查询上R@2指标最高提升+30.05%。

Conclusion: PEARL通过垂直分区子表编码和离线多跳查询增强，有效解决了多表检索中跨表语义丢失的问题，显著提升了检索性能，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in tabular reasoning, retrieving relevant tables remains challenging due to the fragmented and relational structure of real-world data. Existing work typically relies on whole table representations that overlook cross-table semantics induced by join relationships. We propose PEARL, a training-free framework that shifts the paradigm toward vertical partitioning-based sub-table encoding. PEARL augments the retrieval corpus offline by generating multi-hop queries over pre-identified join paths and reorganizing relevant columns into vertically partitioned corpus units, enabling effective multi-table retrieval without query-time LLM inference. Experiments show that PEARL consistently outperforms existing methods, with up to +30.05% gains in R@2 on 3-hop queries. The source code is available at https://github.com/SOOB2NHO/PEARL.

</details>


### [409] [Beyond Ranking Accuracy: Evaluating LLM-Cited Feature Rationales for Next Basket Repurchase Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30333)
*Yanan Cao,Anay Dombe,Murali Mohana Krishna Dandu,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Sinduja Subramaniam,Yogananth Mahalingam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在"下一购物篮回购推荐"任务中的角色，发现LLM作为独立的评分器/排序器表现不如监督学习排序器，但其引用的特征在特定条件下可以作为有效的解释组件（而非主排序器），且解释质量与排序精度需分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境中，回购推荐的质量不仅取决于排序精度，还包括向用户提供"为何现在推荐该商品"的可解释证据。LLM具有生成基于特征的可读性解释的潜力，但尚不清楚其能否作为有效的回购评分器，以及其引用的特征是否携带与结果相关的排序信号。

Method: 构建了涵盖购买节奏、频率、近因性、用户行为和商品流行度等多维度的回购特征；在两个公开杂货数据集和一个专有零售数据集上评估LLM；设计了两种对比实验：(1) 将现成LLM的评分与启发式方法和监督排序器对比；(2) 通过跨模型特征遮蔽协议，将LLM引用的特征与模型特定的归因方法进行对比，测量遮蔽所选特征后排序性能的下降程度。

Result: LLM得分无法与监督排序器竞争，表明现成LLM不应作为独立回购推荐器；但在某些设置下，改变提示和证据表示可以改善基于结果的特征遮蔽结果，即使排序性能没有提升；这种效果依赖数据集，并且并不总是与归因基线一致。

Conclusion: LLM更适合作为经过验证的解释组件而非主排序器，其合理化依据的质量应与排序精度分开评估。

Abstract: Next-basket repurchase recommendation is commonly formulated as a ranking task: given a customer's purchase history, the system ranks previously purchased items that may be needed again. In production settings, however, ranking accuracy is only one component of recommendation quality. Customers may also benefit from concise evidence about why an item is recommended now. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential way to surface such evidence through feature-based, human-readable rationales grounded in interpretable behavioral signals. We construct repurchase features spanning cadence, frequency, recency, user behavior, and item popularity, and evaluate LLMs on two public grocery datasets and one proprietary retail dataset. We investigate (1) whether off-the-shelf LLMs can use these features as next-basket scorers relative to heuristic and supervised rankers, and (2) whether LLM-cited features carry outcome-grounded ranking signal. For the latter, we compare LLM-cited features with model-specific attribution methods under a cross-model feature-masking protocol that measures ranking degradation after masking selected features. Our results show that LLM scores are not competitive with supervised rankers, suggesting that off-the-shelf LLMs should not be used as standalone repurchase recommenders. However, changes in prompt and evidence representation can improve outcome-grounded feature-masking results in some settings even when ranking performance does not improve; the effect is dataset-dependent and does not consistently match attribution baselines. These findings suggest a practical role for LLMs as validated explanation components rather than primary rankers, with rationale quality evaluated separately from ranking accuracy.

</details>


### [410] [HF-SID: High-Fidelity Semantic IDs for Generative Retrieval in Location-Based Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30479)
*Haowen Lin,Jing Li,Zhibin Hao,Fangye Wang,Lihui Su,Song Yang,Xiaojiang Zhou,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HF-SID，一种在表示阶段恢复地理、数值和结构保真度的生成式检索方法。通过将坐标转换为连续3D笛卡尔形式、将数值编码为单一单元，并应用基于结构的对比学习目标，HF-SID使用3-token语义ID（SID）在不增加解码成本的情况下，解决了现有SID在LBS检索中模糊细粒度差异的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于位置服务（LBS）的生成式检索中，SID是POI信息到达生成模型的唯一通道，但现有SID存在三个关键缺陷：(1) LLM对连续坐标的嵌入不连续，数值差异无法反映真实地理距离；(2) 动态数值属性尺度差异大，相同差距对不同属性重要性不同；(3) 短文本无法传达层级隶属关系。这些问题导致SID无法保留对LBS检索至关重要的细粒度信息。

Method: HF-SID在表示阶段（信息写入离散代码之前）恢复三类保真度：(1) 将坐标转换为连续3D笛卡尔形式，并利用Geo-CPT（地理编码预训练）在LLM内部进行类型感知嵌入，恢复地理保真度；(2) 将每个数值编码为单一单元，通过Num-CPT处理，恢复数值保真度；(3) 仅在最后一层残差上应用基于结构的对比学习目标（Structure-based Contrastive Learning），区分共享粗粒度标签但细粒度不同的共定位POI，恢复结构保真度。该方法通过丰富表示而非延长标识符，使用3-token的SID且不增加解码成本。

Result: HF-SID通过3-token SID在无额外解码成本的情况下实现了地理、数值和结构的保真度恢复。论文还提及该方法在大规模工业场景中进行了验证（摘要被截断，未完整呈现具体性能数据）。

Conclusion: HF-SID的核心贡献在于将保真度恢复从解码/后处理阶段前移到表示阶段，通过连续3D坐标编码、单单元数值编码和结构感知对比学习，在不延长标识符的前提下实现了更精细的LBS生成式检索。这一设计表明，在信息固定到离散代码之前增强表示质量，是提升生成式检索性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Generative retrieval has attracted increasing attention in Location-Based Services (LBS), where each Point-of-Interest (POI) is represented as a Semantic ID (SID). As the SID is the only channel through which POI information reaches the generative model, whatever it fails to preserve is irrecoverable at decoding time, and LBS retrieval is especially sensitive to the fine-grained differences that existing SIDs blur. Specifically, (1) LLMs embed continuous coordinates discontinuously, so their numeric differences do not reflect true geographic distance; (2) dynamic numerical attributes differ vastly in scale, so an identical gap may be decisive for one attribute yet negligible for another; and (3) short text cannot convey hierarchical affiliation, as text-similar POIs may belong to different hierarchies. We therefore propose HF-SID, which restores geographic, numerical, and structural fidelity at the representation stage, before any information is committed to a discrete code. It transforms coordinates into a continuous 3D Cartesian form and encodes each numerical value as a single unit, consolidated inside the LLM by Geo-CPT and Num-CPT with type-aware embeddings; a Structure-based Contrastive Learning objective, applied only to the last-layer residual, then separates co-located POIs that share a coarse tag but differ at the fine level. Because these mechanisms enrich the representation rather than lengthen the identifier, HF-SID uses a 3-token SID at no extra decoding cost. On a large-scale industrial

</details>


### [411] [Local-to-Global Sentence-Level Graph Reranking for Scientific Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30525)
*Zheng Dou,Zhao Zhang,Hao Geng,Ningjing Wang,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LoG-Reranker，一种面向科学文献综合的局部到全局句子级图重排框架，通过角色感知的局部句子评分和跨候选集的句子图全局精排，为生成器提供结构化上下文，提升生成综合的质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强的科学综合依赖于重排器筛选和组织信息，但现有重排器存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 段落级操作导致关键的方法论、实证和比较信息被淹没在冗长的扁平上下文中，削弱生成主张的接地性；(2) 依赖独立的查询-候选打分，忽略了候选之间互补、上下文和对比的关系，限制了信息覆盖面和综合的全面性。

Method: LoG-Reranker由两阶段组成：首先进行角色感知的局部评分（role-aware local scoring），识别细粒度的、与查询相关的句子；然后在候选集上构建句子图（sentence graph），建模句子间的关系，进行全局精排以细化句子排名。最终将排名靠前的句子及其邻居组织为结构化输入上下文，供生成器生成更全面和接地气的综合回答。

Result: 在科学综合和重排基准上的大量实验表明，LoG-Reranker持续优于竞争性重排器，产生更可靠的排名，并显著提升生成综合的质量。

Conclusion: 句子级图重排框架能够有效弥补段落级重排的局限性，通过捕获跨候选的细粒度关系来增强科学综合的全面性和接地性，验证了从局部句子评分到全局图精排的两阶段范式的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented scientific synthesis aims to answer complex research questions by integrating information from multiple papers into comprehensive and well-grounded responses. Since the generator can only synthesize the information selected and organized by the reranker, the quality of the generated synthesis depends critically on the reranked results. However, most rerankers operate at the passage level, which leaves key methodological, empirical, and comparative information buried in long and flat contexts, weakening the grounding of generated claims. Moreover, existing rerankers mainly rely on independent query-candidate scoring which overlooks complementary, contextual, and contrasting relations across scientific candidates, limiting information coverage and the comprehensiveness of the resulting synthesis. To address these limitations, we propose LoG-Reranker, a local-to-global sentence-level graph reranking framework for scientific synthesis. LoG-Reranker performs role-aware local scoring to identify fine-grained, query-relevant sentences and then models their relations on a sentence graph across the candidate set to globally refine sentence rankings. Top-ranked sentences and their connected neighbors are organized into a structured input context for generator to produce more grounded and comprehensive synthesis.Extensive experiments on scientific synthesis and reranking benchmarks show that LoG-Reranker consistently outperforms competitive rerankers, yielding more reliable rankings and improving the quality of generated synthesis.

</details>


### [412] [Preference Shapes Relevance: Cross-component Hierarchical Semantic Alignment for Personalized Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30553)
*Gaoming Zhang,Angqing Jiang,Jianchun Song,Kena Qi,Dayao Chen,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CHAP框架，一种从层次化视角解决生成式检索中语义对齐与个性化建模问题的个性化生成式检索方法。通过层次化语义对齐模块对齐查询与物品的潜空间，协同离散SID与连续表示建模用户行为，并引入残差级联生成机制将多步Transformer解码器压缩为单次推理，显著提升推理吞吐量。"


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索中的语义ID仅由物品内容派生，存在语义鸿沟，无法对齐动态查询意图与静态物品表示；同时现有生成范式很少建模用户行为序列，且受限于beam-search自回归解码的高推理延迟。

Method: 提出CHAP，包含三个关键设计：(1) 层次化语义对齐模块，对齐查询潜空间与物品量化路径，同步多粒度语义；(2) 个性化生成式检索框架，协同离散SID提供结构化指导与连续表示进行细粒度语义精化来建模用户行为；(3) 残差级联生成机制，将昂贵的多步Transformer解码器限制为单次推理，提升吞吐量并缓解信息损失。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、一个专有工业数据集以及在线A/B测试中，CHAP均展现出优越性能，验证了方法的有效性与实际应用价值。

Conclusion: CHAP从层次化角度有效解决了生成式检索中的语义对齐与个性化问题，并通过残差级联机制大幅提升推理效率，具备显著的工业实用性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm by mapping queries directly to Semantic IDs (SIDs) with powerful representation capabilities for candidate items. However, existing SIDs derived solely from item content create a semantic gap, failing to align dynamic query intents with static item representations. Furthermore, current generative paradigms rarely model user behavior sequences and are always bottlenecked by the high inference latency of beam-search autoregressive decoding. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{C}$ross-component $\textbf{H}$ierarchical semantic $\textbf{A}$lignment for $\textbf{P}$ersonalized generative retrieval ($\textbf{CHAP}$), a novel personalized GR framework from a hierarchical perspective. First, we design a Hierarchical Semantic Alignment module to align query's latent space with item's quantization path and synchronize multi-granular semantics. Second, we construct a personalized GR framework that models user behavior by synergizing discrete SIDs for structural guidance and continuous representations for fine-grained semantic refinement. Notably, we introduce a Residual Cascading Generation mechanism to restrict the costly multi-step Transformer Decoder to a single-pass inference, boosting inference throughput while mitigating information loss. Extensive experiments on three public datasets, one proprietary industrial dataset, and online A/B tests demonstrate CHAP's superiority, validating the effectiveness and practical value of our approach. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/zzzgm/CHAP.

</details>


### [413] [Generative Retrieval for E-commerce: Jointly Learning Embedding and Codebook with Same Product Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30606)
*Songtao Fang,Zihao Xu,Shaowei Wei,Jin Zhang,Zhuojun Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合训练嵌入模型和码本（codebook）的生成式检索方法，并引入产品簇信息作为额外监督信号，以解决电商场景中两阶段训练策略导致的误差累积和码本学习缺失交互建模的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的生成式检索采用两阶段训练策略（先训练产品嵌入模型，再学习映射嵌入到产品ID的码本），存在两个关键问题：(1) 级联误差累积——第一阶段嵌入模型的偏差无法在第二阶段被纠正；(2) 码本学习仅依赖产品嵌入，缺乏对查询-产品和产品-产品交互的建模，导致同一簇的产品可能被分配不一致的ID，损害检索准确性。

Method: 提出一种新方法，将嵌入模型和码本进行联合训练，不再采用级联的两阶段策略；同时引入同一产品簇的信息作为额外的监督信号，使码本学习能够建模查询到产品和产品到产品的交互关系。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提升了电商检索性能，同时增强了嵌入学习和码本学习的效果。

Conclusion: 联合训练嵌入模型和码本并加入产品簇监督信号，能有效解决两阶段训练策略的级联误差和ID不一致问题，全面提升电商场景下的生成式检索性能。

Abstract: With the development of large language models (LLMs), generative retrieval is becoming increasingly important in e-commerce scenarios. Current mainstream approaches typically use a two-stage training strategy: first train a product embedding model, and then learn a codebook that maps embeddings to product IDs. This cascaded approach suffers from two major issues: (1) error accumulation-if the embedding model in the first stage produces biased representations, the codebook in the second stage cannot correct these errors, degrading final retrieval performance; and (2) codebook learning relies solely on product embeddings and lacks modeling of query-to-product and product-to-product interactions. As a result, products belonging to the same cluster may be assigned inconsistent IDs by the codebook, further hurting retrieval accuracy. To address these problems, we propose a novel method that jointly trains the embedding model and the codebook, and incorporates same product cluster information as an additional supervision signal. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves e-commerce retrieval performance while simultaneously enhancing both embedding and codebook learning.

</details>


### [414] [Learning from What You Retrieve: Online RL Fine-Tuning for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30753)
*Shaowei Wei,Chong Huang,Songtao Fang,Jin Zhang,Zhuojun Wang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PAO（Positive-Advantage-Only），一种选择性强化学习优化方法，通过仅对具有正优势的检索项应用梯度更新，避免在冻结的高维空间中破坏预训练语义流形，从而解决双编码器检索器与下游重排序器目标不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商检索中，双编码器检索器针对对比相似度优化，而下游重排序器捕捉更细粒度的相关性偏好，两者目标不匹配限制了端到端检索质量。强化学习可用于利用奖励模型反馈适配检索器，但标准策略梯度更新会退化嵌入几何结构，尤其在工业场景中文档索引必须保持冻结的情况下。

Method: PAO方法的核心思想是：在标准RL训练中，无差别地惩罚负样本（推开）会破坏冻结高维空间中的预训练语义流形。因此，PAO选择性地仅对具有正优势的检索项应用梯度更新，将查询嵌入拉向高奖励区域，同时保持全局拓扑稳定性。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集和公开基准上的实验表明，PAO显著优于标准RL方法和蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 选择性强化学习优化（仅更新正优势项）能够在冻结文档索引条件下有效适配检索器，既改善检索质量又保持嵌入空间的拓扑完整性，为工业级检索系统的RL适配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In large-scale e-commerce retrieval, dual-encoder retrievers are op- timized for contrastive similarity, whereas downstream rerankers capture finer-grained relevance preferences; this objective mis- match limits end-to-end retrieval quality. Reinforcement Learning offers a way to use reward-model feedback for retriever adaptation, but we observe that standard policy-gradient updates can degrade embedding geometry, especially when the document index must remain frozen due to industrial constraints. To address this, we propose PAO (Positive-Advantage-Only), a selective RL optimization method. Our analysis reveals that in- discriminate penalization of negative samples (pushing away) in a frozen high-dimensional space disrupts pre-trained semantic man- ifolds. PAO selectively applies gradient updates only to retrieved items with positive advantages, effectively pulling query embed- dings toward high-reward regions while preserving global topo- logical stability. Experiments on both a massive industrial dataset and public benchmarks demonstrate that PAO significantly outper- forms standard RL and distillation baselines.

</details>


### [415] [MULTI3IR: A Benchmark for Multi-perspective Multi-domain Multi-modal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30949)
*Seokwon Song,Sohyeon Kim,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi³IR基准数据集，用于评估检索器在跨领域、跨模态的开放式查询中覆盖多维度视角的能力，并提出了SPIN方法（一种参数和标签高效的噪声向量学习方法）来引导嵌入向量朝多样且有意义的语义方向偏移，有效缓解了现有多模态检索器的单视角偏见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息检索日益面向开放式查询，这类查询允许多种不同的观点和视角，但现有IR基准主要聚焦于封闭式查询，即使开放式基准的查询也大多局限于单一学科领域和单一模态，无法全面评估检索器对开放式查询多视角覆盖的能力。

Method: 论文提出Multi³IR基准，包含104.9K个Stack Exchange查询，每个查询都标注了捕获其隐含视角的视角描述。同时提出SPIN方法，通过在嵌入空间中学习噪声向量来引导嵌入朝向多样且有意义的语义方向偏移，该方法在参数和标签使用上均高效。

Result: 实验表明现有多模态检索器存在严重的单视角偏见，而SPIN在Multi³IR上显著提升了视角覆盖率，并且能很好地泛化到未见过的开放式IR基准上。

Conclusion: 通过Multi³IR基准和SPIN方法，研究揭示了现有检索系统在多视角覆盖方面的不足，并提供了一种高效缓解方案，推动IR系统更好地服务于开放式、多视角的查询需求。

Abstract: Information retrieval (IR) increasingly targets open-ended queries that admit diverse perspectives. Existing IR benchmarks, however, focus primarily on closed-ended queries, while even open-ended benchmarks largely consist of queries whose supporting documents span a single subject domain and modality. We introduce Multi$^3$IR, a benchmark that evaluates how well retrievers cover the multifaceted perspectives of open-ended queries across diverse domains and modalities. It comprises 104.9K Stack Exchange queries, each annotated with perspective descriptions that capture the query's implicit viewpoints. We further propose SPIN, a parameter- and label-efficient method that learns noise vectors to steer embeddings toward diverse yet meaningful semantic directions. Experiments show that existing multimodal retrievers suffer from single-perspective bias, while SPIN substantially improves perspective coverage on Multi$^3$IR and generalizes well to unseen open-ended IR benchmarks. The dataset and experimental code are available at https://github.com/seokwon99/Multi3IR.

</details>
